Abstract: Variational quantum algorithms (VQAs) suffer from unstable optimization due to temporal noise drift and static qubit mappings that distort gradient signals across iterations. We present BAHAMAS, an online control framework that stabilizes noise exposure by adaptively selecting physical mappings via consensus-based fidelity estimation, without requiring simulators, offline training, or prior executions. Across real quantum devices, BAHAMAS improves optimization reliability and supports inference-time retargeting under drift through robust, per-iteration control.

</details>


### [207] [Exact Haar Statistics of Planar $k$-Purity in Multipartite Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28914)
*Kourosh Mirsohi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入平面k-纯度作为k-一致平面态的忠实代价函数，通过四副本计算推导了Haar随机纯态中平面割的协方差核与闭合方差，并发现平衡二能级情形下方差具有奇偶依赖的渐近形式，表明子系统几何排布虽不影响单割分布却控制着集体涨落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面k-一致态要求环上每个k位区间具有最大混合约化态，但此前缺乏衡量该多重约束满足程度的忠实代价函数。研究随机态中平面k-纯度的统计行为，可为超越绝对最大纠缠态存在范围的量子多体纠缠提供几何敏感的基准。

Method: 引入平面k-纯度作为区间约化态纯度的均值；利用Haar随机纯态下两个区间联合纯度仅依赖其重叠度的性质，进行四副本计算得到完整的循环协方差核与所有k的闭合方差；通过位因子化置换表示给出所有原始矩并精确求有限和得到偏度；用固定种子模拟验证非平衡二能级与三能级情况。

Result: 平衡二能级情形下，当k=floor(n/2)且N=2^n时，方差渐近为偶数n时的20/(3nN²)和奇数n时的6/(nN²)；模拟验证至n=10的奇偶公式与有限尺寸偏度。平面与绝对平衡纯度具有相同的Haar均值但涨落不同，n=10时平面方差约为后者的2.83倍。

Conclusion: 子系统几何排列（平面间隔关系）虽然在单割边际分布中不可见，却显著影响几何相关割的集体涨落。结合普适可达性与平衡态下线性数量的区间约束，该方法为绝对最大纠缠态存在域之外的多体纠缠提供了几何敏感的基准。

Abstract: Planar $k$-uniform states have maximally mixed reductions on every $k$-site interval of a ring. We introduce planar $k$-purity---the mean purity of those intervals---as a faithful cost function for this simultaneous constraint. For a Haar-random pure state of $n$ parties with local dimension $p$, the joint purity of two intervals depends only on their overlap. An exhaustive four-replica calculation therefore yields the complete cyclic covariance kernel and a closed variance for every $1\le k\le\lfloor n/2\rfloor$. In the balanced qubit case, $k=\lfloor n/2\rfloor$ and $N=2^n$, the variance is asymptotic to $20/(3nN^2)$ for even $n$ and $6/(nN^2)$ for odd $n$. A site-factorized permutation representation gives all raw moments and permits exact finite-sum evaluation of the skewness. Fixed-seed simulations validate nonbalanced qubit and qutrit cases, while balanced-qubit simulations through $n=10$ validate the parity formulas and finite-size skewness. Balanced planar and absolute balanced purity have the same Haar mean but different fluctuations; the planar variance is approximately $2.83$ times larger at $n=10$. Thus subsystem incidence, although invisible to every one-cut marginal distribution, controls the collective fluctuations of geometrically related cuts. Together with universal attainability and a linear number of interval constraints at balance, this provides a geometry-aware benchmark for multipartite entanglement beyond AME existence regimes.

</details>


### [208] [Probabilistic generation of two-mode binomial cat states using cross-Kerr interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29173)
*S. Zhao,A. Metelmann,S. Qvarfort*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种仅使用高斯初始态和报弃式外差测量即可生成双模二项式猫态的方案，利用超导电路中常见的交叉Kerr相互作用，避免了非高斯初始资源的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双模二项式猫态是连续变量量子计算中实现精确量子纠错的关键资源，但传统制备方案需要非高斯初始态（如Fock态或NOON态），这些态在实验上难以实现，限制了其实际应用。

Method: 提出一个制备协议，仅需高斯初始态配合报弃式外差测量（heralded heterodyne measurements），利用玻色子模式间的交叉Kerr相互作用（常见于超导电路架构）来生成双模二项式猫态。通过调节输入态参数，该协议在真实实验条件下对耗散保持鲁棒性。

Result: 通过调节输入态参数，协议在真实实验条件下对耗散保持鲁棒性。数值或理论分析表明该方案能够克服现有制备方案的关键局限性。

Conclusion: 该工作为在当前实验平台上实现多项猫态提供了一条实用路径，无需非高斯初始资源，克服了现有制备方案的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Superpositions of macroscopically distinct coherent states, or cat states, are a key resource for quantum technologies. In particular, two-mode binomial cat states can give rise to exact quantum error correction in continuous-variable quantum computing. However, their preparation typically requires non-Gaussian initial states, such as Fock or NOON states, which are challenging to realize experimentally. Here, we propose a protocol to generate two-mode binomial cat states where the only requirements are Gaussian initial states in combination with heralded heterodyne measurements. Our approach utilizes cross-Kerr interactions between bosonic modes commonly realizable in superconducting circuit architectures. We show that by adjusting the input state parameters, our protocol remains robust to dissipation under realistic experimental conditions. Our work provides a practical route to realizing multinomial cat states in current experimental platforms without requiring non-Gaussian initial resources, overcoming a key limitation of existing preparation schemes.

</details>


### [209] [Representation Learning with Quantum Signal Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28828)
*Junqi Wang,Junyu Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了量子信号处理（QSP）作为一个可解析求解的表示学习量子模型，精确计算了量子神经正切核的均值和方差，证明了稀疏数据下的非线性梯度流收敛性，并给出了有限深度速度极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的表示学习分析通常依赖冻结核（frozen-kernel）假设，即仅对固定几何进行重新加权，而无法捕捉训练过程中特征本身发生变化所带来的表示学习效应。作者希望建立一个可解析求解的量子模型，在超出冻结核极限的情况下，提供受控的量子数据几何学习理论。

Method: 利用量子信号处理（QSP）作为表示学习量子模型：在任意深度下精确计算量子神经正切核（QNTK）的均值与方差；对完整非线性梯度流给出稀疏数据保证，证明其收敛到可积标量流并具有时间依赖的核闭包；通过数值方法研究高数据密度下的耦合演化。

Result: QNTK表现出输入相关的角几何，其对角线即使在酉算子趋近Haar随机时仍保持非自平均性；稀疏数据下非线性的完整训练动力学收敛到可积标量流，具有明确的收敛时间；对任意数据集和轨迹均存在有限深度速度极限；高数据密度下观察到超越标量和冻结核描述的耦合演化。

Conclusion: 该工作为学习到的量子数据几何提供了受控理论，证明了超越冻结核极限的可验证训练动力学，为量子表示学习的理论分析建立了新的框架。

Abstract: Representation learning begins when training changes the features that define similarity between data. A frozen-kernel model only reweights a fixed geometry. We establish quantum signal processing (QSP) as a solvable quantum model of the representation-learning regime. At arbitrary depth, we compute the exact mean and variance of its quantum neural tangent kernel, revealing an input-dependent angular geometry whose diagonal remains non-self-averaging even when the underlying unitary approaches Haar randomness. We also prove a sparse-data guarantee for the full nonlinear gradient flow without freezing or ensemble-averaging the kernel: the realized dynamics converges to an integrable scalar flow with a time-dependent kernel closure and explicit convergence times. A finite-depth speed limit holds for every data set and trajectory. At higher data density, numerical results show coupled evolution beyond both the scalar and frozen-kernel descriptions. These results give a controlled theory of learned quantum data geometry with provable training dynamics beyond the frozen limit.

</details>


### [210] [Anomalous Frequency Shift in Low-Loss Superconducting Granular Aluminum Resonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29282)
*Patrick Winkel,Daniil S. Antonenko,Vishakha Gupta,Neel Thakur,Pavel Kurilovich,Luigi Frunzio,Leonid I. Glazman,Robert J. Schoelkopf*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了超导高动力学电感材料（如颗粒铝 grAl）在微波频率下的损耗机制。与标准双能级系统（TLS）模型的预测相反，作者观察到在最低温度下共振频率随温度和读出功率的升高而增加。部分数据可由具有空间非均匀能隙的颗粒系统中超导准粒子的效应解释，但频率变化与损耗率变化不成比例。此外，还观察到高能事件后异常的瞬态正频率响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒铝（grAl）是电路量子电动力学（cQED）工具库中的重要组成部分，但前提是其微波损耗足够低。要进一步推动grAl在量子器件中的应用，必须识别其主导损耗机制。

Method: 采用标准方法，将谐振器几何结构的电磁模拟与测量的温度和功率依赖的共振频率及损耗率相结合，并与标准TLS模型预测进行对比。

Result: 观察到共振频率在最低温度下随温度和读出功率升高而增加的异常行为，与TLS模型矛盾。部分数据可由具有空间非均匀能隙的颗粒系统中超导准粒子的机制解释，但频率变化与损耗率变化不成比例。还观察到高能事件后具有多个时间尺度的异常正频率响应。

Conclusion: 由于器件具有极低的损耗率，异常行为与标准模型的分歧尤为明显。这些发现表明grAl中的损耗机制超出了标准TLS理论范畴，需要新的物理模型来解释。

Abstract: Superconducting high-kinetic inductance materials like granular aluminum (grAl) are a versatile part of the circuit quantum electrodynamics (cQED) toolbox, provided their losses at microwave frequencies are low enough. To further advance the use of grAl in quantum devices, it is indispensable to identify the dominant loss mechanisms. The standard approach pairs electromagnetic simulations of resonator geometry with measured temperature and power dependence of resonance frequency and loss rate. Most materials follow the phenomenological theory of two-level systems (TLSs) at low temperatures, resulting in an initial decrease of the resonance frequency with increasing temperature. In our work, we observe an opposite behavior at the lowest temperatures: The resonance frequency initially increases with both temperature and readout power, contradicting the standard TLS model predictions. We are able to explain a part of the data by an alternative mechanism associated with the effect of superconducting quasiparticles in a granular system with spatially-nonuniform gap. Yet, the observed change in the resonance frequency with temperature is not matched by a proportional change in the loss rate. We also observe anomalous positive frequency responses following high-energy events, characterized by several time scales. While anomalous behavior has been reported previously in grAl, the disagreement with standard models is particularly visible in our devices thanks to their exceptionally low loss rates.

</details>


### [211] [Quantum Preconditioning For Constrained Optimization Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28842)
*Anurag Ramesh,Bhuvanesh Sundar,Maxime Dupont,David E. Bernal Neira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究量子预条件对约束组合优化问题的影响，聚焦平衡图二分问题。利用QAOA生成的两点相关性构造修正目标函数，供混合整数规划（MIP）求解器使用。实验表明预条件化的MIP实例能更快逼近最优解，且最浅QAOA深度即可获得大部分收益，支持量子算法指导经典精确MIP求解器的混合优化框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子计算（尤其是QAOA）如何为经典精确求解器提供有价值的启发式信息，以加速约束组合优化问题的求解，特别是在资源有限的NISQ时代背景下，用量子方法增强传统MIP求解器的实用路径。

Method: 从QAOA中提取决策变量间的两点相关性，利用这些相关性重构目标函数（即预条件化），将修改后的目标函数输入MIP求解器。预条件化保持原有硬约束不变，所有可行解仍按原始目标函数评估。在稠密、带权完整图实例上进行计算实验，并分析求解器回调轨迹。

Result: 预条件化的问题实例能更快达到近最优解，且大部分收益在最浅QAOA深度即可获得。回调轨迹分析表明加速源于搜索过程中更早发现高质量可行解（incumbent）。

Conclusion: 量子算法提供的相关性信息可以有效引导经典精确MIP求解器的搜索过程，支持量子-经典混合优化框架的可行性，其中量子部分提供问题特定的指导信息而非追求完全量子求解。

Abstract: We study the effect of quantum preconditioning on constrained combinatorial optimization problems, focusing on balanced graph bi-partitioning. The proposed approach uses two-point correlations between decision variables derived from the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) to construct a modified objective function that is subsequently provided to mixed-integer programming (MIP) solvers. The preconditioned MIP formulation retains the original hard constraint, and all incumbent solutions are evaluated under the original objective. Computational experiments on dense, weighted complete-graph instances show that the preconditioned problem instances reach near-optimal solutions faster, with most of the benefit already realized at the shallowest QAOA depth tested. Solver callback trajectories show this arises from earlier discovery of high-quality incumbents during the solution search. These results support a hybrid optimization framework in which quantum algorithms provide problem-specific information to guide classical exact MIP solvers.

</details>


### [212] [Convex conservation of von Neumann entropy implies unitarity or antiunitarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29373)
*Nicolas G. Underwood,Jonte R. Hance*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有限维希尔伯特空间中提出了一个与Kadison定理类似的新型定理，将可逆性条件替换为熵守恒条件，论证量子力学中的可逆性假设可被熵守恒原理取代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kadison定理表明密度矩阵空间上的所有可逆凸变换都是酉或反酉的，但可逆性假设在量子力学中并非基本公理。作者希望探索用更基础的熵守恒概念替代可逆性假设，从而给出量子力学变换的更根本性刻画。

Method: 在有限维希尔伯特空间中，将Kadison定理中的可逆性要求替换为熵守恒条件，考察所有保持熵的凸变换k:D(H)→D(H)，证明这类变换同样必然为酉或反酉变换。

Result: 在有限维情形下，熵守恒的凸变换同样归约为酉或反酉变换，从而建立了与Kadison定理平行的结果。

Conclusion: 量子力学中的可逆性假设可以被熵守恒原理替代，为量子力学的基本结构提供了新的原理性理解。

Abstract: On the space of density matrices $D(H)$, Kadison's theorem establishes that all invertible convex transformations $K:D(H)\to D(H)$ are unitarity or antiunitary. For a finite Hilbert space, we propose a similar theorem which replaces the invertibility requirement with that of entropy conservation. Thus, we argue, the supposition of reversibility in quantum mechanics may be replaced with a principle of entropy conservation.

</details>


### [213] [Rényi Entanglement of Purification Is Non-additive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28897)
*Amir-Reza Negari,Zahra Baghali Khanian*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了两量子比特上经典态在不同Rényi阶数下的纠缠纯化(additivity of entanglement of purification)的可加性问题。对α∈[0,1)，证明了该族内存在非可加性（由单个态的两份拷贝见证）；对α∈[2,∞]，证明了该族内张量积下的可加性；α∈[1,2)（含von Neumann情形α=1）仍开放，并猜想其可加性成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠纯化(entanglement of purification)是衡量总关联的基本度量，但其可加性问题长期以来悬而未决。解析其在不同Rényi阶数下的可加性行为，有助于深入理解这一度量在量子信息理论中的结构性质。

Method: 首先精确求解了整个经典两比特态族在所有Rényi阶数下单拷贝的优化问题；随后将两拷贝的优化限制在一组自然的有限纯化集合上，找到一个熵严格低于乘积值的纯化；对于α∈[2,∞]的情况则证明了该族内的可加性。

Result: 在α∈[0,1)范围内证明了非可加性（由单个态的两份拷贝即可见证）；在α∈[2,∞]范围内证明了可加性；α∈[1,2)区间（包括von Neumann情形α=1）的问题留待开放。

Conclusion: 纠缠纯化的可加性表现出对Rényi阶数的非平凡依赖：低阶(α<1)非可加，高阶(α≥2)可加，而中间区间(1≤α<2)仍开放，作者猜想该区间内可加性成立。

Abstract: Entanglement of purification is a fundamental measure of total correlations whose additivity remains unresolved. We study its additivity for classical states on two qubits at different Rényi orders. For every $α\in[0,1)$, we prove nonadditivity within this family, witnessed by two copies of a single state. We first solve the one-copy optimization exactly for the entire family at every Rényi order. We then restrict the two-copy optimization to a natural finite set of purifications and exhibit one whose entropy is strictly below the product value. In contrast, for $α\in[2,\infty]$ we prove additivity under tensor products within this family. The interval $α\in[1,2)$, including the von Neumann case $α=1$, remains open, and we conjecture additivity there throughout the same family.

</details>


### [214] [Quantum work extraction from partial information and with finite resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29394)
*Giacomo Guarnieri,Diego Maragnano,Giulia Gamba,Lorenzo Zoppelletto,Marco Liscidini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究有限资源条件下量子麦克斯韦妖的工作提取问题，提出了部分信息与有限资源（PIFR）量子麦克斯韦妖协议：对N个相同量子态副本中的M个进行测量以估计状态及相应的ergotropic幺正变换，再将其应用于剩余的N-M个副本。论文推导出普适的闭式权衡界限，将ergotropic效率约束在Carnot型上限1-M/N与由有限样本量子估计理论决定的精度下限之间，并优化得到M*∝N^(2/3)及N^(-1/3)收敛到理想极限的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子力学中，信息不会自由获取，必须通过对有限数量副本的测量来统计推断。传统上工作提取的理论研究往往假设对状态有完全信息，但在实际有限资源场景中，信息获取本身就是一项成本。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究有限副本条件下信息获取、状态重建精度与热力学产率之间的根本权衡。

Method: 论文引入部分信息与有限资源（PIFR）量子麦克斯韦妖协议。具体而言，给定N个已知哈密顿量的相同量子态副本，妖怪先测量其中M个副本以估计量子态及相应的ergotropic幺正变换，然后将该变换应用于剩余的N-M个副本进行工作提取。作者推导了ergotropic效率（总提取功归一化到理想ergotropic基准）满足的普适闭式权衡界限，并利用有限样本量子估计理论分析重建精度。通过优化副本分配，得到最优测量数M*∝N^(2/3)以及N^(-1/3)的收敛速率，并用标准量子态层析进行数值验证。

Result: 核心结果是ergotropic效率被限制在Carnot型上限1-M/N和一个由保守最坏情形重建精度控制的底限之间。优化副本分配得到M*∝N^(2/3)，理想极限以N^(-1/3)速率趋近。数值上通过标准量子态层析验证了最优资源分配的存在，且该分配依赖于所考虑量子态的纯度。

Conclusion: 有限副本条件下的工作提取本质上是一个任务依赖的推断问题，估计策略应以热力学性能而非单纯的重建保真度来评判。这一视角为量子热力学与量子估计理论的交叉提供了一个统一的理论框架。

Abstract: Information can be converted into work, but in quantum mechanics information about a state is not freely available: it must be inferred statistically from measurements on a finite number of copies. We study work extraction in this finite-resource setting by introducing a partial-information and finite-resources (PIFR) quantum Maxwell's demon. Given $N$ identical copies of a state with known Hamiltonian, the demon measures $M$ copies to estimate the state and the corresponding ergotropic unitary, which is then applied to the remaining $N-M$ copies. This protocol induces a trade-off between information acquisition, reconstruction accuracy, and thermodynamic yield, making the total extracted work normalized to the ideal ergotropic benchmark the relevant figure of merit. As our central result, we derive a universal closed-form trade-off bound that places this ergotropic efficiency between a Carnot-type ceiling $1-M/N$ and a floor controlled by a reconstruction precision rooted in finite-sample quantum estimation theory; optimizing the copy allocation yields $M^*\propto N^{2/3}$ and an $N^{-1/3}$ approach to the ideal limit, set by a conservative, worst-case reconstruction precision. By considering standard quantum state tomography, we numerically verify the presence of an optimal resource distribution, which also depends on the purity of the state under consideration. Our results identify finite-copy work extraction as a genuinely task-dependent inference problem, in which estimation strategies should be judged by thermodynamic performance rather than reconstruction fidelity alone.

</details>


### [215] [Quantum optomechanics with arbitrary mirror displacement: nonlinear Langevin equation with non-Markovian back-action noises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29429)
*Adrian E. Rubio Lopez,Hing-Tong Cho,Bei-Lok Hu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文采用泛函微扰方法处理量子光机力学（QOM）系统，无需假设镜面位移x为小量，即可处理任意幅度的镜面运动。通过从量子场的影响作用量导出自洽的非马尔可夫噪声反馈，得出非线性Langevin方程，并与文献中Nx耦合到x^3阶的模型进行了统一链接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子光机力学中光-机耦合是非线性的，现有理论通常假设镜面位移x很小，仅展开到x^2或x^3阶。作者希望发展一种不依赖小位移展开的方法，能够处理任意大小的镜面位移，以支持高精度QOM实验。

Method: 采用文献[1]提出并经[2]丰富的泛函微扰方法，适用于足够弱的光-机耦合。首先推导量子场对运动镜面的影响作用量和非马尔可夫噪声，再以此导出自洽的非线性Langevin方程，并将结果与文献中辐射压Nx耦合至x^3阶的模型进行对应和对比。

Result: 成功推导出由量子场反作用噪声驱动的非线性Langevin方程，无需将镜面位移限制在小量范围。证明该一般方法可以统一描述文献中从辐射压Nx耦合到x^3阶的各种模型和Langevin方程。

Conclusion: 该泛函微扰方法为量子光机力学提供了超越小位移近似的理论框架，有望为未来高精度QOM实验提供有力的理论支撑。

Abstract: Quantum optomechanics (QOM) explores the interaction between a quantum field, often confined in a cavity, and the quantum motion of mirrors, membranes or material media, usually assumed slow enough with negligible particle production, which is the central concern of dynamical Casimir effects, and a drive laser field for effective control. This rapidly developing field has a wide range of applications, from quantum sensing to the detection of gravitational waves. Because the opto-mechanical coupling is nonlinear, most theoretical investigations assume that the amplitude of mirror motion $x$ remains small. Recent years saw several papers treating $x^2, x^3$ orders in the mirror displacement. In our work we take a different route, deploying the functional perturbative method presented in [1] and enriched in [2], applicable for sufficiently weak opto-mechanical couplings. With this we can treat arbitrary displacement of the mirror, not just at a higher order in a power series expansion in $x$. We first derive the influence action of the quantum field and its non-Markovian noises back-reacting on the moving mirror with self-consistency. From it we derive a nonlinear Langevin equation driven by these back-action noises from the quantum field. We then show how results from our general modeling and treatment can be linked with the models and the Langevin equations presented in the literature, starting with the popular radiation pressure $Nx$ coupling, with $N$ the photon number, up to the recent $x^3$ order results. We hope this new approach and the results presented here can provide useful theoretical support for high precision QOM experimentation in the future.

</details>


### [216] [Parametric amplification in a Kerr Oscillator based on Ne FIB Nanobridges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29435)
*Parth Bhandari,Laith Meti,Gemma Chapman,Edward Romans,John Gallop,Ling Hao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了基于Nb纳米桥弱连接的DC SQUID超导电路，在15 mK、高达2.48 mT的磁场下，利用双音谱学研究了其非线性行为。通过蓝失谐泵浦估算Kerr因子，利用红失谐泵浦观测到附加闲频模式并获得最大15 dB的放大增益，实验结果与Kerr非线性振子模型吻合良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导电路是量子计算和量子传感领域的关键技术，而电路的潜在应用取决于工程化的非谐性（Kerr因子）和谐振线宽。因此，研究并优化超导电路中的非线性行为对推进量子技术应用至关重要。

Method: 制备了嵌入DC SQUID（由Nb纳米桥作为弱连接）的Nb基CPW谐振器；在15 mK下使用双音谱学进行表征，磁场范围至2.48 mT；通过蓝失谐泵浦下的谐振频率降低估算Kerr因子；应用红失谐泵浦超过分岔阈值以观察闲频模式并实现信号放大；使用Kerr非线性振子模型对透射谱进行拟合验证。

Result: 蓝失谐泵浦导致谐振频率降低用于估算Kerr因子；红失谐泵浦超过分岔阈值后出现具有净增益的附加闲频模式；通过进一步降低泵浦频率最大化增益，实现了最大15 dB的放大。

Conclusion: 实验测得的透射谱与Kerr非线性振子模型一致，验证了该器件的非线性行为模型；同时提出了进一步改进器件增益和带宽的设计修改方向。

Abstract: Superconducting circuits play a crucial role in the advancement of quantum computing and quantum sensing. Typically such circuits require the presence of a non-linear element, where the engineered anharmonicity (Kerr factor) and resonant linewidth determine the potential applications of the circuit. In this work we have fabricated Nb-based CPW resonators embedded with a DC SQUID incorporating Nb nanobridges as the weak links. We use two-tone spectroscopy to study the non-linear behaviour of the device at 15 mK up to a field of 2.48 mT. Under the application of a blue-detuned pump, the device shows a decrease in the resonant frequency which is used to estimate the Kerr factor. We further apply a red-detuned pump to go beyond the bifurcation threshold and observe the appearance of an additional idler mode with net gain. The gain of the device was maximized by further decreasing the pump frequency, showing a maximum amplification of 15 dB. Finally, we show agreement between the Kerr non-linear oscillator model and the measured transmission spectrum, and highlight further design modifications to improve the gain and bandwidth of such devices.

</details>


### [217] [On the entropy of a pseudo-density matrix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28946)
*James Fullwood,Boyu Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了冯·诺依曼熵到伪密度矩阵（PDM）存在唯一扩展，该扩展满足酉不变性和关于[-1,1]区间内仿射组合的强可加性两个简单假设，并证明该扩展对单量子比特动力学是次可加的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDM形式体系将密度算子自然推广到时空领域，但PDM可能具有负特征值，而标准冯·诺依曼熵仅对正算子定义。因此需要寻找一种合理的熵扩展方式，以量化PDM所编码的时空关联中的量子信息特性。

Method: 作者首先证明特征值落在[-1,1]区间内，然后基于两个关键公理——酉不变性和关于[-1,1]区间内仿射组合的强可加性——推导出冯·诺依曼熵到单位迹厄米矩阵的唯一扩展形式。随后通过单量子比特动力学验证该熵扩展的次可加性，并通过多个实例分析其行为。

Result: 证明了满足酉不变性和强可加性这两个简单假设的冯·诺依曼熵到PDM的唯一扩展存在；证明了该唯一扩展对单量子比特动力学具有次可加性；并通过多个示例分析了该熵的行为特征。

Conclusion: 该研究为PDM形式体系提供了公理化且唯一的熵定义，填补了PDM无法直接使用标准冯·诺依曼熵的空白，为时空量子关联的量化提供了严格的理论基础。

Abstract: The pseudo-density matrix (PDM) formalism naturally extends the notion of density operator to the spatiotemporal domain. While PDMs are Hermitian and of unit trace, they admit negative eigenvalues when encoding temporal correlations unattainable for spacelike separated systems. Consequently, there have been various approaches to extending the von~Neumann entropy---which is only defined for positive operators---to PDMs. Here, we prove that there exists a unique extension of the von~Neumann entropy functional to Hermitian matrices of unit trace satisfying two simple assumptions: unitary invariance and strong additivity with respect to affine combinations within the interval $[-1,1]$, which we prove contains the eigenvalues of a PDM. We also prove that this unique extension of von~Neumann entropy to PDMs is subadditive for single qubit dynamics, and we analyze its behavior in a number of examples.

</details>


### [218] [Universal recovery in approximate quantum error correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28962)
*Dor Elimelech,Victor V. Albert,Alexander Barg*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了近似量子纠错（AQEC）中"通用恢复"（universal recovery）的第二部分：单个恢复映射能够同时纠正由给定误差集控制的所有信道。作者证明环境泄漏距离和Knill-Laflamme Hellinger距离这两个参数同样控制通用解码，并研究了与误差集自然关联的Petz映射作为显式通用恢复方案，给出了均匀的最坏情况和平均情况保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在精确量子纠错中，线性结构保证了一个码纠正给定误差集的线性张成中的所有信道，且单个恢复映射即足够。然而，近似量子纠错（AQEC）缺乏这种结构。此前工作（arXiv:2607.22995）证明了近似设置中线性形式的部分保留，但尚未建立通用恢复的第二部分——即单个恢复映射能否同时纠正误差集控制的所有信道。本文旨在补齐这一缺口。

Method: 本文基于先前提出的误差集理论，利用两个关键参数——环境泄漏距离（最坏情况性能）和Knill-Laflamme Hellinger距离（平均情况性能）——来量化近似可纠正性。作者证明这两个量同时控制通用解码性能，并研究从误差集自然构造的Petz映射作为通用恢复映射，推导出其在整个信道族上的均匀平均情况和最坏情况保证。

Result: 作者成功完成了近似设置中通用恢复的完整理论：单个恢复映射确实可以同时纠正由给定误差集控制的所有信道。环境泄漏距离和Knill-Laflamme Hellinger距离均能控制通用解码的性能，且Petz映射被证明是一种有效的显式通用恢复方案，为整个信道族提供了均匀的平均情况和最坏情况纠错保证。

Conclusion: 本文为近似量子纠错理论补齐了通用恢复的完整图景，表明AQEC虽然放宽了完美恢复的要求，但依然保留了类似精确QEC的通用恢复结构。这为近似量子纠错的实际应用提供了理论基础，尤其是在同时面对多种误差信道需要统一恢复策略的场景中。

Abstract: Universal recovery -- the existence of a single recovery map that corrects an entire family of error channels -- is a central feature of quantum error correction (QEC). In exact QEC, linearity guarantees that a code correcting a given error set also corrects every channel whose Kraus operators lie in its linear span, and that a single recovery map suffices for all such channels. Approximate quantum error correction (AQEC), which relaxes perfect recovery to recovery with controlled error, has traditionally lacked this structure. In a recent paper (arXiv:2607.22995), we developed a theory of approximate quantum error correction showing that a restricted form of linearity persists in the approximate setting, yielding uniform AQEC guarantees for the family of channels controlled by a given error set.
  In this work, we complete the picture by establishing the second half of universal recovery in the approximate setting: a single recovery map can simultaneously correct every channel controlled by a given error set. The error-set theory we proposed quantifies approximate correctability through two parameters: the environment-leakage distance, governing worst-case performance, and the Knill--Laflamme Hellinger distance, governing average-case performance. We show here that both quantities also control universal decoding. We further study the Petz map naturally associated with an error set as an explicit universal recovery, and obtain uniform average- and worst-case guarantees across the entire family of channels.

</details>


### [219] [Measure of set imaginarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28985)
*Yu Guo,Jiabo Pan,Yuqin Wang,Shuanping Du*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究qubit系统中集合虚部（set imaginarity）作为量子资源的问题，通过Bargmann不变量表征自由操作，构建了统一的与完整的集合虚部度量框架，并提出两种基于三态子集Bargmann不变量的度量，同时改进了鲁棒性度量使其成为完整的qubit SIM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究表明Bargmann不变量是集合虚部的有效探测器，但集合虚部在qubit系统中的自由操作表征和精细化量化度量尚未明确，需要建立公理化框架以进行更细粒度的资源量化。

Method: 利用Bargmann不变量的结构，证明qubit集合虚部的自由操作恰好由共同幺正操作和共同平面化操作组成。基于此表征，提出统一SIM和完整SIM两个细化概念，并从三态子集的Bargmann不变量构造两个qubit SIM，同时改进已有的鲁棒性度量。

Result: 构造的两个qubit SIM中，一个为统一SIM，另一个满足完整SIM的更严格要求；已有鲁棒性度量虽是有效的qubit SIM但既非统一SIM也非完整SIM；改进后的鲁棒性度量被严格证明定义了一个完整的qubit SIM。

Conclusion: 本文通过Bargmann不变量建立了qubit集合虚部资源的公理化度量框架，提供了统一SIM和完整SIM的精细化量化工具，并克服了已有鲁棒性度量的局限。

Abstract: Recent studies have shown that Bargmann invariants provide effective detectors of set imaginarity. In this paper, we investigate set imaginarity as a quantum resource in qubit systems. By exploiting the structure of Bargmann invariants, we show that the free operations for qubit set imaginarity consist precisely of common unital operations and common planarized operations. Based on this characterization, we introduce an axiomatic framework for set-imaginarity measures (SIMs). In particular, we propose two refined notions, namely unified SIMs and complete SIMs, which allow a more fine-grained quantification of set imaginarity. To make these notions concrete, we construct two qubit SIMs from the Bargmann invariants of three-state subsets. We prove that one of them is a unified SIM, while the other satisfies the stronger requirements of a complete SIM. Furthermore, we revisit the robustness of set imaginarity previously introduced in the literature. We show that, although this robustness is a valid SIM for qubit systems, it is neither a unified SIM nor a complete SIM. To overcome this limitation, we propose an improved robustness-type measure and rigorously prove that it defines a complete qubit SIM.

</details>


### [220] [Hardware-Efficient Exchange-Only QML: Singlet-Triplet Spin Chains via Inter-pair Coupling without Magnetic Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29017)
*Yuichiro Minato*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种资源高效的量子机器学习架构，仅使用双自旋单元和Heisenberg交换相互作用（无需磁场梯度）实现高表达力。通过利用自旋链的固有时间动力学作为学习资源，模型在MNIST测试集上达到90.9%±0.2%的准确率，优于经典基线（83.2%）和未训练储层版本（53.0%），并对实验噪声具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交换型量子比特每个逻辑量子比特需要三个物理自旋，硬件开销大；双自旋单元虽然密度高但依赖局域磁场梯度控制，集成复杂度高。作者旨在用最简硬件（双自旋+Heisenberg交换）实现高表达力的QML，避免漏泄编码和复杂微磁集成。

Method: 从通用门控范式转向利用自旋链的固有时域动力学作为学习资源。设计基于双自旋单元的量子特征映射，通过自旋对间交换耦合打破隔离自旋对的对称性保护约束，实现干涉介导的态混合以增强Hilbert空间表达力。采用输入依赖的可训练交换脉冲作为特征映射，结合线性读出层进行MNIST数字分类。

Result: 在完整10,000张MNIST测试集上取得90.9%±0.2%准确率（五个独立种子）。训练后的量子特征映射（88.1%）优于相同PCA输入上的经典线性基线（83.2%）和同动力学未训练储层版本（53.0%）。在10%准静态脉冲面积噪声下准确率保持89.9%，在每次观测量用10^3次测量射弹估计时保持89.8%。

Conclusion: 竞争性QML可在最简半导体自旋链硬件上实现，无需漏泄倾向的编码或复杂微磁体集成。该工作展示了利用系统固有动力学而非通用门控作为学习资源的可行性，为超低硬件开销的量子机器学习铺平道路。

Abstract: Standard universal quantum computing using exchange-only qubits typically requires three physical spins per logical qubit, leading to significant hardware overhead. Conversely, two-spin units offer higher density but rely on local magnetic field gradients for control, increasing integration complexity. In this paper, we propose a resource-efficient quantum machine learning (QML) architecture that achieves high expressibility using minimal two-spin units and Heisenberg exchange interactions alone, without any magnetic gradients. We shift the paradigm from universal gate-based control to utilizing the intrinsic, time-domain dynamics of a spin chain as a learning resource. Numerical simulations on MNIST digit classification demonstrate that the symmetry-protected constraints of isolated spin pairs are bypassed by leveraging inter-pair exchange coupling. This interference-mediated state mixing significantly enhances the expressibility of the Hilbert space. The model reaches a test-set accuracy of 90.9% +/- 0.2% over five independent seeds on the full 10,000-image MNIST test set. Under an identical linear readout, the trained quantum feature map (88.1%) clearly outperforms a classical linear baseline on the same PCA inputs (83.2%) as well as an untrained (reservoir-style) version of the same dynamics (53.0%), demonstrating that the learned, input-dependent exchange pulses implement a genuinely non-linear and trainable feature map. The protocol is also robust to experimentally relevant imperfections: accuracy remains at 89.9% under 10% quasi-static pulse-area noise and at 89.8% when every observable is estimated from 10^3 measurement shots. These findings suggest that competitive QML can be executed on the simplest possible semiconductor spin-chain hardware, bypassing the need for leakage-prone encodings or complex micro-magnet integration.

</details>


### [221] [Exact Entanglement Swapping through Single-Occupancy Measurements in Gaussian Fermion States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29933)
*Jiyuan Fang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了任意半填充自由费米子高斯态两个相同副本的纠缠结构。通过对m个对应梯级进行测量并后选择相同的归一化单费米子态，发现测量后的条件态仍为高斯态，并精确分解为N-m个正交模式，其中包括m个副本间纠缠模式和N-2m个光谱模式。纠缠熵为S=m h₂(|v|²)，与初始态无关，成功概率由初始关联决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子测量和后选择对多体系统纠缠结构的影响是量子信息科学中的核心问题。特别是对于自由费米子高斯态，测量诱导的纠缠交换机制及其普适性尚未被完全阐明，本文旨在精确刻画这一过程的纠缠结构。

Method: 采用解析方法分析任意半填充自由费米子高斯态的两个副本。在每个对应梯级上测量并后选择相同的归一化单费米子态|ψ⟩=u|10⟩+v|01⟩。通过分析条件波函数、高斯态因式分解以及相关行列式的计算，推导出纠缠熵和成功概率的精确表达式。

Result: 对于m<N/2，条件波函数一般依赖于初始态，但当选择结果非零概率时，未测量位点上的态仍为高斯态，精确因式分解为N-m个正交模式，包括m个副本间纠缠模式和N-2m个单个副本内的光谱模式。纠缠熵为S=m h₂(|v|²)，与初始态无关；成功概率P_m=det C_L det(I_m-C_L)=det(C_LR C_RL)，仅由初始关联决定，与(u,v)无关。

Conclusion: 该结果表明，即使在一般高斯初始态下，通过后选择单费米子态实现的纠缠交换具有普适性：每次成功的测量交换固定量h₂(|v|²)的纠缠，且成功概率由初始关联的几何结构决定。等权Bell后选择是实现最大纠缠交换的特例。

Abstract: We determine the exact entanglement structure of the conditional state obtained by measuring $m$ corresponding rungs ($m\leq N/2$) in two identical copies of an arbitrary half-filled free fermion Gaussian state and post-selecting the same normalized single-fermion state ($|ψ\rangle = u|10\rangle+v|01\rangle$, where 0 and 1 denote the fermion occupancy on each sites) on each rung. For $m<N/2$, the conditional wavefunction generally depends on the initial state. Nevertheless, whenever the selected outcome has nonzero probability, the state on the unmeasured sites remains Gaussian and factorizes exactly into $N-m$ different orthogonal modes including $m$ inter-copy entangling modes and $N-2m$ spectator modes localized in one copy. Consequently, the entanglement entropy between the unmeasured parts of the two copies is $S=m h_2(|v|^2)$, independent of the initial state, where $h_2(x)=-x\ln x-(1-x)\ln(1-x)$. The success probability is given by $P_m=\det C_L\det(I_m-C_L)=\det(C_{LR}C_{RL})$, determined solely by the initial correlations and independent of ($u$,$v$). Equal-weight Bell post-selection serves as a special case that achieves the maximal entanglement swapping.

</details>


### [222] [Eigenvalues of multipartite entanglement witnesses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29049)
*Nalan Wang,Lin Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了多体块正算子、可分解纠缠见证（DEWs）与非可分解纠缠见证（NDEWs）的各种性质，给出了利用多体GHZ态构造特殊DEW的充要条件，并刻画了多体DEW的最大/最小特征值的上下确界及其平方的迹，同时推导了2×n纠缠见证的特征值不等式及其物理含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纠缠见证（Entanglement Witness）是量子信息中检测纠缠的重要工具，特别是在多体系统中。理解多体块正算子、可分解与非可分解纠缠见证的性质，有助于更深入地认识多体纠缠结构以及如何有效构造和判定纠缠态。

Method: 该文采用数学分析方法，系统地研究多体块正算子的代数性质。具体方法包括：利用多体GHZ态构造特殊类型可分解纠缠见证的充要条件推导；对多体可分解纠缠见证的最大/最小特征值及迹的平方进行精确的上下界刻画；对2×n纠缠见证的特征值不等式进行推导并通过具体算例检验这些不等式的紧致性。

Result: 给出了基于多体GHZ态构造特殊可分解纠缠见证的充要条件；精确描述了多体可分解纠缠见证的最大、最小特征值的上下确界及迹的平方；推导了非可分解纠缠见证与2×n纠缠见证的相关结果；并通过具体例子验证了所得特征值不等式的紧致程度。

Conclusion: 该研究丰富了对多体块正算子和纠缠见证的理论理解，特别是对可分解与非可分解纠缠见证的特征值结构和构造方法提供了系统性刻画，研究成果对于多体量子纠缠的检测与分类具有理论指导意义。

Abstract: We investigate various properties of multipartite block-positive operators, decomposable EWs (DEWs), and non-decomposable EWs. We provide a necessary and sufficient condition to construct a special DEW using the multipartite GHZ state. For multipartite DEW, we explicitly characterize the supremum and infimum of maximum and minimum eigenvalues, as well as the trace of its square. We also derive other results concerning NDEW, eigenvalues of $2 \times n$ EWs and the corresponding physical implications. Furthermore, we investigate the tightness of inequalities of these eigenvalues with examples.

</details>


### [223] [The Inverse Eigenvalue Problem for Partial Transposes of Two-Qubit States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29068)
*Ruoting Dou,Shengjun Wu,Zeng-Bing Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文完全解决了二量子比特系统中部分转置谱的逆特征值问题：任意非负迹为1的谱都能被某个PPT态实现，而NPT态的候选特征值列表(x,y,z,-q)可实现的充要条件是q≤y且qy≤xz，并给出了显式的X态构造及量子转向椭球体的几何解释。进一步基于此推导出由两个最低非平凡PT矩(p2,p3)约束下的精确负性上下界，并将相关不等式推广到qubit-qudit系统作为必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分转置谱携带了刻画纠缠的重要信息（如通过PPT判据和negativity），且可由多副本测量获取。然而此前并不知道哪些特征值列表可以实际作为某个二量子比特态的部分转置谱出现，这一逆特征值问题悬而未决，限制了对可观测约束下纠缠估计能力的理解。

Method: 通过对二量子比特态进行完整的结构分析，将NPT态的候选特征值(x,y,z,-q)的可实现性刻画为不等式约束q≤y和qy≤xz；构造了一个显式X态来证明充分性，其量子转向椭球中心c=(y-q)/(1-z)、归一化体积V/V_max(c)=qy/(xz)，从而将两个不等式几何化为椭球中心允许区域和固定中心体积上界。此外利用这些特征值不等式推导出在给定矩p2、p3约束下的精确负性极值，并分析极值点的边界条件。

Result: 证明了二量子比特部分转置谱的完整特征描述：PPT态可实现任意非负迹1谱，而NPT态的特征值列表(x,y,z,-q)可实现当且仅当q≤y且qy≤xz。基于该逆定理，在给定PT矩p2和p3时精确确定了最小和最大negativity，给出了最优性条件（最小值在x=y或qy=xz处取得，最大值在y=z或q=y处取得），并将不等式作为必要条件推广至qubit-qudit系统。

Conclusion: 该工作完整解决了二量子比特部分转置的逆特征值问题，建立了谱可实现性与量子转向椭球几何之间的深刻联系，并为仅依赖低阶PT矩进行纠缠量级估计提供了精确的最优定界，同时揭示了该刻画在更高维系统中的推广局限与持续性。

Abstract: For a bipartite state $ρ$, information about the spectrum of its partial transpose $ρ^{Γ_B}$ can be inferred from measurements on multiple copies of $ρ$, without full state tomography. This raises a natural question: which eigenvalue lists can arise as $\operatorname{spec}(ρ^{Γ_B})$ for a density operator $ρ$? We completely solve this inverse eigenvalue problem for two qubits. Every nonnegative trace-one spectrum is realized as $\operatorname{spec}(ρ^{Γ_B})$ by some PPT state $ρ$, whereas an ordered candidate eigenvalue list $(x,y,z,-q)$, with $x\ge y\ge z\ge0$, $q>0$, and $x+y+z-q=1$, is realized by an NPT state iff $q\le y$ and $qy\le xz$. Sufficiency in the latter case is established by an explicit $X$ state whose quantum steering ellipsoid has center $c=(y-q)/(1-z)$ and normalized volume $V/V_{\max}(c)=qy/(xz)$, providing a geometric interpretation of the inequalities $q\le y$ and $qy\le xz$ as the allowed ellipsoid-center region and the fixed-center volume bound. Beyond this geometric picture, the two-qubit inverse theorem also yields exact negativity bounds from the two lowest nontrivial PT moments. Given fixed values of $p_2=Tr[(ρ^{Γ_B})^2]$ and $p_3=Tr[(ρ^{Γ_B})^3]$, we determine the exact minimum and maximum negativity over all two-qubit states subject to these moment constraints. When no PPT state is consistent with the pair $(p_2,p_3)$, the minimum is attained either at $x=y$ or $qy=xz$, while the maximum is attained either at $y=z$ or $q=y$. Finally, we show how the two-qubit inequalities persist as necessary constraints for the inverse eigenvalue problem in qubit--qudit systems.

</details>


### [224] [Work Extraction Across a Thermodynamic Hierarchy in Quantum Many-Body Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31001)
*Akihiro Hokkyo,Masahito Ueda*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了孤立量子多体态存在一个热力学层级结构，同一量子态在不同观测/操作层级上可以同时表现为热态和非热态。扩大允许操作集合可将"隐藏非热性"转化为功提取资源，功增益上界由两个层级间的熵密度之差（即互信息密度）决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学是操作性的：热平衡概念依赖于观测量的选择，可提取功也相对于允许操作定义。作者旨在系统性地刻画孤立量子多体系统中，不同操作层级之间隐藏非热性如何成为功提取资源的理论框架。

Method: 作者建立了热力学层级结构概念，将观测集合和允许操作进行层级划分，并分别从三个操作性扩展方向（空间分辨率增加、控制持续时间增加、非局域连接）推导功增益的界限。每个方向对应接触不同类型的信息：位置态Holevo信息、相邻区域关联、空间非局域关联。

Result: 证明了功提取增益的上界由不同热力学层级之间的熵密度差（即互信息密度）所限定。三个操作性扩展方向分别对应的互信息密度项不同，但统一的结论是：层级间熵差决定了访问更少受限层级时功增益的上界。

Conclusion: 孤立量子多体系统的热力学行为天然具有层级化结构，熵在不同操作层级间的差异是量化"隐藏非热性"作为功提取资源的统一度量，为理解热力学在更广泛操作性框架下的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Thermodynamics is operational in the sense that the very concept of thermal equilibrium depends crucially on the choice of observables, while the amount of extractable work is defined relative to the allowed operations. Here we show that isolated quantum many-body states admit a thermodynamic hierarchical structure of observables and allowed operations, where the same state can be thermal at one level of the hierarchy and athermal at another. Enlarging the set of allowed operations therefore renders such hidden athermality a potential resource for work extraction; the resulting work gain is bounded in terms of the difference between the entropy densities of the two levels. This entropy difference takes the form of the mutual-information density. We establish the bounds for three extensions of operational access: increased spatial resolution, increased duration of control, and nonlocal connectivity, which provide access to position-state Holevo information, correlations between neighboring regions and spatially nonlocal correlations, respectively. Thus the difference between entropies at different levels of the thermodynamic hierarchy governs the bound on the work gain associated with moving to a less restricted level.

</details>


### [225] [Quantum discord of Gaussian states in non-inertial frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29079)
*Siwei Li,Xiaofen Huang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Unruh效应下连续变量高斯量子失协在两个均匀加速观测者系统中的重新分布行为，发现初始量子关联并未被破坏而是在Rindler模式对之间重新分配，并系统表征了压缩参数s和场频率ω对不同模式对类别中量子失协的影响规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解量子信息在相对论加速运动背景下的命运是量子信息与量子场论交叉领域的重要问题。先前研究多关注量子纠缠在Unruh效应下的退化，而量子失协作为更广义的量子关联度量，其在加速参考系中的重新分布机制尚不清晰，特别是多观测者情形下的系统性分析缺乏。

Method: 采用协方差矩阵方法和高斯态量子失协解析公式，分别对单观测者和双观测者均匀加速情形进行建模，分析惯性模式与加速模式之间、以及不同Rindler模式对之间的量子失协变化，并通过系统扫描压缩参数s和场频率ω来刻画各参数对不同模式对类别的影响。

Result: 研究发现：惯性模式与可访问加速模式之间以及两个可访问加速模式之间的量子失协随加速度单调衰减并在a→∞时消失，而Unruh效应在所有因果断开的Rindler模式对中生成量子失协，表明初始量子关联被重新分布而非被破坏。参数效应具有模式类别依赖性：因果连接模式对中s和ω效应相当且可互换；跨区域模式对中压缩参数主导；观测者内跨区域模式对中场频率更关键。双观测者情形下A_II-B_II模式对呈现非单调行为，峰值位置随ω增大向更大a移动。

Conclusion: 结论表明Unruh效应本质上是量子关联的重分配机制而非破坏机制，量子信息并未在加速过程中丢失，而是被转移到因果断开模式的关联中。s和ω对不同模式类别的差异性影响揭示了参数控制量子关联分布的灵活性，特别是双观测者情形中A_II-B_II对在特定s/ω比附近的最佳工作点。

Abstract: We investigate the redistribution of continuous-variable Gaussian quantum discord under the Unruh effect for both one and two uniformly accelerated observers. The discord shared between the inertial mode and the accessible accelerated mode, as well as that between the two accessible accelerated modes, decays monotonically with acceleration and vanishes in the limit $a\to\infty$, while the Unruh effect generates discord in all causally disconnected Rindler mode pairs. The initial quantum correlation is therefore redistributed across Rindler partitions rather than destroyed. We further characterize how the squeezing parameter $s$ and field frequency $ω$ control the discord in different mode-pair classes. For causally connected pairs, $s$ and $ω$ exert comparable, interchangeable effects; for cross-region pairs, the squeezing parameter dominates; and for intra-observer cross-region pairs, the field frequency is more influential. In the two-observer case, the $A_{II}$-$B_{II}$ pair exhibits non-monotonic behavior with a peak whose position shifts to larger $a$ as $ω$ increases, and both parameters contribute only weakly except near an optimal $\fracω{s}$ ratio.

</details>


### [226] [Second harmonic phase locking and synchronization blockade in quadratically coupled driven quantum van der Pol oscillators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29185)
*Nissi Thomas,M. Senthilvelan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了二次耦合振荡器系统在外驱动下展现出2:1锁相的高阶同步现象，揭示了第一个振荡器中的"同步阻断"效应（1:1锁相被完全抑制），而直接驱动的第二个振荡器则在二次谐波频率上与驱动实现1:1锁相。通过经典平均场分析和量子分析相结合，阐明了这种非对称锁相几何的宏观起源与微观机制，并进一步展示了系统在共振条件下的相互同步以及共振附近因能态谱分裂导致的同步抑制。研究还提出捕获离子和光机械系统中的实验实现方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非线性二次耦合在高阶同步与量子同步现象中的核心作用，特别是揭示非对称锁相结构（同步阻断）背后的经典与量子机制，为量子控制策略和量子信息平台提供理论支撑。

Method: 采用经典平均场分析重现非对称锁相几何结构；结合量子分析揭示同步阻断的微观起源；在低激发子空间中对主方程进行微扰分析，辅以稳态相位分布、同步度量、布居统计和谱响应分析；并提出捕获离子和光机械系统的实验实现方案。

Result: 二次耦合系统在外驱动下呈现2:1高阶同步，第一个振荡器出现同步阻断（1:1锁相被完全抑制），第二个振荡器在二次谐波频率上与驱动1:1锁相。系统在双振子满足共振条件时展现相互同步，但在共振附近因能态谱分裂出现同步区间的微妙抑制。

Conclusion: 二次耦合是产生非经典同步现象的关键机制，同步阻断的微观起源可通过量子分析揭示，这为量子同步控制及量子信息平台的发展提供了新的物理洞察和实验路径。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamics of a quadratically coupled system under the influence of an external drive applied to the second oscillator, where the coupling facilitates a high-order synchronization with phase-locking emerging in the form of 2:1 between the oscillators. Our analysis reveals a synchronization blockade in the first oscillator, characterized by the complete suppression of conventional 1:1 phase-locking with the drive. In contrast, we observe that the directly driven second oscillator synchronizes with the drive, showing 1:1 phase-locking but notably at second harmonic frequency. A classical mean-field analysis of the corresponding equations of motion reproduces this asymmetric phase-locking geometry which demonstrates that the phase-locking structure itself can be understood from the nonlinear classical dynamics. The quantum analysis, however, reveals the microscopic origin of the synchronization blockade. Furthermore, we show that the system exhibits mutual synchronization when both the oscillators satisfies the resonance condition, enabling coherent energy exchange facilitated by nonlinear quadratic coupling. The mutual synchronization shows synchronized regimes and also subtle suppression of synchronized regimes near resonance occurring due to spectral splitting of the energy states. Using perturbation analysis of the master equation within the low excitation subspace, we analyze steady-state phase distribution and synchronization measures, supported by population statistics and spectral responses. We also propose possible experimental realizations in trapped-ions and optomechanical setups. These findings highlight the crucial role of quadratic coupling in enabling nonclassical synchronization phenomena, offering deeper insights for quantum control strategies and the development of quantum information platforms.

</details>


### [227] [Engineering multi-photon dissipation with a dc-voltage-biased Josephson junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31154)
*Marco Paradina,Ambroise Peugeot,Roberto Negrin,Oscar Novat,Tristan Villain,Anil Murani,Jean-Loup Ville,Sébastien Jezouin,Raphaël Lescanne,Audrey Bienfait,Benjamin Huard*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用直流电压偏置的SQUID来工程化多光子耗散，替代传统的参数泵浦约瑟夫森耦合器方案，从而消除寄生非线性项，并展示了单、双、四光子到损耗模式的转换，通过Wigner断层成像表征双光子耗散，为猫量子比特的稳定提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多光子耗散是玻色子量子比特（特别是猫量子比特）的关键资源，但传统的实现方式（参数泵浦约瑟夫森耦合器）会引入杂散非线性项，影响量子比特性能，需要寻找更干净的实现方案。

Method: 使用直流电压偏置的SQUID来工程化多光子耗散，利用直流偏置使得寄生项在时间上平均消除；实现高Q模式中的一个、两个或四个光子转换为损耗模式中的单个光子；通过Wigner断层成像对双光子耗散进行全面表征。

Result: 成功实现了单、双、四光子到损耗模式的转换，并通过Wigner断层成像表征了双光子耗散，验证了直流偏置约瑟夫森结作为储层工程资源的可行性。

Conclusion: 直流偏置的SQUID为多光子耗散提供了一种更干净的实现方式，是通往猫量子比特稳定的可行路径，拓展了储层工程的实现手段。

Abstract: Multi-photon dissipation -- a key resource for bosonic qubits -- is usually realized by parametrically pumping a Josephson coupler at the cost of spurious nonlinear terms. Here we instead engineer it using a dc-voltage-biased SQUID, such that these parasitic terms average out. We activate the conversion of one, two, or four photons of a high-Q mode into a single photon of a lossy mode. We characterize the two-photon dissipation by Wigner tomography, establishing dc-biased junctions as a resource for reservoir engineering and a viable route to cat-qubit stabilization.

</details>


### [228] [Fully integrated continuous-variable quantum key distribution with composable security over 100 km](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29200)
*Yankai Xu,Xinhang Li,Tao Wang,Jisheng Dai,Xueqin Jiang,Yuyao Guo,Peng Huang,Linjie Zhou,Guihua Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文报道了一种全集成连续变量量子密钥分发（CV-QKD）平台，采用两个混合III-V/Si₃N₄集成激光器、硅基发射机和硅基相干接收机实现核心终端功能，在25-150 km光纤链路上实现本地本振（LLO）运行，通过贝叶斯机器学习算法维持相位锁定，并在100 km距离上认证了29.3 kbps的可组合有限尺寸密钥速率（基于1400亿符号块），创造了集成CV-QKD的最远有限尺寸/渐近传输距离和最高每符号密钥速率记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子密钥分发（QKD）虽然能通过物理定律保证信息论安全性，但大规模部署需要紧凑、可制造的光子终端。CV-QKD因其电信兼容、常温相干探测的特性而非常适合这一转变，但实现芯片核心终端全集成、常温运行、高损耗容限和长距离可组合端到端安全性的统一仍是一个关键瓶颈。

Method: 采用全集成CV-QKD平台：两个混合III-V/Si₃N₄集成激光器、硅基发射机和硅基相干接收机实现核心终端功能，使用本地本振（LLO）方案在25-150 km光纤上运行。通过贝叶斯机器学习算法维持长记录所需的稳健相位锁定（优于传统无迹卡尔曼滤波器），并采用速率匹配的多维协商方法逼近香农极限。

Result: 系统在100 km距离认证了29.3 kbps的可组合有限尺寸密钥速率（基于1400亿符号块）；125 km下有限尺寸分析得到12.9 kbps；150 km下渐近分析得到9.17 kbps。这是集成CV-QKD中报道的最长有限尺寸和渐近传输距离，以及最高的每符号密钥速率。

Conclusion: 该工作通过建立集成CV-QKD在有限尺寸和渐近分析下最远的传输距离以及最高的每符号密钥速率，推动了实用化芯片级量子网络的发展。

Abstract: Quantum key distribution (QKD) guarantees information-theoretic security by the laws of physics, but deployment at scale requires compact, manufacturable photonic terminals. Continuous-variable QKD (CV-QKD) is well suited for this transition through telecom-compatible, room-temperature coherent detection. However, unifying full on-chip core terminal integration, room-temperature operation, high loss tolerance, and composable end-to-end security in long-distance QKD remains a key bottleneck. Here we report a fully integrated CV-QKD platform in which two hybrid III--V/Si$_3$N$_4$ integrated lasers, a silicon transmitter, and a silicon coherent receiver implement the core terminal functions, operating with a local local oscillator (LLO) over fibre links of 25--150 km. A Bayesian machine-learning algorithm maintains robust phase lock throughout the long records required for composable security, consistently outperforming the conventional unscented Kalman filter, while rate-matched multidimensional reconciliation approaches the Shannon limit. The system certifies a composable finite-size secret-key rate of 29.3 kbps at 100 km from a 140-billion-symbol block, with 12.9 kbps at 125 km under finite-size analysis and 9.17 kbps at 150 km under asymptotic analysis. By establishing the longest finite-size and asymptotic reaches and the highest secret-key rate per symbol reported for integrated CV-QKD, this work advances the development of practical chip-based quantum networks.

</details>


### [229] [Protected Logical Qudits in Kitaev Quantum Double Models via Stable Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31011)
*Naihong Hu,Futao Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于表示论的框架，用于在有限群Kitaev量子双体模型中构造受保护的逻辑量子比特（qudit）。通过引入ε-稳定不可约表示，建立了存在受保护逻辑子空间的充要判据，并推导出ribbon-投影算子对易关系，从而得到d维受保护逻辑子空间。该构造应用于对称群和交错群，获得了S_n（n≥3）的逻辑量子比特和A_4的逻辑qutrit，并证明了(ℤ₂)^d⋊ℤ_d族可实现任意维度d≥2的受保护逻辑qudit。最后，针对D(A_4)描述了利用ribbon逻辑操作实现通用逻辑qutrit计算的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算需要对外编码量子信息提供稳健保护。现有Kitaev量子双体模型在保护逻辑量子比特方面有潜力，但缺乏系统化构造高维受保护逻辑qudit的一般性框架，因此需要发展一种表示论工具来统一和推广此类构造，并探索其在通用量子计算中的应用。

Method: 建立了表示论框架，核心是引入ε-稳定不可约表示的概念，借此给出存在d维受保护逻辑子空间的充要条件；推导ribbon-投影算子的对易关系以构造逻辑子空间；将通用框架应用于对称群、交错群及半直积族，并利用ribbon逻辑操作设计通用逻辑qutrit计算方案。

Result: 得到了S_n（n≥3）的逻辑量子比特和A_4的逻辑qutrit；(ℤ₂)^d⋊ℤ_d族实现了任意维度d≥2的受保护逻辑qudit；对D(A_4)给出了基于ribbon逻辑操作的通用逻辑qutrit计算方案。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个系统性构造受保护逻辑qudit的通用框架，不仅覆盖了对称群和交错群等具体实例，还能实现任意维度的逻辑qudit，并为通用容错量子计算提供了基于拓扑模型的可行路径。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computation requires robust protection of encoded quantum information. In this work, we develop a representation-theoretic framework for constructing protected logical qudits in finite-group Kitaev quantum double models. By introducing $\varepsilon$-stable irreducible representations, we establish a necessary and sufficient existence criterion and derive ribbon--projector commutation relations that yield a $d$-dimensional protected logical subspace. We apply this construction to symmetric and alternating groups, obtaining logical qubits for $S_n$ ($n\ge 3$) and a logical qutrit for $A_4$. Moreover, the family $(\mathbb Z_2)^d\rtimes\mathbb Z_d$ realizes protected logical qudits of arbitrary dimension $d\ge 2$. Finally, for $D(A_4)$, we describe a scheme for universal logical qutrit computation using ribbon-based logical operations.

</details>


### [230] [Efficient search for excitable zero-modes in constrained systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31165)
*Jean-Yves Desaules*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在动力学约束系统中高效寻找平移不变可激发零模（EZM）解析表达式的协议，基于局部无约束哈密顿量的特征分解，并在修饰Rydberg链上展示了该方法的强大能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学约束系统（如阻塞机制下的Rydberg原子阵列）因其谱中存在非典型的特征态而备受关注，这些特征态体现为量子多体疤痕以及异常大的零模子空间。现有方法难以系统地找到这些解析可处理的特征态，尤其是平移不变的EZm。

Method: 提出一种基于局部无约束哈密顿量特征分解的协议，用于在约束系统中找到平移不变的EZM解析表达式。该方法直接产生位于周期边界条件下零能量特征空间中的连续矩阵乘积态（MPS）流形。

Result: 在修饰Rydberg链模型中，该协议直接产生连续MPS流形，其张成的空间随系统大小指数增长，对于奇数系统尺寸完全覆盖零动量零能量特征空间。此外，该流形的物理结构还允许推导非零动量下的ZMs以及E=±√3处多项式数量的精确疤痕态的解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种系统化的方法，不仅能够发现约束系统中的EZm解析特征态，还能利用流形的物理结构进一步推导非零动量ZMs和精确量子疤痕的解析表达式，为理解动力学约束系统中的非典型特征态提供了新工具。

Abstract: Kinetically constrained systems, such as those representing Rydberg atom arrays in the blockade regime, have gathered considerable attention due to the presence of atypical eigenstates in their spectrum. The latter manifests itself through the presence of quantum many-body scars as well as unusually large zero-mode (ZM) subspaces which contain analytically tractable eigenstates with various entanglement scalings. In particular, some of the latter are excitable zero-modes (EZMs), meaning that they can be promoted to a non-zero energy for open boundary conditions. In this work, I present an efficient protocol for finding analytical expressions for translation-invariant EZMs in constrained systems, based on the eigendecomposition of the local unconstrained Hamiltonian. I demonstrate the power of this method on a decorated Rydberg chain. In that model, my protocol directly produces a continuous matrix-product-state manifold located entirely in the zero-energy eigenspace for periodic boundary conditions. The span of that manifold grows exponentially with the system, and for odd system sizes it covers the entire zero-momentum eigenspace with zero energy. I then show how the physical structure of the manifold, which is tied to the local eigenbasis, also allows one to derive analytical expressions for ZMs at non-zero momentum and for a polynomial number of exact scars at $E=\pm\sqrt{3}$.

</details>


### [231] [Zero-Energy Problems for Supersymmetric Hamiltonians on a Chain Are $\mathrm{QMA}_1$-Complete](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29333)
*Yibin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维链上超对称量子系统中精确零模的存在性问题，证明了厄米超荷与显式编码的幂零N=2形式化下，该问题均为QMA_1-完全问题，且困难实例可构造为链上几何局域的哈密顿量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算复杂性理论中，超对称量子系统的零模问题是理解量子自然问题复杂度的关键。此前关于此类问题的复杂度分类尚不明确，特别是缺乏对一维几何局域实例的系统性刻画。

Method: 作者利用"同时稀疏线性约束的验证定理"和同一套一维无挫折哈密顿量构造方法，针对两类超对称形式化（厄米超荷与显式编码的幂零N=2）建立QMA_1-完全性归约。构造中还给出了每个约化NO实例基态能量的显式逆多项式下界，将其与零分离。

Result: 证明了厄米超荷情形下零模判定为QMA_1-完全，且在链上几何局域；同时证明显式编码的幂零N=2形式化的精确零问题也为QMA_1-完全，包括哈密顿量限制在链上局域的情形。构造提供了基态能量的逆多项式下界。

Conclusion: 一维链上的超对称量子零模问题在复杂性与量子计算之间建立了新的联系，两类超对称表述在精确零模问题的复杂度上具有同等困难性，且困难的实例可嵌入到一维几何局域结构中。

Abstract: We study exact zero modes of supersymmetric quantum systems whose interactions are arranged on a one-dimensional chain. For Hermitian supercharges, deciding whether a zero mode exists is $\mathrm{QMA}_1$-complete, and the hard instances are geometrically local on a chain. We also classify an explicitly encoded nilpotent $\mathcal{N}=2$ formulation: its exact-zero problem is $\mathrm{QMA}_1$-complete, including the restriction in which the associated Hamiltonian is local on a chain. Both classifications use a verification theorem for simultaneous sparse linear constraints and the same one-dimensional frustration-free Hamiltonian construction. The construction gives an explicit inverse-polynomial lower bound on the ground energy of every reduced NO instance, separating it from zero.

</details>


### [232] [Quantum Natural Gradient on Quotient Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29350)
*Zeyu Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了参数化量子电路中由于状态保持冗余导致的量子Fisher信息矩阵（QFIM）奇异问题，提出了在参数商空间上的量子自然梯度（QNG）公式化框架。作者证明了当预定冗余耗尽Fisher核时，Moore-Penrose更新是商黎曼梯度的最小范数水平提升；通过电路到轨道转移原理分离了内禀状态可区分性与电路坐标畸变；利用表示论在最高权标志轨道上得到根分辨的Fisher尺度，并为Slater和费米子高斯流形构造了各向同性内禀度量。在余极小嵌入上，内禀保真度QNG守恒主缺陷比率并简化为单一标量方程；Lie收缩步长在η=2处为局部三次，稳定性边界为η=4。对有限测量实现，作者区分了精确规范移除与物理软模式正则化，在结构投影下获得零累积规范漂移，并推导了置信度控制的软模式规则。在去极化下，逆Fisher缩放通过放大涨落恢复确定性尺度，因此无法恢复丢失的更新信噪比。冗余Slater/Givens电路验证了转移律和预测的有限测量权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数化量子电路中普遍存在的状态保持冗余导致QFIM奇异，即使在物理状态流形正则的情况下也是如此。这一奇异性破坏了标准QNG的形式化基础，因此需要一种能够正确处理参数商结构、区分内禀几何与坐标畸变的理论框架。

Method: 本文采用微分几何与表示论的综合方法：在参数商空间上重新表述QNG，利用Moore-Penrose伪逆作为最小范数水平提升；提出电路到轨道转移原理分离内禀与坐标效应；运用表示论分析最高权标志轨道的Fisher几何；对有限测量情形提出规范移除与软模式正则化的分离策略，并通过结构投影保证零累积规范漂移。

Result: 建立了QFIM奇异情形下QNG的严格几何基础，给出了电路实现内禀轨道QNG方向的精确条件；在余极小嵌入上得到简化动力学（单一标量方程）及Lie收缩的收敛阶与稳定性边界；对于有限测量，获得了置信度控制的软模式规则和零规范漂移保证；并通过冗余Slater/Givens电路数值验证了转移律与有限测量权衡预测。

Conclusion: 本文的框架为奇异QFIM下的QNG提供了完整的几何刻画，其核心洞见在于：合理地利用冗余结构（而非回避）可以将QNG简化为可解析处理的动力学。同时，研究揭示了在去极化噪声下逆Fisher缩放无法恢复信噪比这一根本局限，指出了有限测量QNG实践中软模式处理的关键作用。

Abstract: Parametrized quantum circuits often contain state-preserving redundancies that make the quantum Fisher information matrix (QFIM) singular even when the physical state manifold is regular. We formulate quantum natural gradient (QNG) on the resulting parameter quotient and prove that, when the prescribed redundancy exhausts the Fisher kernel, the Moore--Penrose update is the minimum-norm horizontal lift of the quotient Riemannian gradient. A circuit-to-orbit transfer principle separates intrinsic state distinguishability from circuit-coordinate distortion and gives the exact condition under which a circuit realizes an intrinsic orbit-QNG direction. Representation theory then yields root-resolved Fisher scales on highest-weight flag orbits and isotropic intrinsic metrics for Slater and fermionic-Gaussian manifolds. On cominuscule embeddings, intrinsic fidelity QNG conserves principal-defect ratios and reduces to one scalar equation; Lie-retracted steps are locally cubic at $η=2$, with stability boundary $η=4$. For finite-shot implementations, we separate exact gauge removal from regularization of physical soft modes, obtain zero cumulative gauge drift under structural projection, and derive confidence-controlled soft-mode rules. Under depolarization, inverse-Fisher scaling restores deterministic scale only by amplifying fluctuations and therefore cannot recover a lost update signal-to-noise ratio. A redundant Slater/Givens circuit verifies the transfer law and the predicted finite-shot tradeoffs.

</details>


### [233] [Spinor Structure from Relativistic Mass-Shell Factorisation in Phase Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29353)
*Mark Everitt*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出，自旋的量子起源可能源于对相对论相空间施加过于严格的标量哈密顿结构。通过在完整质量壳约束下要求动量线性表示，自然推导出Clifford代数及其最小四维复表示，进而产生矩阵值相空间分布。在Weyl排序刘维尔方程投影到质量壳子空间后，获得经典矩阵李维尔输运方程，而更强的星积约束则复现Dirac-Wigner方程，并引入ℏ作为内禀代数到物理角动量的转换尺度。这暗示旋量结构可具有非量子起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋通常被视为最本质的量子现象之一，但它在经典物理学中缺乏自然对应物，这阻碍了相空间形变量子化理论对其起源的解释。作者认为这一困难可能源于对相对论相空间施加了过度限制的标量哈密顿结构。

Method: 作者从完整质量壳约束出发，要求其由一个对动量四个分量均线性的有限维表达式表示，从而迫使系数矩阵满足Clifford代数。该代数的最小复表示为四维。在质量壳因子选定的秩二子空间中要求统计完备性，得到四乘四矩阵值系综分布。通过将Weyl排序刘维尔方程投影到每个壳上动量的二维子空间，得到相对论输运方程，同时保留任意布居和相干性。展开矩阵Moyal星积对易子，在领头阶得到对称化的经典矩阵李维尔算符；施加双侧星积约束则复现左右Dirac-Wigner方程。

Result: 论文证明线性质量壳约束自然引出Clifford代数与四维旋量结构，无需预先假设量子力学。经典系综在壳上由2×2矩阵描述，投影后的输运方程保留布居与相干性，展开星积给出经典李维尔领头项。双侧星积约束恢复Dirac-Wigner方程，并将ℏ确定为内禀代数与物理角动量之间的转换尺度。

Conclusion: 该研究显示了自旋结构可以具有非量子起源的可能性，为调和相对论协变性、经典相空间输运与量子自旋提供了一条新路径。即自旋的数学结构可能内禀于相对论相空间的经典几何中，而非必须引入量子化假设。

Abstract: Spin is usually regarded as one of the most intrinsically quantum phenomena, while its lack of a natural analogue in classical physics presents an obstacle to phase-space deformation-quantisation accounts of its origin. We show that this difficulty may arise from imposing an overly restrictive scalar Hamiltonian structure on relativistic phase space. Requiring the complete massive mass-shell constraint to be represented by a single finite-dimensional expression that is linear in all four components of momentum forces its coefficient matrices to satisfy a Clifford algebra. The minimal complex representation of this algebra is four-dimensional. Requiring statistical completeness within the rank-two subspace selected by the mass-shell factor then leads to a four-by-four matrix-valued ensemble distribution. At each on-shell momentum, the linear mass-shell operator selects a two-dimensional subspace, so a general classical ensemble is described by a $2 \times 2$ matrix before quantisation. Projecting the Weyl-ordered Liouville equation into this subspace gives relativistic transport while preserving arbitrary populations and coherences and the remaining projector components determine the first deformation correction. Expansion of the matrix Moyal star commutator yields the symmetrised classical matrix Liouvillian at leading order. If the stronger two-sided star constraints are imposed, they reproduce the left and right Dirac-Wigner equations and introduce $\hbar$ as the scale converting the dimensionless internal algebra into physical angular momentum. These results suggest a non-quantum origin for spinor structure and a route to reconciling relativistic covariance, classical phase-space transport, and quantum spin.

</details>


### [234] [Analytical diagonalization of the open-boundary bosonic Kitaev chain: An asymmetric plane-wave ansatz approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29684)
*Ning Wu,Wen-Long You*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种纯代数方法求解开放边界条件下的玻色子Kitaev链，该方法基于标准玻色子Bogoliubov变换，通过非对称平面波假设和简并分辨技术构造物理准粒子算符，并适用于更广泛的玻色子配对系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放边界条件下的玻色子Kitaev链因其在驱动-耗散系统中的实现及其引人入胜的非厄米边界物理而受到关注。该模型此前已知可通过位置-动量表象中的局域压缩变换求解，本文旨在提供一种替代的、纯代数的求解方案，其完全依赖于标准的玻色子Bogoliubov变换。

Method: 对于N格点链，作者提出具有不等左/右传播动量的非对称平面波假设（asymmetric plane-wave ansatz），用以解析求解关联的2N×2N非厄米"关联矩阵"。左本征值问题给出N个互异特征值，每个特征值二重简并；通过玻色子对易关系仔细分辨这些简并，构造N个物理Bogoliubov准粒子算符。

Result: 构造揭示了准粒子算符的非唯一性，在特殊情形下精确反映了原始方法中局域压缩变换的自由度。哈密顿量的对角形式被显式获得，并证明与原哈密顿量等价。

Conclusion: 所提出的非对称平面波假设和简并分辨技术不局限于当前模型，可推广到其他玻色子配对系统，包括具有非均匀配对或跳迁项的体系。

Abstract: The bosonic Kitaev chain under open boundary conditions has attracted recent attention due to its realization in driven-dissipative systems and its intriguing non-Hermitian boundary physics. The model is known to be solvable via local squeezing transformations in the position-momentum representation. In this paper, we present an alternative, purely algebraic solution that relies entirely on the standard bosonic Bogoliubov transformation. For an $N$-site chain, we propose an asymmetric plane-wave ansatz with unequal left- and right-moving momenta to analytically solve the associated $2N\times 2N$ non-Hermitian ``associated matrix". The left eigenvalue problem yields $N$ distinct eigenvalues, each of which is twofold degenerate. By carefully resolving these degeneracies using the bosonic commutation relations, we construct the $N$ physical Bogoliubov quasiparticle operators. The construction reveals non-uniquenesses that in special cases exactly mirror the freedom in the local squeezing transformations of the original approach. The diagonal form of the Hamiltonian is obtained explicitly and is shown to be equivalent to the original Hamiltonian. The proposed asymmetric plane-wave ansatz and degeneracy-resolution technique are not limited to the present model and can be generalized to other bosonic pairing systems, including those with inhomogeneous pairing or hopping terms.

</details>


### [235] [FIREQ: FPGA Instrumentation for Readout and Qubit control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29399)
*Giuseppe La Capra,Fabio Calabrese,Giorgio D'Amico,Christian Conti,Andrea De Simone,Deborah Volpe,Roldolfo Carobene,Claudio Gatti,Andrea Giachero,Fabrizio Riente*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: FIREQ是一个基于AMD Zynq UltraScale+ RFSoC的开源框架，用于超导量子比特的控制与读取。它结合模块化AXI固件架构与PYNQ软件栈，支持确定性实验时序、低开销重复校准工作流、直接RF合成与采集、触发序列、可编程脉冲生成及频分复用读取。在ZCU216上实现了9.3 GHz RF脉冲、107 ps脉宽分辨率和1.7 ns事件时序分辨率，并通过超导量子比特上的谱学、Rabi、Ramsey和弛豫测量验证了端到端操作（T1=6.94 μs, T2*=13.50 μs）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导量子比特的控制与读取需要高精度、低延迟、可扩展的实验硬件平台，但现有开源RFSoC控制框架在资源效率、时序确定性和可扩展性方面存在不足。论文旨在提供一个灵活、开源且低资源占用的RFSoC控制框架，既作为量子比特控制平台，又作为评估替代控制/读取IP架构的实验环境。

Method: 采用模块化AXI兼容固件架构与基于PYNQ的客户端-服务器软件栈相结合的设计。固件层实现直接RF合成与采集、触发序列、可编程脉冲生成、频分复用读取和内存高效波形缓冲；软件层采用流式数据传输与依赖感知的配置更新机制，减少参数扫描期间的主机-设备交互和重构开销。

Result: 在AMD Zynq UltraScale+ RFSoC ZCU216上，FIREQ支持最高9.3 GHz的RF脉冲生成，脉宽分辨率107 ps，事件时序分辨率1.7 ns。FPGA资源占用（尤其是BRAM）优于其他代表性开源RFSoC框架，同时保持全速率I/Q生成与采集。通过谐振腔谱学、Rabi、Ramsey和弛豫测量在超导量子比特上验证了端到端操作，测得T1=6.94 μs、T2*=13.50 μs。

Conclusion: FIREQ不仅可作为超导量子比特控制的实用平台，还能作为评估和开发替代控制/读取IP架构的实验环境，其低BRAM占用和全速率性能表明它在可扩展性和资源效率方面具有明显优势。

Abstract: We present FIREQ (FPGA Instrumentation for Readout and Qubit control), an open-source RFSoC-based framework for the control and readout of superconducting qubits. FIREQ combines a modular AXI-compliant firmware architecture with a PYNQ-based software stack designed to support extensible hardware integration, deterministic experiment timing, and low-overhead execution of repeated calibration and characterization workflows. The firmware implements direct RF synthesis and acquisition, trigger-based sequencing, programmable pulse generation, frequency-multiplexed readout, and memory-efficient acquisition and waveform buffering. The software adopts a client-server architecture with streamed data transfer and dependency-aware configuration updates to reduce host-device and reconfiguration overhead during parameter sweeps. On an AMD Zynq UltraScale+ RFSoC ZCU216, FIREQ generates RF pulses up to 9.3 GHz with a pulse-duration resolution of 107 ps and an event-timing resolution of 1.7 ns. FPGA resource utilization is compared with representative open-source RFSoC control frameworks, showing a low BRAM footprint while retaining full-rate I/Q generation and acquisition. The RF output is characterized in terms of phase noise, noise spectral density, and inter-channel timing skew. End-to-end operation is validated on a superconducting qubit through resonator spectroscopy, Rabi, Ramsey, and relaxation measurements, yielding T1 = 6.94 us and T2* = 13.50 us. FIREQ can therefore be used both as a qubit-control platform and as an experimental environment for evaluating alternative control and readout IP architectures.

</details>


### [236] [The ZZ feature map induces a signless Laplacian metric: a closed-form classical surrogate for quantum kernel regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29422)
*Erkut Tekeli*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明ZZ特征映射在低带宽条件下产生的量子核等价于各向异性高斯核，其度量由纠缠图的符号拉普拉斯矩阵决定，并验证了该经典核在预测性能上与量子核无显著差异，从而精确定位了量子电路所计算的经典核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前观察到带宽调优后的量子核与径向基函数核极为相似，但理论支撑仅适用于可分离编码电路，对纠缠电路只有定性描述。作者希望为ZZ特征映射这一具体纠缠电路给出严格的解析证明，明确其在小带宽极限下对应的经典核形式。

Method: 对ZZ特征映射在小带宽（编码参数小）条件下进行解析展开，证明诱导核的leading order为各向异性高斯核，度量M = I + π²Q，其中Q为纠缠图的符号拉普拉斯矩阵；进一步证明该二次结构在任意电路深度下都是Fubini-Study度量的拉回。通过路径图、环图和完全图上的直接仿真验证相对误差低于10⁻³。随后在近红外光谱数据集上通过配对bootstrap检验、预处理管道选择一致性比较等方式进行实证验证。

Result: 解析展开证明各向异性高斯核成立且与仿真一致（相对误差<10⁻³）。各向异性完全取决于相位约定：非偏移约定下度量为单位阵，解释了此前的各向同性观察。实证上，20个单元中有18个的配对95% bootstrap区间包含零，典型测试误差相对差异仅2.7%，两个核族在96%的分裂中选择相同的预处理管道；在经典核失效的带宽区域量子核无额外预测价值，限制到经典区域可使平均测试误差提升3.8%。

Conclusion: 带宽调优后的ZZ特征映射量子核精确等同于一个无需拟合参数、无需量子仿真的经典各向异性高斯核，量子电路可在无预测损失的前提下被移除。该研究填补了纠缠编码电路下量子核经典等价性的解析空白，为量子核方法提供了明确的解构。

Abstract: Quantum kernel methods lose their advantage over classical kernels once the encoding bandwidth is tuned, and bandwidth-tuned quantum kernels have been shown to resemble radial basis function kernels closely. The analytical support for that observation rests on separable encoding circuits and captures entangling circuits only qualitatively. We close this gap for the ZZ feature map. We prove that in the small-bandwidth regime the induced kernel is, to leading order, an anisotropic Gaussian kernel with metric M = I + pi^2 Q, where Q is the signless Laplacian of the entanglement graph, and that the quadratic structure persists at every circuit depth as a pullback of the Fubini-Study metric. The anisotropy depends entirely on a phase convention: under the unshifted convention the metric is the identity irrespective of entanglement, which explains the isotropic resemblance previously reported. The derivation is verified against direct simulation for path, cycle and complete entanglement graphs, with relative error below 10^-3. The corresponding classical kernel requires no fitted parameters and no quantum simulation. Compared with the quantum kernel across two near-infrared spectroscopic benchmarks, four targets, five preprocessing pipelines and 100 resampled splits per cell, the paired 95% bootstrap interval contains zero in 18 of 20 cells, the typical relative difference in test error is 2.7%, and the two families select the same preprocessing pipeline in 96% of splits. The regime in which the reduction fails begins at the same bandwidth on both datasets and adds no robust predictive value: restricting the grid to the classical regime improves mean test error by 3.8%, a gain that an equally large random restriction does not reproduce. The quantum circuit can thus be removed without detectable predictive loss, and we can state precisely which classical kernel it was computing.

</details>


### [237] [A statistical model for quantum spin and photon number states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2304.13535)
*Sam Powers,Guangpeng Xu,Herbert Fotso,Tim Thomay,Dejan Stojkovic*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于二元序列（0和1）构造量子态的新形式主义，将量子理论的概率归结为更基本的"本体状态"计数，并建立了一个基于Z2循环群、集合论和组合数学的自洽框架。该框架用于计算任意自旋粒子通过连续旋转的Stern-Gerlach探测器时的概率分布，与量子力学预言在序列长度趋于无穷时精确一致（有限n修正为O(1/n)），且无自由参数。模型还扩展到分束器系统中的光子数态，并提出了桌面实验的测试方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索量子理论概率的更基本微观基础，试图用最基本的二元符号序列来构造量子态，以提供对量子现象更深入的理解。作者希望建立一个不依赖额外自由参数、与现有量子力学预言一致的替代性形式主义，并保持与Bell不等式、Kochen-Specker定理和PBR定理等重要无go定理的一致性。

Method: 作者构建了一种基于有限循环群Z2={0,1}、集合论和组合数学的完全自洽形式主义。核心思想是将量子理论中的概率归结为对"本体状态"（事件网络）的计数，并用二元序列建模。关键规则是将计数标签转换为物理旋转角度的"半角定律"tan(θ_ab/2)=B̃_map/Ã_map，该赋值由连续旋转的合成一致性唯一确定。方法还扩展到光子数态通过分束器的光学系统，并利用高精度实验数据提出桌面实验测试方案。

Result: 计算得到任意自旋粒子通过旋转Stern-Gerlach探测器序列的概率分布，在序列长度n趋于无穷时与非相对论量子力学预言精确一致，有限n修正以O(1/n)速度消失，且无自由或拟合参数。有限n的残余偏差不违反相关的no-go定理（Bell不等式、Kochen-Specker定理、PBR定理）。

Conclusion: 作者得出该二元序列计数模型可以作为量子理论的一个可行替代基础，能够在无自由参数的情况下复现量子力学预言，同时保持与关键no-go定理的一致性。模型可扩展到光学系统，并提出了可实施的高精度桌面实验来测试该模型的物理有效性。

Abstract: The most irreducible way to represent information is a sequence of two symbols. In this paper, we construct quantum states using this basic building block. Specifically, we show that the probabilities that arise in quantum theory can be reduced to counting more fundamental ontic states, which we interpret as event networks and model using sequences of 0's and 1's. A completely self contained formalism is developed for the purpose of organizing and counting these ontic states, which employs the finite cyclic group $\mathbb{Z}_2 = \{0, 1\}$, basic set theory, and combinatorics. This formalism is then used to calculate probability distributions associated with particles of arbitrary spin interacting with sequences of two rotated Stern-Gerlach detectors. A central ingredient of this construction is the rule which converts the abstract counts labelling an operation into the physical angle of rotation it represents. We show that this rule is not linear in the counts, but is instead fixed by the half-angle law $\tan(θ_{ab}/2)=\tilde{B}_{map}/\tilde{A}_{map}$, which is the unique assignment consistent with the composition of successive rotations. These calculations are compared with the predictions of non-relativistic quantum mechanics and shown to agree exactly in the limit of large sequence length $n$, with finite $n$ corrections which vanish as $O(1/n)$ and with no free or fitted parameters. The residual deviation at finite $n$ does not lead to violations of relevant no-go theorems, such as Bell's inequalities, the Kochen-Specker theorem, or the PBR theorem. The proposed model is then extended to an optical system involving photon number states passing through a beam splitter. Leveraging recent advancements in high precision experiments on these systems, we then propose a means of testing the new model using a tabletop experiment.

</details>


### [238] [Bosonic codes from compact phase spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31156)
*David Roberts,Aaron Slipper,Alireza Parhizkar,Victor V. Albert,Mohammad Hafezi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了黎曼面上玻色子量子纠错码的代数结构，在亏格为2的黎曼面上构造了作为自守形式的码字，并证明了所有权重下码空间的完整塔结构。核心是一个no-go定理：当亏格大于1时，稳定子群是非可服的（non-amenable），导致稳定子哈密顿量具有严格正谱隙，因此不存在可归一化的量子态满足所有稳定子条件。这与标准GKP码形成鲜明对比——后者因稳定子群Z²的可服性允许任意精度的近似码字。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准GKP码基于欧几里得晶格（Z²稳定子群），其可服性保证了近似码字的构造可行性。作者试图将GKP码推广到更高亏格的黎曼面上，并探究在这种更复杂的拓扑结构中量子纠错码的代数性质与可行性边界，从而揭示稳定子群的群论性质（可服性）与量子纠错能力之间的根本联系。

Method: 作者在亏格为2的黎曼面上显式构造了作为自守形式的码字，利用自守形式的代数结构在所有权重下解析地生成完整的码空间塔。通过分析高亏格（g>1）黎曼面上稳定子群的群论性质，证明该群是非可服的，并据此推导出稳定子哈密顿量的正谱隙以及不存在可归一化精确码字的结论。

Result: 证明了亏格大于1时稳定子群的非可服性，由此推出稳定子哈密顿量具有严格正谱隙，且不存在可归一化的量子态满足全部稳定子条件。这一结果为高亏格黎曼面上的精确玻色子量子纠错码设定了根本性的不可能性边界。

Conclusion: 高亏格黎曼面上的精确玻色子量子纠错码在数学上是不存在的，其根源在于稳定子群的非可服性而非具体构造方法的局限。这与GKP码形成鲜明对照：GKP码的可服稳定子群Z²保障了近似码字可达到任意精度。因此，若要在高亏格拓扑结构上实现玻色子纠错，需要转向近似方案或其他非稳定子编码策略。

Abstract: We present the algebraic structure of bosonic quantum error-correcting codes on genus-two Riemann surfaces. We explicitly construct the code words as automorphic forms and analytically generate the full tower of code spaces at all weights. We prove a fundamental no-go theorem: for any genus greater than one, the stabilizer group is non-amenable, forcing a strictly positive spectral gap in the stabilizer Hamiltonian. Consequently, no normalizable quantum state can satisfy all stabilizer conditions. This sharply contrasts with standard Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) codes, where the amenability of the stabilizer group $\mathbb{Z}^2$ permits approximate code words with arbitrary precision.

</details>


### [239] [The resource cost of magic in a code block](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29438)
*Jiachen Shen,Hui Zhong*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究后选择（post-selected）逻辑测量中魔法资源（magic resource）的界限问题。作者证明，当一个代码块内包含单个逻辑量子比特并采用自适应测量-前馈-接受协议时，接受的魔法效应（magic effect）受到产生它的资源约束。无条件界表明接受的魔法最多为资源单元到自由集距离之和的常数倍。主要结果在"有界扩散精确恢复骨架"条件下成立：当阈值正比于码距时，具有有界插入度、单格扩张振幅O(1/d)的多项式个单元，其接受魔法乘以接受概率至多为exp[-Ω(d log d)]。作者通过一个具体的电路（稳定子测量加分裂读出）证明了阈值等于码距，其中测量逻辑X（接受分支）和逻辑Z（拒绝分支）的分裂读出可认证单层弱Z旋转模式下的码距阈值，且仅当支持集包含逻辑Z串时该族成员才携带魔法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在量子纠错与容错量子计算中，魔法态（magic states）是超越Clifford可育区所需的稀缺资源。一个关键问题是：在带后选择的逻辑测量协议中，测量结果所体现的魔法效应是否可能被系统性放大——即一个概率极小的分支是否可能产生不成比例的魔法效应。论文动机在于对后选择逻辑测量的魔法效应给出严格的资源边界，特别是在真实电路实现下验证这些边界条件是否可满足。

Method: 论文采用资源理论框架分析。核心方法包括：(1) 逐结果（outcome-by-outcome）而非平均信道地比较接受效应与魔法自由集的距离；(2) 使用"有界扩散精确恢复骨架"（bounded-spread exact-recovery skeleton）结构，在其中构造逐转录本（transcript-by-transcript）的恢复映射，将所有插入历史中低于阈值的路径映射到单一自由分支上；(3) 通过精确分量展开（exact-component expansion）控制连接簇的权重；(4) 构造具体电路——一轮稳定子测量加分裂读出（split readout），该读出在接受的纤维上测量逻辑X、在拒绝的纤维上测量逻辑Z，从而从电路层面认证码距大小的阈值。

Result: 论文得到两个主要结果：无条件界为接受的魔法不超过资源单元到自由集距离总和的常数倍；在有界扩散精确恢复骨架条件下，当阈值线性于码距、每个单元插入度有界且扩张振幅为O(1/d)时，接受魔法乘以接受概率至多为exp[-Ω(d log d)]。分裂读出电路认证了阈值等于码距，适用于所有单层弱Z旋转模式（每个数据量子比特一个旋转）的族，且仅当成员的支持集含逻辑Z串时才携带魔法。某个成员确实达到了该指数界，且抑制效果由阈值而非块的拓扑决定。

Conclusion: 后选择逻辑测量的魔法效应可以被其产生的资源严格限制。通过放弃平均信道视角而采用逐结果分析，论文表明在合理的电路实现（分裂读出）下，阈值可以干净地达到码距，使得魔法效应以exp[-Ω(d log d)]被指数抑制。后选择不会将低概率分支放大为魔法效应，因为接受转录本的求和发生在后选择被处理之后。这一结果对量子计算机中魔法资源的编目和管理具有重要理论意义。

Abstract: We bound the magic of a post-selected logical measurement by the resource that produced it. The setting is one code block with one logical qubit and an adaptive protocol that measures, feeds forward and accepts. The witness reads the accepted effect against the free set of the resource theory of magic, outcome by outcome and not on the averaged channel, since a channel can be free while one of its outcomes measures the magic axis. Our first bound is unconditional. The accepted magic is at most a constant times the summed distance of the cells from the free set. The second is the main result. When the resource cells sit inside a bounded-spread exact-recovery skeleton, the recovery puts every insertion history below a threshold onto a single free branch, transcript by transcript, so only connected clusters reaching the threshold contribute and an exact-component expansion controls their weight. With a threshold linear in the code distance, polynomially many cells of bounded insertion degree and per-cell dilation amplitude $O(1/d)$, the accepted magic times the acceptance probability is at most $\exp[-Ω(d\log d)]$. Post-selection is disposed of before accepted transcripts are summed, so a branch of vanishing probability cannot be amplified into a magic effect. The threshold is certified from a circuit, and we run it on one exact round of stabilizer measurement followed by a split readout, which measures logical $X$ on the accepted fibre and logical $Z$ on the rejected ones. That certifies a threshold equal to the code distance for every single-layer pattern of weak $Z$-rotations, one per data qubit, so the hypotheses are met by a family and not one design. A member carries magic only if its support contains a logical $Z$ string. One member attains the exponent, again at the level of the accepted effect. Suppression is set by the threshold and not by the topology of the block.

</details>


### [240] [Quantum Pessiland](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29493)
*Boyang Chen,Tomoyuki Morimae,Takashi Yamakawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了"量子悲观岛"(Quantum Pessiland)的存在性：存在量子预言机使得UP∩coUP在平均情形下对带量子建议的量子多项式时间算法困难，但辅助输入EFI对不存在；同时存在经典预言机使得UP∩coUP平均困难但类安全辅助输入单向谜题不存在。这意味着在这些预言机下几乎没有量子密码学，且SampBQP=SampBPP意味着没有基于采样的量子优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典世界中Pessiland指的是NP平均困难但单向函数不存在的世界，几乎所有经典密码学都无法存在。而量子密码学即使在OWF不存在时也可以存在（如EFI对、单向谜题等）。自然的问题是：是否存在一个类似Pessiland的世界，使得NP（或相关类）平均困难但量子密码学也不存在？回答这个问题对于理解量子密码学的本质边界和分离相对化/非相对化证明技术具有重要理论意义。

Method: 作者构造了两种预言机：(1) 量子预言机，相对于该预言机UP∩coUP对带量子建议的量子多项式时间算法平均困难，且辅助输入EFI对不存在；(2) 经典预言机，相对于该预言机UP∩coUP平均困难，但类安全辅助输入OWPuzzs不存在。利用几乎全部量子密码学原语都蕴含EFI对或OWPuzzs这一事实，得出在这些预言机下几乎不存在量子密码学。同时证明了相对于经典预言机有SampBQP=SampBPP。

Result: (1) 证明了量子Pessiland的存在性：存在量子预言机使得平均情形困难性与量子密码学的不存在性可以同时成立。(2) 存在经典预言机下同样结论成立，且SampBQP=SampBPP说明无采样型量子优势。(3) 由于UP∩coUP的平均困难性蕴含P^#P不包含于i.o.BQP/qpoly，该结果还暗示构造仅基于P^#P不包含于i.o.BQP/qpoly的OWPuzzs必然需要非相对化证明技术，对Khurana和Tomer 2025年的开放问题给出了部分否定回答。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一个"量子悲观世界"——在该世界中即使NP变体问题平均困难，量子密码学也几乎完全不存在。这界定了量子密码学相对于经典密码学在Pessiland型世界中的优势边界，并揭示了构造OWPuzzs所需证明技术的局限性（必然需要非相对化技术）。

Abstract: Pessiland is a world where NP is hard on average but one-way functions (OWFs) do not exist [Impagliazzo 1995]. Because almost all classical cryptographic primitives imply OWFs [Impagliazzo and Luby 1989], there is almost no classical cryptography in Pessiland. On the other hand, quantum cryptography can exist even when OWFs do not [Kretschmer 2021; Morimae and Yamakawa 2022; Ananth, Qian and Yuen 2022]. Is there a quantum analogue of Pessiland where NP is hard on average but even quantum cryptography does not exist? In this paper, we show that such a miserable world, Quantum Pessiland, exists: there is a quantum oracle relative to which $UP\cap coUP$ is hard on average against quantum polynomial-time algorithms with quantum advice, yet auxiliary-input EFI pairs do not exist. We also show that there is a classical oracle relative to which $UP\cap coUP$ is hard on average against quantum polynomial-time algorithms with quantum advice, yet classically-secure auxiliary-input one-way puzzles (OWPuzzs) do not exist. Almost all quantum cryptographic primitives imply EFI pairs or OWPuzzs, and therefore these results mean that there is almost no quantum cryptography relative to these oracles. We further show that relative to the classical oracle, SampBQP = SampBPP, and therefore there is no sampling-based quantum advantage in Quantum Pessiland. Finally, because our average-case hardness of $UP\cap coUP$ implies $P^{\#P}\not\subseteq i.o.BQP/qpoly$, our result also implies that a non-relativizing proof technique is necessary to construct OWPuzzs solely from $P^{\#P}\not\subseteq i.o.BQP/qpoly$, which gives a partial negative answer to the open problem of [Khurana and Tomer 2025].

</details>


### [241] [Demonstration of traveling-wave interactions between spontaneous photon emissions and atoms in a chiral F-P cavity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29515)
*Jiajin Lu,Minjie Wang,Haole Jiao,Xiang Chen,Hongze Zhang,Shujing Li,Hai Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在F-P腔中实验展示了行波原子-光相互作用，通过在F-P腔内产生非经典关联的光子-自旋波对，利用圆偏振发射场在腔内的传播方向依赖性，实现了保持光螺旋度的行波相互作用，解决了线性F-P腔中驻波模式导致的耦合不均匀和量子存储寿命短的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性F-P腔中发射场为驻波模式，导致原子-光子耦合不均匀以及腔增强自旋波量子存储寿命短。为了克服这一限制，需要实现能保持光螺旋度（helicity）的行波原子-光相互作用。

Method: 1) 沿z轴施加偏置磁场定义量子化轴，消除塞曼简并并破缺时间反演对称性；2) 基于Duan-Lukin-Cirac-Zoller方案产生非经典关联的Stokes光子与自旋波对；3) 单圆偏振原子跃迁发射的Stokes光子沿+z（前向）和-z（后向）传播时分别为左旋和右旋圆偏振，在手性腔中保持；4) 前向和后向Stokes场与腔共振时以行波方式与原子相互作用；5) 通过测量与前向和后向Stokes场关联的自旋波检索效率来验证。

Result: 实验证实了前向和后向Stokes场均能以行波方式与原子相互作用，通过时间依赖的检索效率测量验证了手性腔中光螺旋度的保持以及行波原子-光相互作用的存在。

Conclusion: 该工作为在F-P腔中演示行波原子-光相互作用开辟了道路，有望改善量子光学和原子物理平台中腔增强自旋波量子存储的性能。

Abstract: The enhancement of atom-photon interactions with F-P cavities provides a suitable platform for studying quantum optics and atomic physics. However, the emission fields in linear F-P cavities are in the standing-wave mode, which leads to non-uniform atom-photon coupling and a short storage lifetime of cavity-enhanced spin-wave quantum storages. This study experimentally demonstrates traveling-wave atom-light interactions in an F-P cavity that can preserve light helicity. First, a bias magnetic field is applied along the z-axis to define the quantization axis, which lifts the Zeeman degeneracy and breaks the time reversal symmetry. Next, non-classically correlated pairs of Stokes photons and spin waves are produced based on the Duan-Lukin-Cirac-Zoller scheme. The Stokes photons initially emitted from a single circularly polarized atomic transition have left-and right-hand circular polarizations when propagating along the +z (forward) and -z (backward) directions, which are preserved in the chiral cavity within the atom-photon interaction region. Thus, when the forward and backward Stokes fields resonate with the cavity, they may interact with the atoms in a traveling-wave manner. This is confirmed by measuring the time-dependent retrieval efficiencies of spin waves correlated with the forward and backward Stokes fields. This work paves the way for demonstrating traveling-wave atom-photon interactions in F-P cavities.

</details>


### [242] [Thresholdless dynamical instability in transverse $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric scattering systems: The hidden role of bound states in the continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29557)
*Chao Zheng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了横向PT对称系统中由对称性保护的连续谱束缚态（BIC）驱动的无阈值动力学不稳定性，指出即使散射矩阵为幺正的系统也可能出现时间增长的束缚态，并建立了微观稳定性判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
