Motivation: 横向PT对称系统的稳态散射特性已被广泛研究，但动力学稳定性（任何稳态描述的前提）尚未被充分探索。理解何时系统在动力学上稳定，对于设计可实际运行的PT对称散射器件至关重要。

Method: 通过解析分析S矩阵极点与零点在复波数平面上的行为，利用对称性保护的BIC作为核心机制：Hermitian对称破缺微扰使极点半平面下移（产生费米黄金规则衰变宽度），而反Hermitian PT对称微扰则反转二阶能量移位符号，将极点推入上半平面，导致时间增长束缚态。在三维侧耦合模型和四维菱形模型中验证该机制。

Result: 发现当BIC与连续谱的黄金规则耦合Γ_V>0时，不稳定性在任意小的增益-损耗强度γ下即出现（无阈值）。不稳定性增长率满足Im E = γ²Γ_V/2 + O(γ³)。即使系统具有幺正散射矩阵（三站点侧耦合模型），仍可能动力学不稳定；横向与纵向增益-损耗布置分别产生零和有限阈值（四站点菱形模型）。

Conclusion: 建立了横向PT对称系统的微观稳定性判据，为设计稳定PT对称散射器件提供了原理性指导。散射矩阵幺正性不能保证动力学稳定性，必须检查BIC相关的黄金规则耦合是否为零。

Abstract: The stationary scattering properties of transverse parity-time ($\mathcal{PT}$) symmetric systems have been extensively studied, yet their dynamical stability, a prerequisite for any stationary description, remains largely unexplored. Here we uncover a thresholdless dynamical instability in such systems, driven by symmetry-protected bound states in the continuum (BICs). Without gain and loss, the up-down mirror symmetry of the transverse geometry generically protects a BIC, which manifests as the coalescence of an $S$-matrix pole and zero on the real axis of the complex wave-number plane. A Hermitian symmetry-breaking perturbation shifts the poles into the lower half-plane, converting the BIC into a resonance with a Fermi-golden-rule decay width. An anti-Hermitian $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric perturbation instead reverses the sign of the second-order energy shift, driving the poles into the upper half-plane and producing a time-growing bound state with $\operatorname{Im}E=γ^{2}Γ_{V}/2+O(γ^{3})$, where $γ$ is the gain-loss strength and $Γ_{V}$ is the golden-rule coupling of the BIC to the continuum. The instability therefore sets in at arbitrarily small $γ$ whenever $Γ_{V}>0$. We confirm this mechanism in a three-site side-coupled model that is unstable despite possessing a unitary scattering matrix, and in a four-site rhombic model where transverse and longitudinal gain-loss placements yield vanishing and finite thresholds, respectively. These results establish a microscopic stability criterion and a design principle for stable $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric scattering devices.

</details>


### [243] [Indirect stabilization of N -level quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30308)
*Francesca Carlotta Chittaro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究通过带噪声的辅助量子比特（ancilla）进行间接控制来实现N能级量子系统的可镇定性。在建立任意N的一般理论框架后，重点分析了N=2的情况，推导出渐近稳定性的完备充要条件，并通过双自旋系统的数值示例展示了随机初始条件下收敛到贝尔态的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接控制量子系统在物理实现上往往受到技术限制，间接控制架构（通过辅助系统进行控制）是一种重要的替代方案。然而，辅助系统本身携带噪声时，系统的可镇定性分析变得更加复杂。本文旨在系统性地回答：在存在噪声的间接控制架构下，量子系统何时能够被镇定到目标状态。

Method: 首先建立适用于任意N能级量子系统的间接控制一般理论框架；随后针对两能级（N=2）系统，推导出一组完备的渐近稳定性必要且充分条件；最后通过一个双自旋系统的数值仿真示例验证理论分析的有效性。

Result: 对两能级量子系统给出了渐近稳定性的完备充要条件，并在双自旋系统上通过数值实验展示了系统在随机初始条件下能够收敛到贝尔态。

Conclusion: 本文为带噪辅助量子比特的间接控制架构提供了严格的稳定性分析框架，证明了在满足所推条件时，即使辅助系统存在噪声，量子系统仍能被渐近镇定到目标状态（如贝尔态），为量子控制的实际实现提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We study the stabilizability of a N -level quantum systems using an indirect control ar- chitecture mediated by a noisy ancilla. After outlining the general theoretical framework for arbitrary N , we focus on the two-level case (N = 2), for which we derive a complete set of necessary and sufficient conditions for asymptotic stability. Finally, we illustrate the analyti- cal results with a simple numerical example on a two-spin system, showing convergence to a Bell state for a set of random initial conditions.

</details>


### [244] [Evolution Generators for Complex Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29602)
*Jen-Yin Yeh,Chia-Yi Ju*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过Wirtinger导数将演化生成元形式从实值参数扩展到复值参数，推导了量子态和Hilbert空间丛度量的演化方程，并证明度量演化需要生成元及其共轭的耦合贡献以保持态归一化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子系统受外部因素影响的理论研究通常通过Hamiltonian中的参数变化来表述。已有的演化生成元框架适用于实值参数，但外部因素往往涉及复值参数（如增益损耗、复数势能等），因此需要将该形式推广到复参数情形。

Method: 将复参数及其共轭视为独立变量，利用Wirtinger导数推导量子态和Hilbert空间丛度量的演化方程。通过显式算例验证该形式的实现方式与物理一致性。

Result: 分析表明，量子态关于复参数的演化自然地由相应的演化生成元控制；而度量演化则需要生成元与其共轭部分的耦合贡献，以保证态归一化的保持。显式算例验证了该形式主义的物理一致性。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将演化生成元形式推广到复值参数域，为非Hermitian量子体系中复参数驱动的动力学提供了统一的理论框架，深化了对Hilbert空间束几何结构的理解。

Abstract: Theoretical studies on how quantum systems are affected by external factors are often formulated through parameter changes in the system's Hamiltonian. Beyond Berry connections and phases, which focus on specific Hamiltonian eigenstates, recent studies inspired by non-Hermitian quantum formalisms provide a framework for obtaining general state evolution generators for real-valued parameters. This study extends the applicability of the evolution generator formalism to complex-valued parameters via Wirtinger derivatives. By treating a complex parameter and its conjugate as independent variables, the evolution equations are derived for both quantum states and the metric of the Hilbert space bundle. The analysis demonstrates that while state evolution with respect to a complex parameter is naturally governed by its corresponding evolution generator, metric evolution requires a coupled contribution from both the generator and its conjugate counterpart to preserve state normalization. Explicit examples are worked out to illustrate the implementation and physical consistency of the formalism.

</details>


### [245] [Marginal spectral distributions on regular bipartite unitary orbits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30370)
*Lin Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了两体系统密度矩阵的随机特征基下边缘态的谱分布。通过将联合特征函数表示为Harish-Chandra-Itzykson-Zuber积分，推导出两量子比特和量子比特-量子三态情形下边缘态谱密度的显式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解随机纯态或随机密度矩阵的边缘态谱分布是量子信息理论中的核心问题，与量子纠缠、量子兼容性和量子态的可实现性密切相关。作者旨在通过数学工具给出这些分布的精确解析描述。

Method: 论文综合运用了多种数学工具：将联合特征函数表示为外部本征值为成对和的HCIZ积分；通过重合行列式极限处理重根本征值；利用分布意义下的Fourier反演、轨道测度以及SU(2)和SU(3)导数原理，推导边缘态谱密度的分段多项式公式。

Result: 在两量子比特情形下，导出了两个边缘Bloch半径联合密度的显式交错样条公式，其支撑区域为Bravyi-Klyachko兼容区域；还得到了单个量子比特边缘Bloch半径密度的紧凑截断幂公式。在量子比特-量子三态情形下，得到了量子比特Bloch半径密度的截断幂公式以及量子三态边缘最大和最小本征值的二元样条公式。

Conclusion: 所得到的密度函数在由全局本征值子集和确定的分室上是分片多项式的，这与Duistermaat-Heckman对投影余伴随轨道测度的描述一致，表明该方法具有一般性和内在统一性。

Abstract: Fix the spectrum of a bipartite density matrix and randomize its eigenbasis according to Haar measure. We study the probability distributions induced on the spectra of the two marginal states. For arbitrary subsystem dimensions $m$ and $n$, the joint characteristic function of the reduced density matrices is expressed as a Harish-Chandra-Itzykson-Zuber integral whose external eigenvalues are the pairwise sums $x_i + y_j$. Repeated external eigenvalues are handled by confluent determinant limits. In the two-qubit case, we derive an explicit alternating-spline formula for the joint density of the two marginal Bloch radii. Its support is the Bravyi-Klyachko compatibility region. We also obtain a compact truncated-power formula for the Bloch-radius density of either individual qubit marginal. In the qubit-qutrit case, we derive a truncated-power formula for the qubit Bloch-radius density and a bivariate spline formula for the joint density of the largest and smallest eigenvalues of the qutrit marginal. The latter two variables determine the full qutrit spectrum because the trace is fixed. The derivations combine confluent HCIZ integrals, distributional Fourier inversion, orbital measures, and the SU(2) and SU(3) derivative principles. The resulting densities are piece-wise polynomial on chambers determined by subset sums of the fixed global eigenvalues, in agreement with the Duistermaat-Heckman description of projected coadjoint-orbit measures.

</details>


### [246] [Asymptotic Entanglement across Dynamical Regimes of a Dissipative Bosonic Dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29672)
*Chenghe Yu,Mingsheng Tian,Feng-Xiao Sun,Qiongyi He*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究参数驱动耗散玻色二聚体中动力学不稳定性对长时间纠缠的影响，发现动力学不稳定性并不意味着无界纠缠增长：尽管玻色粒子数发散，对数负性仍然有界并趋于有限渐近值。通过非厄米动力学谱统一描述稳定与不稳定区域的粒子数和纠缠动力学，并揭示了公共浴池、暗态（BIC）保护机制对渐近纠缠的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参数驱动玻色系统中的动力学不稳定性通常与粒子数发散和无稳态相关联，但其对长时间纠缠演化的影响此前尚不明确。作者希望厘清动力学不稳定性是否必然导致无界纠缠增长，并建立统一的理论框架来刻画稳定与不稳定区域的纠缠动力学。

Method: 采用含局域耗散和可调公共浴池的耗散玻色二聚体模型，包含分束器和双模压缩相互作用。利用控制高斯矩演化的非厄米动力学谱，划分参数空间为不同动力学区域，统一描述稳定和不稳定区域的粒子数与纠缠动力学（以对数负性为纠缠度量），并引入暗态（BIC）及准BIC机制解释公共浴池增强纠缠的物理根源。

Result: （1）动力学不稳定性不导致无界纠缠增长，粒子数发散而对数负性有界并趋于有限渐近值；（2）非对称局域耗散同时改变稳定性边界和渐近纠缠非零阈值；（3）在相同局域耗散下，公共浴池不改变动力学区域边界但显著改变长时间纠缠图景，在稳定区产生宽增强区域、在不稳定区产生窄增强区域；（4）纯公共浴池极限下纠缠增强源于精确暗态（BIC）保护机制，在独立耗散下退化为准BIC残余。

Conclusion: 确立了非厄米动力学谱作为连接开放二次玻色系统中稳定性、非平衡动力学与渐近纠缠的统一框架，揭示了动力学不稳定性与纠缠增长之间的非平凡关系，为理解和调控开放量子系统中的纠缠动力学提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Dynamical instability in parametrically driven bosonic systems is generally associated with diverging occupations and the absence of a stationary state, but its implications for long-time entanglement remain unclear. We investigate this question in a dissipative bosonic dimer with beam-splitter and two-mode-squeezing interactions, local loss, and a tunable common bath. The non-Hermitian dynamical spectrum governing the Gaussian moments partitions the parameter space into distinct regimes and provides a unified description of population and entanglement dynamics across both stable and unstable regions. We find that dynamical instability does not imply unbounded entanglement growth: although the bosonic population diverges, the logarithmic negativity remains bounded and approaches a finite asymptotic value. We further characterize the finite asymptotic entanglement and show that asymmetric local dissipation shifts both the stability boundary and the threshold for nonzero asymptotic entanglement. For identical local dissipation, the presence of a common bath leaves the dynamical-region boundaries unchanged while qualitatively reshaping the long-time entanglement landscape, producing broad enhancement regions within the stable regimes and a narrow enhancement region within the unstable regime. In the common-bath-only limit, this enhancement is attributed to an exact dark-mode (bound-state-in-the-continuum, BIC) protection mechanism, which reduces to a quasi-BIC remnant under independent dissipation. Our results establish the non-Hermitian dynamical spectrum as a framework for connecting stability, nonequilibrium dynamics, and asymptotic entanglement in open quadratic bosonic systems.

</details>


### [247] [Imaging Stars at the Quantum Compatibility Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29689)
*Xinyao Guo,Haixing Miao,Zheng Cai,Huan Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用多参数量子计量学工具,证明N站干涉阵列中时间分辨的重复或自适应测量在可见度估计上存在阵列级别的根本不相容性,而集体测量(在单次联合读出中相干处理多个时间片的星光)可消除阵列规模惩罚,为可见度测量的方向平均信噪比带来渐近O(√N)的提升,并提出了记忆辅助干涉架构及成像仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多站干涉仪成像本质上是多参数量子估计问题,现有时间分辨重复/自适应测量方案在阵列级别存在可见度估计器之间的根本不相容性,导致阵列规模越大性能惩罚越严重,亟需探索能突破这一限制的测量方案。

Method: 运用多参数量子计量学理论分析时间分辨重复/自适应测量与集体测量在可见度估计上的差异;提出记忆辅助干涉架构,通过相干存储和联合量子处理实现集体读出;结合成像仿真和Fisher信息分析评估图像重建效果。

Result: 集体测量消除了阵列规模惩罚(至多仅损失阶数为1的常数因子),为方向平均的可见度测量SNR带来渐近O(√N)的增强;仿真表明集体测量可改善近期阵列的图像重建,并增强未来长基线架构的分辨能力,对NGC 4151和3C 273等AGN目标效果尤为显著。

Conclusion: 集体测量是未来量子辅助干涉阵列进行恒星成像的一个有前景的核心构建模块,能显著提升可见度测量与图像重建性能。

Abstract: Imaging astrophysical sources with a multi-station interferometer is intrinsically a multiparameter quantum-estimation problem. {Using tools from multiparameter quantum metrology,} we show that time-resolved repetitive or adaptive measurements in an \(N\)-station array suffer a fundamental array-level incompatibility among visibility estimators. Collective measurements, {which coherently process the received starlight across multiple time bins in a single joint readout}, remove the array-size penalty up to an order-unity factor, yielding an asymptotic \(O(\sqrt{N})\) enhancement for the {directional-averaged} SNR of visibility measurement. We then propose a memory-assisted interferometric architecture designed to implement collective readout through coherent storage and joint quantum processing. Imaging simulations and Fisher-information analyses demonstrate that collective measurements improve image reconstruction in near-term arrays and enhance the resolving power of future long-baseline architectures, with pronounced benefits for representative AGN targets such as NGC~4151 and 3C~273. These results highlight collective measurement as a promising building block for future quantum-assisted interferometric arrays for stellar imaging.

</details>


### [248] [Tight Global Bound on Pairwise Entanglement of Formation in Three-Qubit Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29707)
*Wei Song,Xiao-Lan Zong,Ming Yang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了三量子比特系统中两两之间纠缠形成度平方和的全局上界，该上界是紧致的，可由含最大纠缠二粒子对及不相关第三量子比特的状态达到饱和。进一步将三对二体纠缠映射为三维坐标，赋予该全局不等式几何图像，并研究了马尔可夫噪声下纠缠的动力学演化轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三量子比特系统中三对两体纠缠（即重组纠缠的分配）是量子信息中的基本问题。已有的Coﬀman-Kundu-Wootters（CKW）不等式给出了单量子比特对一个量子比特的纠缠上界，但缺少全局范围内的紧致约束。本文旨在给出三对两体纠缠形成度平方和的全局上界，并提供几何直观解释及动力学行为分析。

Method: 作者推导了三量子比特系统中三个两两纠缠形成度的平方和满足一个全局紧致不等式，并证明其可被包含最大纠缠二粒子对且第三量子比特与之不相关的状态饱和。利用该关系将三对纠缠值映射为三维空间中的三个坐标，使每个三量子比特态对应三维空间中的一个点。随后选取代表性的初态，在多种马尔可夫噪声模型（如去极化、幅值阻尼等）下研究三个两两纠缠的动力学演化，将其视为几何空间中的轨迹。

Result: 得到了一个紧致的全局上界不等式，该上界可由特定类型的三量子比特态饱和。基于该不等式构建了三维几何表示，使得纠缠分配具有直观的几何意义。同时在多种马尔可夫噪声通道下，刻画了代表性初态的纠缠动力学轨迹，揭示了不同噪声下三对纠缠演化的行为模式。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了三量子比特系统三对两体纠缠的一个全局紧致约束，并通过几何映射提供了直观解释，进一步揭示了噪声环境下纠缠演化的动力学特征。这项工作对理解多体纠缠分配结构及量子噪声下的纠缠鲁棒性具有重要意义。

Abstract: We derive a global bound on the sum of pairwise squared entanglement of formation in three-qubit systems. The bound is tight and can be saturated by states containing a maximally entangled bipartite pair with an uncorrelated third qubit. Moreover, using this relation, we can map the three bipartite entanglements to three coordinates, such that any three-qubit state corresponds to a point in three-dimensional space, thereby endowing this global inequality with a very natural geometric picture. Accordingly, the dynamical evolution can be interpreted as trajectories in this geometric space; we select representative initial states and investigate the dynamical behavior of the three pairwise entanglements under various Markovian noise models.

</details>


### [249] [The quantum supremum of the $I_{3322}$ Bell inequality is not attained in finite dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29734)
*Jef Pauwels*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了Pál和Vértesi在2010年对I_3322 Bell不等式提出的猜想：有限维量子策略在维度增长时存在一个极限值，该值是所有有限维量子策略的上确界，但没有任何有限维策略能达到该值。证明表明有限维量子关联集合在(3,3,2,2)场景中不封闭，且逼近该上确界需要无界局部维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pál和Vértesi在2010年通过数值计算发现I_3322 Bell不等式的一族有限维策略优化值随局部希尔伯特空间维度增长而收敛，并猜测该极限是所有有限维量子策略的极大值（supremum），但没有有限维策略能达到它。验证这一猜想对理解有限维量子关联集合的性质（尤其是闭合性）至关重要。

Method: 利用Bell泛函的对称性，将每个策略与一个有限概率矩阵关联，该矩阵对应Alice和Bob的谱子空间的每一对。该矩阵提供Bell值的上界。基于Pál和Vértesi发现的重复结构构造的有限维策略随维度增长逐渐逼近该上界。若该上界在有限维度中恰好达到，最优性条件将要求一个无法归一化的态，从而导出矛盾。整个证明的核心部分在Lean 4定理证明器中形式化验证。

Result: 证明了Pál-Vértesi猜想：极限值确实等于所有有限维量子策略的supremum，且没有任何有限维策略能达此值。由此推出有限维量子关联集合在(3,3,2,2)场景中不封闭，这是能发生此情况的最小Bell场景。同时，逼近该上确界需要无界局部维度。证明核心已在Lean 4中形式化。

Conclusion: 该结果首次严格证明了(3,3,2,2)场景中有限维量子关联集合不是闭集，且达到了这一非封闭性的最小Bell场景。此外，证明的形式化验证展示了使用交互式定理证明器处理量子信息领域复杂数学证明的可行性。

Abstract: In 2010, Pál and Vértesi found a family of finite-dimensional strategies for the $I_{3322}$ Bell inequality whose optimized values appeared to converge as the local Hilbert-space dimension grew. They conjectured that this limit is the supremum over all finite-dimensional quantum strategies, but that no finite-dimensional strategy attains it. We prove both claims. The proof uses the symmetry of the Bell functional to associate every strategy with a finite matrix of probabilities, one for each pair of spectral subspaces of Alice and Bob. This matrix gives an upper bound on the Bell value, and finite-dimensional strategies built from the repeating structure found by Pál and Vértesi approach it as the dimension grows. If the bound were attained exactly in finite dimension, the optimality conditions would then require a state that cannot be normalized. Consequently, the set of finite-dimensional quantum correlations is not closed in the $(3,3,2,2)$ scenario, the smallest Bell scenario where this can happen. Moreover, approaching the supremum requires unbounded local dimension. The core of the proof was formalized in Lean~4.

</details>


### [250] [Pathwise Random Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29756)
*Davide Cugini*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出路径随机哈密顿模拟（PRHS）算法，将qDrift从一阶扩展至任意阶，通过对时间步细分并利用闭式拟概率分布采样实现阶数提升，其偏置随切片数M呈阶乘衰减，查询复杂度亚多项式于1/ε，并在五个分子哈密顿量的数值实验中验证了显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: qDrift等随机乘积公式尽管消除了对哈密顿项数的多项式依赖，但受限于一阶精度，查询复杂度为O(1/ε)线性增长。需要一种能保持qDrift资源效率同时提升时间阶数的方法，以突破精度瓶颈。

Method: 将每个时间步细分为M个互相关的切片，每个切片从闭式构造且被证明唯一的拟概率分布中采样哈密顿项演化。联合优化切片数M与独立块数N，在长时间极限退化为标准qDrift，在高精度区间实现低于任意幂次的查询代价增长，无需辅助量子比特。

Result: 数值模拟五个分子哈密顿量表明，在相同查询成本下，PRHS相比qDrift可将精度提升2到4个数量级，确认了其在高精度模拟场景下的实际优势。

Conclusion: PRHS实现了对qDrift的任意阶推广，在保持无辅助比特和资源高效性的同时，从根本上改善了查询复杂度对精度的依赖，为高精度哈密顿模拟提供了更优的随机化方案。

Abstract: Randomized product formulas such as qDrift offer a resource-efficient alternative to deterministic Trotter--Suzuki decompositions for Hamiltonian simulation, removing their polynomial dependence on the number of Hamiltonian terms. qDrift, however, is intrinsically limited to first order in the evolution time, so its query complexity remains linear in the inverse of the target accuracy, $1/ε$. We introduce Pathwise Random Hamiltonian Simulation (PRHS), which extends qDrift to arbitrary order by subdividing each time step into $M$ correlated slices, each evolving under a term sampled from a quasi-probability distribution that we construct in closed form and prove unique, with a bias decaying factorially in $M$. Optimizing jointly over the number of slices $M$ and the number of independent blocks $N$ interpolates between the standard qDrift protocol at long times and a high-precision regime where the query cost grows slower than any power of $1/ε$, without requiring ancillary qubits. Numerical simulations of five molecular Hamiltonians confirm this advantage, with PRHS achieving accuracies two to four orders of magnitude beyond qDrift at equal query cost.

</details>


### [251] [Dark state as a measurable state by a dispersive readout without a Purcell limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29811)
*Wei-Chen Chien,Jyh-Yang Wang,Yen-Yu Chiang,Cheng-Chengh Huang,Lih-Chieh Hsaio,Yen-Chun Chen,Cen-Shawn Wu,Chiidong Chen,Watson Kuo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出在耦合量子系统中，暗模可以通过引入与亮模之间的ZZ相互作用，在保持接近亮态色散位移的同时，实现与谐振子光子之间的有效零耦合强度和零Purcell损耗，从而突破传统量子比特-谐振子耦合增强带来的Purcell损耗限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观念认为增强量子比特-谐振子耦合可以改善色散读取，但会引入更大的Purcell损耗。作者希望找到一种方式在保持良好可测性的同时避免Purcell限制。

Method: 提出在耦合量子系统中利用暗模式，通过暗模与亮模之间的ZZ相互作用，使暗模展现出有效的零耦合强度和零Purcell损耗。利用高阶激发态引入的高阶微扰，使得色散位移几乎与亮态相同。

Result: 暗模可以同时实现有效的零耦合强度、零Purcell损耗，同时保持与亮态几乎相同的色散位移，从而在电路量子电动力学中展示了无需Purcell限制的可测性。

Conclusion: 该工作违反并推翻了"增强耦合必然带来更大Purcell损耗"的传统规则，为电路量子电动力学系统提供了一种无Purcell限制的量子态读取方案。

Abstract: It is believed that the enhancement in qubit-resonator coupling allows a better dispersive readout but introduces greater Purcell loss. In this work, we propose that a dark mode in a coupled quantum system may violate this rule by introducing the ZZ interaction between the dark and a bright mode. The dark mode may exhibit an effective zero coupling strength, and zero Purcell loss with the resonator photons. Nevertheless, the dispersive shift is almost the same as the bright state, due to the higher order perturbation introduced by the higher excited states. Such a state demonstrates the measurability without a Purcell limit in circuit quantum electrodynamics.

</details>


### [252] [Ancilla-mediated fixed-point quantum search using Grover iterations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29839)
*Yash Prabhat,Snigdha Thakur,Ankur Raina*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于辅助比特（ancilla）介导的定点量子搜索算法，通过将解振幅映射到专用辅助比特上，利用Grover算法的实数平面反射实现鲁棒收敛，在解决"soufflé问题"（迭代次数过冲导致成功概率骤降）的同时，达到至少92.6%的成功概率和约O(√(N/M))的查询复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grover量子搜索算法虽然提供了从O(N)到O(√N)的二次加速，但其依赖精确迭代次数，存在"soufflé问题"——即过度旋转会导致成功概率急剧下降。当解的状态数量M未知时，这一限制尤为致命。现有基于相位匹配的定点方法虽然解决了该问题，但偏离了Grover原始算法的直观几何结构。

Method: 该方法采用辅助比特（ancilla）介导的定点搜索方案，将解振幅映射到专用辅助量子比特上。与现有相位匹配的定点方法不同，本方法保留了Grover算法原始的实数平面反射机制，维持了算法的直观几何架构，从而实现鲁棒的定点收敛。

Result: 实验证明，该方法在查询复杂度约为O(√(N/M))的条件下，成功概率至少达到92.6%，有效弥合了标准振幅放大与鲁棒定点收敛之间的差距。

Conclusion: 该方法为未知解数量M的场景提供了既保持Grover算法几何直觉又具有鲁棒收敛特性的量子搜索方案，解决了经典Grover算法在过旋转时的脆性问题。

Abstract: Grover's quantum search algorithm provides a fundamental quadratic speedup for unstructured datasets, reducing query complexity from $\mathcal{O}(N)$ to $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{N})$. However, the algorithm's reliance on precise iteration counts leads to the ``soufflé problem,'' where over-rotation results in a sharp decline in success probability. This limitation is particularly restrictive when the number of solution states, $M$, is unknown. In this work, we present an ancilla-mediated fixed-point quantum search algorithm that achieves robust convergence by mapping the solution amplitude to a dedicated ancilla qubit. Unlike existing phase-matching fixed-point methods, our approach utilizes Grover's real-plane reflections, thereby maintaining the intuitive geometric architecture of the original algorithm. We demonstrate that this method achieves a success probability of at least $92.6\%$ with a query complexity of approximately $\mathcal{O}(\sqrt{N/M})$, effectively bridging the gap between standard amplitude amplification and robust fixed-point convergence.

</details>


### [253] [Decay versus dephasing in Rydberg analog optimization: exchange rate, mechanism, and schedule design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29858)
*Seunghyeon Kim,Junwoo Jung*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文将Rydberg原子量子退火中的自发衰减(Γ)和退相(γ)视为两个独立噪声轴，通过数值映射发现平均近似比仅取决于组合标量u=κΓ+γ（其中κ≈8.05），而非各向同性的Γ+γ。一阶微扰论从无噪声演化即可解释该比率，并揭示机制：由于目标算符与退相算符对易，退相不能改变答案；单个驱动原子的κ≈8.5。比率κ是协议属性而非平台属性（斜坡下降部分可在2.3至16.3间移动它），且可从无噪声代价模型进行无噪声噪声感知调度优化，恢复约三分之一的马尔可夫损伤。在标定设备上两个Lindblad通道成本相当，单参数噪声模型仍可用但参数应为u。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Rydberg原子优化数值研究几乎普遍将退相干压缩成单一标量，隐含地等同对待自发衰减(Γ)和退相(γ)两种物理机制不同的噪声通道。本文质疑这种简化，旨在将二者视为独立维度，系统考察它们对量子退火启发式（用于单位圆盘最大独立集）性能的差异化影响，并探明是否存在统一的标量化方式。



Method: 在20个随机N=10的单位圆盘最大独立集图上，于(Γ,γ)平面上映射量子退火启发式的平均近似比，并在每个噪声点重新优化退火时间。采用一阶微扰论从无噪声传播推导有效交换率κ；系统扫描斜坡下降比例、系统规模、相互作用强度和估计量以检验稳健性；并通过联合调节驱动斜坡下降与扫频失谐斜坡来实施无噪声的噪声感知调度优化。

Result: 平均近似比塌缩到单一标量u=κΓ+γ，κ=8.05±0.5（统计）±1.1（系统）；各向同性Γ+γ模型残差高出30倍。一阶微扰论可从无噪声传播再现κ值（单驱动原子κ≈8.5），机制为目标的算符基与退相算符对易，驱动关闭后退相无法改变结果。κ随斜坡下降部分在2.3-16.3间变化，但在固定调度下对系统尺寸、相互作用强度和估计量稳健。无噪声调度优化联合调节驱动斜坡与失谐斜坡可恢复约1/3的马尔可夫损伤；每单位速率衰减成本为退相的八倍，但T1经分支比b≈0.4加权后两通道成本相当。

Conclusion: 自发衰减与退相不可等同对待，但可以被非平凡组合量u=κΓ+γ统一描述。该交换率κ本质上是协议（特别是斜坡形状）的属性而非仅平台属性，因此可以针对设备的具体噪声通道混合进行无噪声调度优化。虽然单参数噪声模型依然可用，但正确参数应为u而非Γ+γ。

Abstract: Numerical studies of noisy Rydberg-atom optimization almost universally compress decoherence into a single scalar, silently pricing spontaneous decay ($Γ$) and dephasing ($γ$) alike. We treat the two as independent axes, mapping a quantum-annealing heuristic for unit-disk maximum independent set on 20 random $N=10$ graphs across the $(Γ,γ)$ plane, with the annealing time re-optimized at every point. The mean approximation ratio does collapse onto one scalar, but onto $u=κΓ+γ$ with $κ=8.05\pm0.5$ (stat) $\pm1.1$ (syst); the isotropic $Γ+γ$ fails by a factor of 30 in residual. First-order perturbation theory reproduces $κ$ from noiseless propagation alone and gives the mechanism: the objective is diagonal in the basis of the dephasing operator, so dephasing cannot change the answer once the drive is off, and a single driven atom already has $κ\simeq8.5$. The exchange rate is thus a property of the protocol as much as of the platform: the ramp-down fraction moves it between 2.3 and 16.3. At a fixed schedule it is stable across system sizes, interaction strengths, and estimators. Because the whole cost model is noiseless, a schedule can be tuned to a device's channel mixture without any noisy simulation: jointly tuning the drive ramp-down and the sweep's detuning ramp recovers about a third of the Markovian damage at no hardware cost, and the ramp the noise-aware objective selects is not the one noiseless optimization would choose. Per unit rate decay is eightfold the dearer channel, but the measured $T_1$ enters weighted by its branching ratio to the ground state ($b\approx0.4$ for the calibrated device), which leaves the two Lindblad channels comparably costly at a present-day operating point. One-parameter noise models remain serviceable, provided the parameter is $u$.

</details>


### [254] [Exact Resource Laws for Passive Wavelength Routing in Entanglement Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29911)
*Ekta Panwar,Gilberto Borges,Saeid Salari,Kartik Kakade,Samgeeth Puliyil,Peter Rapčan,Mario Ziman,Djeylan Aktas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过网络连接图将纠缠光子网络的波长分配建模为资源优化问题，导出了任意网络与扇出限制下的精确优化方案，并针对完全网络及含排斥伙伴的完全网络给出求解；同时提出平衡二叉树层级结构在完全网络中实现最小谱层数并降低接收端负载至对数级，结合被动分束损耗与密钥率关系，给出连续泵浦宽带源下的BBM92 QKD安全密钥率分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有纠缠网络实验已展示多种波长分配方案，但缺乏一般性框架来刻画频谱利用、接收端负载、重复连接与扇出之间的相互约束关系，难以指导实际网络设计与硬件参数选择。

Method: 将波长分配转化为基于网络连接图的资源优化问题；对单侧扇出情况，采用"每条链路分配到中心、同中心链路分组"的方法获得任意网络与扇出限制下的精确最优解；对双侧共轭波长扇出，提出平衡二叉树层级方案以最小化谱层数并降低接收负载至对数；结合被动分束损耗、配对通量对密钥率的影响，计算所需最小配对生成率，并对连续波泵浦宽带源进行BBM92 QKD分析与真假符合计数评估。

Result: 在单侧扇出下求得完全网络及每个用户有一个排斥伙伴的完全网络的精确最优解；双侧扇出下平衡二叉树在完全网络中实现最小谱层数、接收端负载降至用户数对数级；通过八用户完全网络示例阐明谱效率、接收负载、冗余与扇出间的竞争关系；建立了从网络资源规律到被动纠缠架构设计与比较的完整框架。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了从精确网络资源律到被动纠缠网络设计与实验硬件约束下架构比较的直接路径，为多用户量子通信网络的规模化设计奠定了理论框架。

Abstract: Entanglement-based networks provide a scalable framework for multiuser quantum communication by passively routing spectrally correlated photon pairs across interconnected nodes. Several wavelength-allocation schemes have been demonstrated experimentally, but these designs do not yet give a general way to determine how spectral use, receiver load, repeated connections, and fan-out constrain one another. We address this problem through the network's connectivity graph, where the wavelength assignment becomes a resource-optimization problem. For one-sided fan-out, assigning each link to a center and grouping links with the same center gives an exact optimization for arbitrary networks and fan-out limits. We solve this for complete networks and for complete networks in which every user has one excluded partner. Allowing both conjugate wavelengths to fan out changes the resource landscape: a balanced binary hierarchy attains the minimum spectral-layer count for a complete network while reducing the maximum receiver load to logarithmic in the number of users. An eight-user complete network then makes explicit the competing roles of spectral efficiency, receiver load, redundancy, and fan-out. We include the passive-splitter loss and the dependence of the key rate on the delivered pair flux to determine the minimum total pair-generation rate required to meet the prescribed targets. Finally, we formulate the corresponding BBM92 quantum key distribution (QKD) secret-key-rate analysis for a continuous-wave-pumped broadband source, with true and accidental coincidences evaluated between detector channels at the two endpoint users and relative layer pair-generation rates fixed by the source spectrum. This framework, therefore, provides a direct route from exact network resource laws to the design and comparison of passive entanglement architectures under experimentally specified hardware constraints.

</details>


### [255] [Emergence of Strategic Equilibria from Transverse Field Ising Hamiltonian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29926)
*Teena Thomas,S. Balakrishnan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于横向场量子伊辛模型，提出一种算子形式的量子博弈框架，通过哈密顿量驱动动力学自然生成纠缠算子来解决博弈困境，实现优于经典结果的量子优势，并提供可调纠缠的硬件相关性量子博弈设计视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典博弈论可以通过伊辛模型等相互作用模型来描述，而量子博弈允许玩家利用量子叠加与纠缠。现有量子化方案通常依赖于固定的纠缠门，缺乏可调节性和硬件相关性，因此需要一种更灵活、物理上更自然的量子博弈框架。

Method: 本文采用基于横向场量子伊辛模型推导出的算子公式化方法来研究量子博弈。通过哈密顿量驱动的动力学过程，自然生成纠缠算子，并利用哈密顿物理参数直接控制纠缠程度与博弈过程。

Result: 研究表明，哈密顿量驱动的动力学可自然生成纠缠算子，能够解决博弈困境，并展现出相对于经典结果的明确量子优势。此外，该方法实现了可调纠缠，优于依赖固定纠缠门的标准量化方案。

Conclusion: 本工作提供了一个硬件相关的量子博弈设计新视角，通过物理哈密顿参数实现纠缠的可控调节，为量子博弈理论在真实量子硬件上的实现奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: Game theory studies strategic decision-making among rational agents, and many classical games can be mapped onto interaction models such as the Ising model. Quantum game theory extends this framework by allowing players to exploit quantum superposition and entanglement. In this work, we study quantum games using an operator-based formulation derived from the transverse-field quantum Ising model. We show that the Hamiltonian-driven dynamics naturally generate entangling operator which resolve the dilemma in the game settings. This leads to a clear quantum advantage over classical outcomes. Unlike standard quantization schemes based on fixed entangling gates, the present approach enables tunable entanglement, controlled directly by physical Hamiltonian parameters, providing a hardware-relevant perspective on quantum game design.

</details>


### [256] [The MS Unidirectional Current as a Generalization of the ABC Absorption Current](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29969)
*Avi Marchewka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了一维半线几何中自由标量粒子的Robin吸收边界条件可嵌入广义Marchewka-Schuss(MS)单方向流族：每个Robin参数κ对应MS谱函数λ_κ(k)=πκ/(k+κ)²，且MS族更一般化，能构造单位吸收效率（所有波数完全吸收），这是Robin边界无法实现的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Robin吸收边界条件与广义MS单方向流族是两种描述一维量子吸收过程的框架，但二者之间的关系此前缺乏系统比较。理解Robin边界在多大程度上被MS框架涵盖、以及MS框架能提供哪些超出Robin边界的新能力，对于量子检测与吸收过程的建模具有基础意义。

Method: 在单通道半线几何中，对自由标量粒子（一维空间）进行比较分析。Robin吸收由单参数κ>0刻画，其动量依赖吸收剖面固定；广义MS构建则由满足正定性和次归一化条件的谱函数λ(k)描述。作者证明每个Robin选择κ等价于特定的MS标定λ_κ(k)=πκ/(k+κ)²，并进一步给出λ_full(k)=π/(4k)实现对所有波数的单位吸收效率。最后比较了弹道固定射线区域和固定距离晚期极限下的渐近行为。

Result: 证明了每个Robin参数κ都能精确由MS谱函数λ_κ(k)=πκ/(k+κ)²复现，对所有允许的单侧谱幅度和所有时刻产生完全相同的Robin吸收电流。因此整个单参数Robin吸收族是广义MS族的子集。MS构造更一般化：λ_full(k)=π/(4k)对每个波数实现100%吸收效率，这是任何固定Robin参数都无法达到的。

Conclusion: 广义MS检测过程严格扩展了Robin吸收边界族，为量子吸收/检测问题提供了更灵活的工具；Robin边界只是MS框架中的一个子类，而MS框架通过谱函数选择可覆盖Robin无法实现的高效全波数吸收场景。

Abstract: For a free scalar particle in one spatial dimension, in a single-pass half-line geometry, we compare the absorbing Robin boundary condition with the generalized Marchewka--Schuss (MS) unidirectional-current family. Robin absorption is characterized by a single parameter $κ>0$: once $κ$ is specified, its momentum-dependent absorption profile is fixed. In the generalized MS construction, by contrast, the detector response is described by a spectral function $λ(k)$, subject to the positivity and subnormalization conditions of the detection law. We show that every Robin choice of $κ$ corresponds to the particular MS calibration $λ_κ(k)=πκ/(k+κ)^2$, which reproduces the complete Robin absorption current for every admissible one-sided spectral amplitude and at every time. Thus the entire one-parameter Robin absorption family is contained within the generalized MS family. The MS construction is more general: the choice $λ_{\rm full}(k)=π/(4k)$ gives unit absorption efficiency for every wave number, which cannot be achieved by any fixed Robin parameter. In this precise sense, the generalized MS detector process extends the Robin absorbing-boundary family. We also compare their asymptotic behavior in the ballistic fixed-ray regime and in the fixed-distance late-time limit.

</details>


### [257] [Frame phase retrievability and state distinguishability of quantum channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30006)
*Deguang Han,Kai Liu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了框架相位恢复（frame phase retrieval）在纯态识别和量子信道信息保持中的作用，通过提升映射 x↦xx* 将相位恢复问题转化为量子态上的线性泛函问题，并系统建立了相位可恢复信道与纯态注入性之间的等价关系，涵盖Choi秩二线性组合、相对谱、结构障碍和群扭缠（twirling）信道等主题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯态识别中的全局相位不可区分性是量子信息处理的核心问题之一。作者旨在建立框架相位恢复理论与量子信道理论之间的系统对应关系，从而为量子态识别、量子通信和量子纠错等应用提供统一的数学框架。

Method: 通过提升映射 x↦xx* 将框架强度转化为提升态上的线性泛函，将信道的伴随拉回视为输入系统上的算子值测量。利用Choi秩二的Kraus算子线性组合、更高秩相对谱、结构障碍分析以及规定Choi秩的构造方法建立相位可恢复性与纯态注入性的对应。对群扭缠信道，通过交换子、不可约维数、重数、轨道框架正交性、编码指标和相位可恢复子空间等群表示论工具进行分析。

Result: 系统阐明了信道相位可恢复等价于其在纯态上注入，并区分了注入性识别与全层析、完美单次判别、零错误经典通信和精确量子纠错等概念之间的差异。在扭缠信道设定中，从群表示论出发给出了可恢复性的结构性刻画。

Conclusion: 该综述为框架相位恢复与量子信道信息理论建立了一座桥梁，为纯态识别的理论研究和量子信息处理的实际应用提供了统一的分析框架，并明确了相位可恢复性与其他量子信息任务之间的层级关系。

Abstract: This survey introduces the role of frame phase retrieval in pure-state identification and information preservation by quantum channels. For unit vectors, the lift $x\mapsto xx^*$ identifies vectors that differ only by a global phase with the same rank-one quantum state, converts frame intensities into linear functionals of the lifted state, and makes the adjoint pullback of output observables an operator-valued measurement on the input system. From this viewpoint, a channel is phase retrievable exactly when it is injective on pure states. We develop this correspondence through Choi-rank-two linear combinations of Kraus operators, higher-rank relative spectra, structural obstructions, and constructions with prescribed Choi rank. We distinguish injective identification from full tomography, perfect one-shot discrimination, zero-error classical communication, and exact quantum correction. Twirling channels then provide a structured setting in which commutants, irreducible dimensions, multiplicities, orbit-frame orthogonality, coding indices, and phase-retrievable subspaces can be read from group representations.

</details>


### [258] [Newtonian Gravitational Curvature-Induced Entanglement Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30075)
*Mughees Ahmad Khan,Asad Ali,M. I Hussain,H. Kuniyil,Saif Al-Kuwari*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了一个混合量子比特-机械装置中引力曲率控制非局域量子关联产生的方案。潮汐场通过修改共享机械振子的极化率来调谐量子比特间相互作用及纠缠相位，实现了对引力曲率的非局域两量子比特相位传感。通过精确动力学处理揭示了与曲率直接相关的瞬时几何门协议。论文推导了曲率估计的量子极限并验证了宇称测量方案使其饱和。完整主方程求解表明热占据在闭合时刻被完全抵消（任意初始机械温度），而机械阻尼和量子比特退相干则以不可逆方式泄漏分支信息限制纠缠可见度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有引力介导纠缠方案中引力被直接用于生成量子关联，而本文提出一种不同的范式：引力不作为纠缠源而作为经典控制场，通过与机械振子的耦合来调控已有的量子比特-量子比特相互作用。这为引力对量子过程的控制提供了新的视角，并开辟了基于非局域纠缠相位而非局域相位测量的曲率传感途径。

Method: 采用混合量子比特-机械装置：共享机械振子介导两量子比特间的相互作用，近邻源质量的潮汐场修改机械振子的极化率从而调谐有效量子比特耦合。论文使用精确动力学处理推导瞬时（stroboscopic）几何门，将曲率视为未知参数进行量子参数估计理论分析，并求解含机械模式显式保留的完整主方程来考察退相干效应。

Result: （1）热占据在每次闭合时被精确抵消，任意初始机械温度下均恢复幺正极限，因此无需对振子进行基态冷却；（2）机械阻尼和量子比特退相干通过向环境不可逆泄漏分支信息来限制纠缠可见度和可用的询问回路数，起决定作用的是加热率和退相干率而非浴热占据本身；（3）宇称测量可饱和曲率估计的量子极限；（4）所有曲率依赖性均为解析形式，结果可精确重新标度。

Conclusion: 该方案首次展示了引力曲率对量子相互作用的控制，提供了一种基于非局域两量子比特相位而非局域相位测量的曲率传感路由。与引力介导纠缠提案的根本区别在于：纠缠由机械振子产生而引力仅作为经典控制场，这降低了协议对引力量子性质的要求并增强了实验可行性。

Abstract: We show that gravitational curvature can control the generation of nonlocal quantum correlations in a hybrid qubit-mechanical device. The tidal field of a nearby source mass modifies the susceptibility of a shared mechanical oscillator, thereby tuning an oscillator-mediated qubit-qubit interaction and the resulting entangling phase. An exact treatment of the dynamics reveals stroboscopic geometric gates whose accumulated phase is directly sensitive to gravitational curvature. Treating the curvature as an unknown parameter, we derive the ultimate quantum limit for its estimation and identify a parity-based measurement that saturates this bound. Solving the full master equation with the mechanical mode retained explicitly, we find that thermal occupation of the mediator suppresses entanglement between the closure times but is undone at each closure, exactly in the unitary limit for any initial mechanical temperature, so that ground-state cooling of the oscillator is not a prerequisite for the protocol. Mechanical damping and qubit dephasing behave differently: they leak branch information irreversibly to the environment, and it is the heating and dephasing rates, rather than the bath occupation alone, that limit the entanglement visibility and the number of usable interrogation loops. In contrast to gravity-mediated entanglement proposals, entanglement is generated by the mechanical oscillator while gravity acts solely as a classical control field. We quantify the achievable curvature parameters; all curvature dependencies are analytic, allowing for exact rescaling of the results. The scheme therefore demonstrates gravitational control of a quantum interaction and provides a route to curvature sensing based on a nonlocal two-qubit phase rather than on local phase measurements.

</details>


### [259] [Data and code for collision-model Dicke-state preparation: depth-fidelity frontiers, circuit costs, and superconducting-processor measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30176)
*Duc-Kha Vu,Minh Tam Nguyen,Şahin K. Özdemir,Özgür E. Müstecaplıoğlu,Fatih Ozaydin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个用于制备Dicke态（多体纠缠态）的开放数据集及配套代码，涵盖5至14个量子比特系统，记录无噪声制备保真度与电路深度之间的关系，并提供IQM Emerald 54比特超导处理器上的实验结果，作为量子态制备的基准测试工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Dicke态是多比特纠缠态，在量子传感、量子网络和集体量子现象中具有重要价值，但随着系统规模和激发数的增加，高保真度制备Dicke态仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 采用基于碰撞（collision-based）的量子协议来制备Dicke态，系统地生成覆盖5到14个量子比特、宽激发数范围的电路数据，记录最佳无噪声制备保真度与电路深度的关系，并在54量子比特的IQM Emerald超导处理器上对选定态进行实验测量。

Result: 提供了完整的开放数据集，包括无噪声保真度-电路深度曲线、电路资源估计及硬件实验结果，配套代码可复现所有处理数据和验证检查。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个可复用的基准测试框架，用于研究量子态制备中保真度、电路成本与硬件噪声之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: Dicke states are multipartite entangled states in which a fixed number of quantum excitations is coherently shared among many qubits. Originally introduced in the context of cooperative emission and superradiance, they are now important resources for quantum sensing, networking, and collective quantum phenomena. Preparing prescribed Dicke states with high fidelity, however, remains challenging, particularly as the system size and excitation number increase. Here we present an open dataset and accompanying code for preparing Dicke states using a collision-based quantum protocol. The dataset covers systems from five to fourteen qubits over a broad range of excitation numbers and records how the best-found noiseless preparation fidelity changes with circuit depth. It also provides circuit-resource estimates and experimental measurements for selected states on the 54-qubit IQM Emerald superconducting processor. The accompanying code reproduces the processed data and validation checks, providing a reusable benchmark for studying the trade-off between state-preparation fidelity, circuit cost, and hardware noise.

</details>


### [260] [SpiderLS: Leveraging Full ZX Reduction for Lattice Surgery Compilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30228)
*Hyungseok Kim,Changheon Lee,Seungjik Kim,Enhyeok Jang,Youngmin Kim,Seungwoo Choi,Hanbit Lee,Sungho Pyun,Won Woo Ro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: SpiderLS是一款基于ZX-diagram的格点手术编译器，通过充分利的ZX归约变换，平均降低49.2%的时空体积并将编译时间缩短99.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ZX-diagram的格点手术编译方法在ZX归约时受限于必须保留可直接嵌入为格点手术结点的图结构，无法充分利用ZX归约的变换能力来进一步降低时空成本。

Method: SpiderLS采用完整的ZX归约变换，并通过一系列编译器流水线：推导执行顺序、将兼容交互分组为多目标操作生成目标代码、将目标代码降级为Pauli乘积测量，从而将归约后的图翻译为可执行的格点手术操作。生成的显式patch和Pauli边界需求指导逻辑调度和结构感知的时空路由。

Result: 在代表性算法负载和随机负载上，与先前的ZX-based编译器相比，SpiderLS平均降低49.2%的时空体积和99.8%的编译时间。

Conclusion: SpiderLS证明了利用完整ZX归约能力可以显著提升格点手术编译的质量和效率，为容错量子程序的表面码实现提供了更优的编译方案。

Abstract: Lattice surgery compilation plays a central role in translating fault-tolerant quantum programs into efficient surface code realizations, where both spatial and temporal resources directly determine the cost of execution. Recent work has demonstrated the benefits of using ZX-diagrams as an intermediate representation for lattice surgery compilation, enabling semantics-preserving transformations that reduce spacetime cost. However, existing compilation restricts ZX reduction to preserve diagram structures that can be directly embedded as lattice surgery junctions. We present SpiderLS, which extends prior approach by leveraging full ZX reduction. To translate the resulting diagram into executable lattice surgery operations, SpiderLS applies a sequence of compiler passes that derives an execution order, generates target code by grouping compatible interactions into multi-target operations, and lowers the target code to Pauli-product measurements. The resulting explicit patch and Pauli-boundary requirements guide logical scheduling and structure-aware spacetime routing. Across representative algorithmic and random workloads, SpiderLS achieves average reductions of 49.2% in spacetime volume and 99.8% in compilation time compared with the prior ZX-based compiler.

</details>


### [261] [Modular-Annihilator Parent Hamiltonians for Purified Gibbs States: Spectral Design and Controlled Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30272)
*Changhao Yi,Jun Takahashi,Cunlu Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了纯化Gibbs态（purified Gibbs states）的精确有限平方和（SoS）父哈密顿量构造及相关的Lindbladian动力学，基于模消灭子（modular annihilators）提出了无需连续时间积分或Bohr频率分解的无挫SoS表示。对自由费米子系统给出了解析可解的父哈密顿量族（由实对称矩阵S参数化），并证明了标量函数子类在固定算子范数下选择S_opt∝1/√cosh(2βh)可实现与逆温度β无关的快速混合（混合时间上界为2log(2N/ε)）。对相互作用系统引入了Krylov-Lanczos近似方案并给出了基态误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯化Gibbs态是连接有限温度物理、耗散动力学和基态方法的桥梁，但其父哈密顿量的构造通常涉及连续时间积分或Bohr频率的显式分解，计算上较为繁琐。作者旨在提出一种更简洁、精确的有限SoS构造方案，无需上述复杂分解，并能通过优化生成元的选择来改善父哈密顿量的谱性质，同时探索其快速混合特性。

Method: 本文基于模消灭子构建无挫SoS表示。对于有限Hermitian生成元集合，对应的模消灭子产生无搓性的SoS父哈密顿量表示（无需连续时间积分或Bohr频率分解）。利用模变换在Majorana算子上作用的线性性，自由费米子情形下得到由实对称矩阵S参数化的解析父哈密顿量族。对scalar-functional子类S=f(h)，通过固定算子范数优化S的选取（S_opt∝1/√cosh(2βh)）分析混合时间。对相互作用系统，因模修饰生成元无闭式解，引入Krylov-Lanczos近似方案，并用模近似误差和父哈密顿量能隙界定基态误差。

Result: 自由费米子情形下，选择S_opt∝1/√cosh(2βh)时，混合时间上界为2log(2N/ε)，与逆温度β无关，展现出快速混合性质。数值结果验证了自由费米子情形的谱优势，并揭示了相互作用系统构造精度对温度、相互作用强度和Krylov维度的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该研究为纯化Gibbs态提供了精确、可优化谱性质的父哈密顿量SoS构造框架。自由费米子情形获得了解析可解的快速混合方案，而Krylov-Lanczos近似方案为相互作用系统提供了实用的构造途径，并给出了严格的误差上界。

Abstract: Purified Gibbs states provide a bridge between finite-temperature physics, dissipative dynamics, and ground-state methods. In this work, we study the exact finite sum-of-squares (SoS) construction of their parent Hamiltonians and the associated Lindbladian based on modular annihilators. Given a finite set of Hermitian generators, the corresponding modular annihilators yield a frustration-free SoS representation without continuous time integrals or an explicit decomposition into Bohr-frequency sectors. The purified Gibbs state remains a common zero mode while the freedom to choose and combine the generators can be used to optimize the spectral properties of the parent Hamiltonian. For free-fermion Hamiltonians, modular transformations act linearly on Majorana operators, leading to an analytically solvable family of parent Hamiltonians parameterized by a real symmetric coefficient matrix \(S\). For the scalar-functional subclass $S=f(h)$, we show that, at fixed operator norm, the choice $S_{\mathrm{opt}}\propto 1/\sqrt{\cosh(2βh)}$ has mixing time upper bound $2\log(2N/ε)$ for any $β$, which exhibits rapid mixing and is irrelevant to the inverse temperature $β$. For interacting systems, where the modularly dressed generators are not available in closed form, we introduce a Krylov--Lanczos approximation scheme and bound the resulting ground-state error in terms of the modular-approximation error and the parent-Hamiltonian gap. Numerical results illustrate the free-fermion spectral advantage and show how the accuracy of the interacting construction depends on temperature, interaction strength, and Krylov dimension.

</details>


### [262] [Efficient biphoton generation by a waveguide-coupled single atom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30276)
*Mao-Hua Wang,Xiao-Jun Zhang,M. Artoni,G. C. La Rocca,Jin-Hui Wu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用手性波导附近单个原子的自发四波混频过程，高效产生斯托克斯-反斯托克斯光子对，无需损耗抑制或严格相位匹配即可实现增强型双光子产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代片上量子光源需要低损耗、鲁棒、紧凑且可扩展的双光子源，传统方法依赖损耗抑制或严格的相位匹配条件，实际实现困难。

Method: 对四能级原子系统在现实实验条件下进行微扰处理，分析原子向波导导模和非导模衰变率的相互作用，解释双光子产生增强的物理起源及约束条件。

Result: 最优产生速率可相当于自由空间中数百微米长的冷原子团簇，同时天然满足低损耗、鲁棒性、紧凑性和可扩展性等片上量子光源的关键要求。

Conclusion: 手性波导附近单原子自发四波混频为片上双光子量子光源提供了一种高效率、无严格相位匹配要求的新方案，具备良好的实用性与可扩展潜力。

Abstract: A single atom undergoing spontaneous four-wave mixing near a chiral waveguide can efficiently channel an emitted Stokes-anti-Stokes photon pair into two tightly confined waveguide modes, yielding thus enhanced biphoton generation without requiring loss suppression or stringent phase matching. We develop a perturbative treatment, valid for a four-level atomic system under experimentally realistic conditions, to explain physical origins and clarify relevant constraints of such an enhancement determined by the interplay of atomic decay rates toward guided and unguided modes. Besides achieving optimal generation rates equivalent to a cold atomic ensemble hundreds of micrometers long in free space, our biphoton source naturally fulfills key requirements for next-generation on-chip quantum light sources, namely low-loss operation, robustness, compactness, and scalability.

</details>


### [263] [Best Annealing Path of Quantum Annealing via Efficient Adiabatic Phase Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30282)
*Kiyotaka Murashima*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了嵌套模拟退火（NSA）算法与量子退火（QA）中非随机哈密顿量（如XX相互作用）之间的关系，通过将自旋视为连续变量，推导了包含问题哈密顿量和横向电磁场微扰哈密顿量的总能量计算公式，并揭示了局部极大值与收敛速度之间的清晰关系，从而为在经典计算机上模拟量子蒙特卡洛（QMC）提供了有效的参数选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子退火在解决交通拥堵、配送优化等社会问题时已有实际应用，但当基态与第一激发态能量差很小时，跃迁概率增大，需要在极低温度下运行，导致计算时间过长。为加速量子退火，学术界正在探索多种理论方法，包括引入非随机哈密顿量。本文旨在从理论层面探讨此前提出的NSA算法与非随机哈密顿量（如XX相互作用）之间的联系。

Method: 作者将自旋视为连续变量，推导了同时包含问题哈密顿量与横向电磁场微扰哈密顿量的总能量计算公式。通过这一公式化框架，作者分析了在二元自旋计算中局部极大值与收敛速度之间的对应关系，并提出通过合理选择NSA参数来诱导最优绝热相变。具体而言，选取局部极大值最小的退火路径可以在消耗较少计算资源的情况下实现快速收敛。

Result: 研究结果表明，局部极大值最小的退火路径收敛速度最快，同时消耗更少的计算资源。这证明了NSA参数与量子退火中的非随机哈密顿量效应之间存在清晰的对应关系，且通过适当选择NSA参数可以诱导最优的绝热相变。

Conclusion: 本文为在经典计算机上模拟量子蒙特卡洛提供了一种有效的NSA参数选择方法，深化了经典模拟退火类算法与量子非随机哈密顿量之间的理论联系，为加速量子退火计算提供了新的理论支撑和实践指导。

Abstract: Quantum Annealing (QA) has already put into practical use and considered useful for solving many social issues, such as reduction of traffic congestion and delivery optimization. But in QA, when the energy difference between the ground state and the first excited state is small, the transition probability between them increases. Therefore, in order to decrease the transition, QA has to be performed at extremely low temperatures. To simulate the situation, it has the problem in that it takes much time. Many studies are underway to accelerate QA theoretically, one of which is to incorporate non-stoquastic Hamiltonian. On the other hand, I proposed Nested Simulated Annealing (NSA) inspired by Quantum Monte Carlo (QMC). I showed the computational speedup could be achieved dramatically, by considering the idea that the effect of flipping a spin preferentially influenced the spins directly interacting with it. Although NSA was based on such classical concept of causality, the hybrid computation both quantum and classical approach worked well. In this paper, in order to discuss the relationship between NSA and non-stoquastic Hamiltonian like XX-interaction, the spins are treated as continuous variables. I derive the formula to calculate the total energy in both with a problem Hamiltonian and the perturbation Hamiltonian induced by a transverse electromagnetic field. And I will show a clear relationship between local-maxima and the convergence speed when calculating it with the binary spin. More precisely, the annealing path with the smallest local-maxima converges fast, even though it consumes fewer computational resources. Therefore, it is possible to induce an optimal adiabatic phase transition by selecting NSA parameters appropriately. This paper shows an effective method to choose the parameters when simulating QMC on a classical computer.

</details>


### [264] [Quantum-interference metrology of dissipative Kerr solitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30313)
*Yun-Ru Fan,Yong Geng,Ji Liu,Yong-Jun Huang,Hai-Zhi Song,Hao Li,Li-Xing You,Heng Zhou,Kun Qiu,Kai Guo,Guang-Can Guo,Qiang Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于Hong-Ou-Mandel量子干涉的微腔孤子超快计时方法，通过将孤子流衰减至单光子水平并测量四阶干涉，无需光放大和色散补偿即可直接获取近变换极限的脉冲持续时间，并能在25公里标准光纤传播后保持准确性，同时可将多孤子态的时间间隔转换为额外的干涉谷加以表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耗散Kerr孤子微腔光频梳在通信和光谱学中有重要应用，但其飞秒级时间结构的表征面临挑战：传统超快诊断需要光放大和色散补偿，二者都会显著扭曲波形。

Method: 将孤子流衰减至单光子水平，利用Hong-Ou-Mandel干涉测量第四阶干涉，从干涉图直接反演近变换极限的脉冲持续时间；无需放大或色散管理。多孤子态通过孤子间间隔转化为对应延迟处的额外干涉谷进行表征。

Result: 直接获取近变换极限脉冲持续时间，在25公里标准光纤传播后仍保持准确；多孤子态的腔内时间间隔可实现亚皮秒级表征。

Conclusion: 该量子启发的计量范式对放大和色散畸变具有免疫性，为复杂孤子物理的表征提供了全新的计量方法和有力工具。

Abstract: Dissipative Kerr solitons in optical microresonators underpin chip-scale frequency combs with applications ranging from coherent telecommunications to precision spectroscopy. Yet the characterization of their intrinsic femtosecond temporal structure remains challenging, as the low pulse energy and broad spectral bandwidth necessitate optical amplification and careful dispersion compensation in conventional ultrafast diagnostics, both of which can significantly distort the waveform. Here we demonstrate a quantum-interference metrology of microcomb solitons based on Hong-Ou-Mandel interference. By attenuating the soliton stream to the single-photon level and measuring fourth-order interference, we directly retrieve near transform-limited pulse durations without amplification or dispersion management, remaining accurate even after propagation through 25 km of standard fiber. The same interferogram also provides direct access to the temporal separations in multi-soliton states by converting inter-soliton separations into additional interference dips at corresponding delays, enabling sub-picosecond characterization of their intracavity temporal structure. This quantum-inspired paradigm introduces a fundamentally new metrological approach that is immune to amplification and dispersion distortions, offering a powerful tool for the characterization of complex soliton physics.

</details>


### [265] [Exact quantum splitting and the structure of finite algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30340)
*Muhammad Imran*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了Berlekamp算法因式分解的无条件精确量子实现。通过无条件计数论证，对包含s≥2个不可约因子的块，奇数特征下使用二次特征检验、特征2下使用绝对迹检验，以已知精确概率p_{q,s}≥1/2获得非恒定测试元素，再通过精确振幅放大实现确定性成功。整个算法使用恰好r-1轮量子分裂和O(n³log q)量子F_q操作，无需本原根、二次非剩余或不同次预处理，并能推广到任意可分交换F_q代数的分裂以及组合Rónyai经典结构理论实现Wedderburn分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Berlekamp算法通过确定性线性代数将多项式因式分解归约为分裂显式交换代数，但标准高效分裂步骤在大型奇q下是随机化的，现有去随机化方案依赖于扩展黎曼猜想（ERH）。作者的目标是提供无条件的确定性强因式分解方法，消除对ERH的依赖，并进一步将方法推广到一般结合代数的Radical计算和Wedderburn分解。

Method: 核心方法包括：(1) 构造无条件计数论证，证明在包含s≥2个不可约因子的块中，奇数特征下用二次特征测试、特征2下用绝对迹测试，能以精确已知概率p_{q,s}≥1/2获得非恒定测试元素，该概率仅依赖于q和s而不依赖未知因式分解；(2) 利用精确振幅放大(amplitude amplification)将随机测试转化为单次迭代后必定成功的确定性过程；(3) 使用恰好r-1轮量子分裂；(4) 结合Rónyai的经典结构理论，计算根并确定性归约到多项式因式分解，从而获得Wedderburn分解。

Result: 算法使用O(n³log q)量子F_q操作和O(n³)经典操作，无需本原根、二次非剩余或不同度预处理，可处理任意可分交换F_q代数的分裂；结合Rónyai理论后，对任意由结构常数给出的n维结合F_q代数，以O(n⁴log q)量子F_q操作确定性地计算Radical和A/Rad(A)的Wedderburn分解（最小双边理想的分解）。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一种无条件的、精确的量子算法，替代了Berlekamp算法中依赖ERH的去随机化步骤，方法完全确定、无需预处理，并自然推广到一般代数结构（Radical和Wedderburn分解）的计算，在量子计算复杂度上实现了对经典随机算法的高效确定性替代。

Abstract: Berlekamp's algorithm factors a squarefree polynomial $f\in\mathbb{F}_q[x]$ by deterministic linear algebra, reducing the problem to splitting an explicit commutative algebra $B\cong\mathbb{F}_q^r$ into its $r$ simple factors. For large odd $q$, the standard efficient splitting step is randomized, while known derandomizations are conditional on the Extended Riemann Hypothesis. We give an unconditional exact quantum implementation in a circuit model permitting single-qubit rotations through efficiently computable angles. The construction uses an unconditional counting argument. For a block containing $s\ge2$ irreducible factors, a quadratic-character test in odd characteristic and an absolute-trace test in characteristic $2$ yield a nonconstant test element with probability $p_{q,s}\ge\tfrac12$, known exactly in advance and depending only on $q$ and $s$, not on the unknown factorization. Exact amplitude amplification therefore converts each randomized test into a procedure succeeding with certainty after one amplification iteration. The resulting algorithm uses exactly $r-1$ quantum splitting rounds and $O(n^3\log q)$ quantum $\mathbb{F}_q$-operations and $O(n^3)$ classical operations, requiring no primitive root, quadratic non-residue, or distinct-degree preprocessing. The method also splits arbitrary finite-dimensional separable commutative $\\mathbb{F}_q$-algebras given by structure constants. Combined with R'onyai's classical structure theory, which computes the radical deterministically and reduces the remaining tasks deterministically to polynomial factorization, it yields the radical and the Wedderburn decomposition of $A/\mathrm{Rad}(A)$ into minimal two-sided ideals, with certainty, for any $n$-dimensional associative $\mathbb{F}_q$-algebra given by structure constants, using $O(n^4\log q)$ quantum $\mathbb{F}_q$-operations.

</details>


### [266] [Quantum Imaginary Time Evolution on an Infinite 1D Chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30363)
*Hao-Ti Hung,Tung Tsao,Ying-Jer Kao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在无限一维晶格系统上执行虚时演化的量子电路算法，利用参数化量子电路表示均匀矩阵乘积态，结合时间相关变分原理推导ITE算法，并采用量子Lanczos算法改善基态能量估计。通过经典模拟器和IBM量子设备对横场Ising模型进行基准测试，统计分析了有限采样噪声对收敛的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚时演化是求解量子多体系统基态的重要方法，但在无限大晶格系统上执行虚时演化的量子电路实现仍是一个挑战。需要开发能在真实量子设备上高效运行的算法，并理解量子测量噪声对收敛行为的影响。

Method: 使用参数化量子电路表示均匀矩阵乘积态（uniform MPS）作为变分拟设；基于时间相关变分原理（TDVP）推导虚时演化（ITE）算法；引入量子Lanczos算法以改进基态能量估计；在经典模拟器和IBM量子设备上对横场Ising模型进行数值实验。

Result: 在横场Ising模型上成功验证了算法在经典模拟器和IBM量子设备上的可行性；通过对代价函数和能量密度分布的统计分析，揭示了有限采样噪声对收敛特性的影响规律。

Conclusion: 该算法为在无限一维量子格点系统上执行虚时演化提供了一种可行的量子电路实现方案，同时系统分析了量子测量噪声对算法收敛行为的影响，为实际量子设备上的应用提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We introduce a quantum-circuit algorithm for performing imaginary-time evolution on infinite one-dimensional lattice systems. The method uses a parameterized quantum circuit to represent a uniform matrix product state ansatz. We derive the ITE algorithm using the time-dependent variational principle and employ the quantum Lanczos algorithm to improve the ground-state energy estimate. As a benchmark, we simulate the transverse-field Ising model using both classical simulators and IBM Quantum devices. Our analysis includes a statistical study of the distributions of the cost function and energy density obtained from quantum measurements, illustrating the effects of finite-sampling noise on convergence.

</details>


### [267] [Cross-Spectral Reservoir Correlations as a Resource for Finite-Time Quantum Otto Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30496)
*Siddhartha Dutta,Sujay Mondal,Ankush Das,Anumita Mukhopadhyay,Abhijit Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在有限时间量子奥托热机中，纵-横交叉谱储层相关性对热力学性能的影响。通过二阶时间卷积展开框架推导了有限时间等容动力学，发现交叉谱相关性可以增强输出功率，其增强程度由交叉谱相位和特征频率尺度控制，同时保持极限环效率不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子热机研究通常只考虑储层的自谱密度（纵向-纵向或横向-横向），忽略了不同通道间（激发-弛豫通道与退相干通道）的交叉谱关联。本文旨在探究交叉谱相关性这一经常被忽视的资源能否作为控制有限时间量子热机性能的新自由度。

Method: 以两能级工作介质构建有限时间量子奥托热机，储层通过激发-弛豫和退相干通道耦合，用厄米正半定谱密度矩阵（含代表交叉相关的非对角线元素）刻画其涨落。在二阶时间卷积（TCL）框架内推导有限时间等容动力学，不预先施加马尔可夫极限，从而允许有限储层记忆效应通过时间相关的耗散项和储层诱导相干项进入。随后将动力学改写为Bloch矢量形式以构造冲程解析的循环动力学。

Result: 在固定的自谱密度下，交叉谱相关性会改变工作介质的布居数和相干性，从而影响热力学性能。增加相关性强度可以增强输出功率，增强程度由交叉谱相位和特征频率尺度控制。交叉谱相关性还会重塑瞬态循环间演化及趋向周期性运行的路径，但极限环效率仍保持为奥托值（因所采用酉冲程保持布居数不变）。

Conclusion: 这些结果确立了非对角储层谱（即不同耗散通道间的交叉谱相关性）作为控制有限时间量子热机的额外资源。

Abstract: We investigate the thermodynamic consequences of longitudinal-transverse cross-spectral reservoir correlations in a finite-time quantum Otto engine with a two-level working medium. Each reservoir couples through excitation-relaxation and dephasing channels whose fluctuations are characterized by a Hermitian positive-semidefinite spectral-density matrix, with the off-diagonal elements encoding their cross correlations. The finite-time isochoric dynamics is derived within the second-order time-convolutionless framework, without imposing the Markov limit at the outset, so that finite reservoir-memory effects can enter through time-dependent dissipative and reservoir-induced coherent contributions. The resulting dynamics is then recast in Bloch-vector form to construct the stroke-resolved cycle dynamics. At fixed auto-spectral densities, cross-spectral correlations modify the populations and coherences of the working medium and thereby its thermodynamic performance. Increasing the correlation strength can enhance the output power, with the enhancement controlled by the cross-spectral phase and characteristic frequency scale. The correlations also reshape the transient cycle-to-cycle evolution and the approach to periodic operation, while the limit-cycle efficiency remains fixed at the Otto value for the population-preserving unitary strokes considered here. These results establish off-diagonal reservoir spectra as an additional resource for controlling finite-time quantum thermal machines.

</details>


### [268] [Efficient measurement schemes for the Monte Carlo projective quantum eigensolver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30612)
*Divye Baid,Maria-Andreea Filip*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了蒙特卡洛投影量子本征求解器（MC-PQE）中期望值的测量优化问题，将VQE中多种联合测量技术扩展到MC-PQE所需的非对称期望值上，并通过最多12量子比特的分子系统评估了不同技术的性能。结果表明，完全对易泡利项分组结合定制的测量分配策略可实现5-10倍的测量成本降低，且传统哈密顿量分组测量优于可处理的经典阴影层析方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MC-PQE作为一种替代VQE的混合量子算法，虽已通过量子蒙特卡洛思路降低了测量成本，但其所需的非对称期望值测量开销仍可进一步优化。鉴于VQE领域已发展出多种通过联合测量多个可观测量来降低测量开销的技术，作者希望将这些成熟技术适配并扩展到MC-PQE框架中。

Method: 将VQE中的联合测量技术（如完全对易泡利项分组、哈密顿量分组测量、经典阴影层析等）扩展到MC-PQE所需的非对称期望值计算中，并针对不同测量分配策略进行定制优化。实验评估在多达12量子比特的多种分子系统上进行。

Result: 完全对易泡利项分组结合定制的测量分配技术，在相同总量子测量次数下将标准误差降低了5-10倍；对于所考虑的分子体系，传统哈密顿量分组测量性能优于可处理的经典阴影层析技术。

Conclusion: VQE中成熟的联合测量技术可以有效迁移并适配到MC-PQE的非对称期望值计算中，实现显著的测量开销降低，为该算法在实际量子计算中的应用提供了实用优化方案。

Abstract: The Monte Carlo Projective Quantum Eigensolver (MC-PQE) was recently introduced as an alternative to hybrid algorithms like the variational quantum eigensolver (VQE). By using a quantum Monte Carlo-inspired scheme for energy estimation, MC-PQE was found to decrease the required measurement cost relative to comparable conventional approaches. However, in the context of VQE, numerous techniques have been developed to reduce the measurement overhead by joint measurement of multiple observable. In this work, we extend these approaches to the asymmetric expectation values required in MC-PQE and assess the performance of different techniques for various molecular systems of up to 12 qubits. We find full commuting Pauli term grouping combined with tailored measurement allocation techniques leads to a 5-10$\times$ reduction in standard error for the same total number of quantum measurements. Conventional Hamiltonian grouped measurement outperforms tractable classical shadows tomography based techniques for the considered systems.

</details>


### [269] [Gate-Efficient Implementation of the Query-Optimal Time-Dependent Hamiltonian Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30629)
*Boyang Chen,Minbo Gao,Zhengfeng Ji,Tongyang Li,Xinzhao Wang,Shuo Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了[CGWZ26]中时间依赖哈密顿量模拟最优查询算法的电路实现方案，在保持最优查询复杂度的同时，显著降低了门复杂度至O[q(a + log(1 + T(α+βT)/ε))]，核心创新是引入有序更新乘积的精确二进分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: [CGWZ26]算法虽然具有最优的查询复杂度，但其直接电路实现会导致远大于查询复杂度的门开销，这使得该算法在实践中难以高效部署。需要设计一种电路实现方案，在不牺牲查询最优性的前提下降低门复杂度。

Method: 通过对底层单查询换能器中的有序更新乘积进行精确的二进分解（dyadic factorization），实现了一个保留原算法最优查询复杂度的电路实现，门复杂度为O[q(a + log(1 + T(α+βT)/ε))]，其中a为块编码辅助量子比特数，β为哈密顿量的Lipschitz常数。

Result: 给出了与原算法相同查询复杂度（O(αT + log(1/ε)/log(e + log(1/ε)/(αT)))）的电路实现，门复杂度为O[q(a + log(1 + T(α+βT)/ε))]的单比特和双比特门。

Conclusion: 这项工作弥补了[CGWZ26]算法从理论查询最优到实际电路可实现的差距，为时间依赖哈密顿量模拟提供了查询与门复杂度同时接近最优的实现方案。

Abstract: The query-optimal algorithm of [CGWZ26] for general time-dependent Hamiltonian simulation uses $$
  q = O\left( αT +
  \frac{\log(1/\varepsilon)}{\log\left(e + \log(1/\varepsilon)/(αT) \right)}
  \right) $$ queries to $\mathrm{HAM\mbox{-}T}$ within $\varepsilon$ error for a Lipschitz-continuous time-dependent Hamiltonian $H(t)$ on $[0,T]$ satisfying $\left\lVert H(t)\right\rVert\leqα$. However, its direct circuit implementation incurs a substantially larger gate overhead. In this note, we give an implementation of the same algorithm that retains its optimal query complexity and uses $$
  O\left[ q \left( a + \log\left(1 + \frac{T(α+ βT)}{\varepsilon}
  \right) \right) \right] $$ one- and two-qubit gates, where $a$ is the number of block-encoding ancilla qubits and $β$ is the Lipschitz constant of $H$. The main ingredient is an exact dyadic factorization of the ordered update product in the underlying one-query transducer.

</details>


### [270] [Deterministic Universal Logical Gates for Finite-Energy GKP Qubits in Trapped Neutral Atoms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30631)
*Alok Kumar,Aaron N. Raja,Diksha Thapliyal,Ishitwa Kumar Das,Ajay Wasan1*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套用于囚禁中性原子谐振运动态中编码的有限能量Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill（GKP）量子比特的通用逻辑门集合。利用内部电子态作为辅助实现态依赖的相空间条件位移，从而实现任意单比特相位门；通过瞬态里德伯激发介导的原子偶极-偶极相互作用实现受控-Z门。所有门在魔幻囚禁条件下运行，无需中间测量或前馈。基于88Sr原子的可行性评估显示，在压缩参数Δ=0.25（12 dB）时，单比特门和受控-Z门平均保真度分别达0.986和0.985，Δ降至0.1时可提升至0.997和0.995。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GKP量子比特是实现容错量子计算的有力候选，但此前在囚禁中性原子平台上实现通用、无需测量的GKP逻辑门控制仍面临挑战。现有方案往往依赖中间测量和前馈，增加了实验复杂度和误差来源。作者旨在设计一套无需测量和前馈、可在持续囚禁条件下运行的通用GKP逻辑门集合，为中性原子架构提供可行的通用逻辑控制路径。

Method: 采用内部电子态作为辅助量子比特，通过态依赖的相空间条件位移实现任意单比特相位门；利用瞬态里德伯激发介导的原子间偶极-偶极相互作用实现受控-Z门，避免使用阻塞（blockade）机制。所有门操作在魔幻囚禁条件下进行，确保整个门序列期间持续囚禁，从而保持运动编码。以88Sr中性原子为平台进行数值模拟，评估不同压缩参数下的门保真度。

Result: 在压缩参数Δ=0.25（即12 dB压缩）下，单比特相位门平均保真度为0.986，受控-Z门为0.985；当Δ降至0.1时，两者分别提升至0.997和0.995。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了朝向中性原子架构中运动GKP量子比特的通用、免测量逻辑控制的具体路线，具有显著的实验可实现性，为中性原子平台上的容错量子计算奠定了基础。

Abstract: We present a universal logical gate set for finite-energy Gottesman-Kitaev-Preskill (GKP) qubits encoded in the harmonic motional states of trapped neutral atoms. Internal electronic states serve as ancilla for implementing state-dependent conditional displacements in phase space, enabling arbitrary single-qubit phase gates. A Transient Rydberg excitation-mediated atomic dipole-dipole interaction enables a controlled-Z gate without invoking the blockade mechanism. The gates operate under magic-trapping conditions, allowing continuous trapping throughout the gate sequence, thereby preserving the motional encoding without intermediate measurement or feedforward. We assess the experimental feasibility of the proposed protocols using neutral $^{88}\mathrm{Sr}$ atoms and obtain average gate fidelities of $0.986$ for single-qubit phase gates and $0.985$ for the controlled-Z gate at a finite squeezing parameter of $Δ=0.25$ (12 dB of squeezing), increasing to $0.997$ and $0.995$ respectively as $Δ$ reaches $0.1$. These results provide a route toward universal, measurement-free logical control of motional GKP qubits in neutral-atom architectures.

</details>


### [271] [Trade-off between Cooling-Step Count and Geometric Implementation Cost in Non-Markovian Algorithmic Cooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30660)
*Yohei Azumai,Yoshihiko Hasegawa*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子冷却中储库记忆对冷却性能与几何实现成本之间权衡的影响，通过赝模映射将非马尔可夫阻尼Jaynes-Cummings动力学表示为重复碰撞电路，并通过矩阵模拟和ibm_kawasaki处理器实验发现：抑制储库记忆可减少冷却步数，但通常会增加几何协议成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子冷却是可靠量子计算的关键，但冷却性能与实现资源之间存在权衡。尽管储库记忆可以改善冷却性能的特定方面，但其相关资源成本（尤其是电路实现方面）尚未被充分理解。作者旨在阐明储库记忆如何影响冷却性能（冷却步数）与几何实现成本之间的权衡关系。

Method: 采用赝模映射（pseudomode mapping）将非马尔可夫阻尼Jaynes-Cummings动力学表示为重复碰撞电路（repeated collision circuit），从而计算其几何实现成本（geometric implementation cost）。使用基于矩阵的模拟以及ibm_kawasaki Heron r2处理器的实际实现进行验证，分析单量子比特热浴算法冷却中储库记忆对冷却步数与几何协议成本之间权衡的影响。

Result: 识别出一个明确的权衡关系：抑制储库记忆能够减少所需的冷却步数，但通常会增大几何协议成本。该结果在矩阵模拟和ibm_kawasaki Heron r2处理器实验中均得到验证。

Conclusion: 该工作为算法冷却中的储库工程提供了基于资源（resource-based）的视角，揭示了储库记忆在冷却效率和几何实现成本之间扮演的关键角色，有助于指导实际量子计算中冷却协议的资源优化设计。

Abstract: Quantum cooling is important for reliable quantum computation but involves a trade-off between cooling performance and implementation resources. Although reservoir memory can improve particular aspects of cooling performance, the associated resource cost, particularly for circuit implementation, remains insufficiently understood. Here, we investigate how reservoir memory affects the trade-off between cooling performance quantified by the cooling-step count and geometric implementation cost in single-qubit heat-bath algorithmic cooling. Using a pseudomode mapping, we represent the non-Markovian damped Jaynes--Cummings dynamics by a repeated collision circuit and evaluate its geometric implementation cost. Using matrix-based simulations and an implementation on the ibm_kawasaki Heron r2 processor, we identify a trade-off: suppressing reservoir memory reduces the cooling-step count but generally increases the geometric protocol cost. Our work provides a resource-based perspective on reservoir engineering for algorithmic cooling.

</details>


### [272] [Entanglement-enabled Criticality in One-dimensional Quantum Contact Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30681)
*Ya-Xin Xiang,Tianyi Yan,Weibin Li,Yu-Qiang Ma*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过结合Liouvillian谱分析、张量跳跃方法、精确量子跳跃蒙特卡洛和截断Wigner模拟，证明一维量子接触过程（QCP）经历连续吸收态相变，其临界指数与经典情况不同，并指出Liouvillian能隙闭合早于临界点、弱纠缠对正确临界行为至关重要而半经典方法会产生虚假的一阶相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维量子接触过程的相变性质长期存在争议，经典接触过程是理解非平衡动力学的范例，但其量子对应物的过渡性质尚不明确，需要严格的方法来确定其普适类与相变机制。

Method: 结合四种互补方法：Liouvillian谱分析、张量跳跃方法（tensor jump method）、精确量子跳跃蒙特卡洛（quantum jump Monte Carlo）和截断Wigner模拟，对一维量子接触过程进行系统性研究。

Result: 发现一维QCP经历连续吸收态相变，且临界指数与经典情况不同；Liouvillian能隙在远低于临界点处就已闭合，说明谱隙分析无法区分相变与亚稳定性；一维QCP弱纠缠，但弱纠缠对捕捉正确临界行为不可或缺，半经典方法会人为稳定活性态并预测虚假的一阶相变。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了一维QCP中相变的量子起源，并强调纠缠在耗散量子多体系统中的关键作用，为理解量子非平衡相变提供了重要见解。

Abstract: The contact process is a paradigmatic example of nonequilibrium dynamics, with broad applications ranging from chemistry to sociology. Its quantum counterpart, the quantum contact process (QCP), extends the classical model to include coherent processes. Despite sustained interest, the nature of the transition in the one-dimensional (1D) QCP remains debatable. Here, combining Liouvillian spectral analysis, the tensor jump method, exact quantum jump Monte Carlo, and truncated Wigner simulations, we show that 1D QCP undergoes a continuous absorbing-state phase transition, with critical exponents distinct from the classical case. We further find Liouvillian gap closes well below the critical point, highlighting that spectral gap analysis alone cannot distinguish a phase transition from metastability in the QCP. Crucially, the 1D QCP is weakly entangled, yet even this weak entanglement is indispensable for capturing the correct critical behavior, whereas semiclassical methods artificially stabilize the active state and predict a spurious first-order transition. Our work establishes the quantum origin of the phase transition in the 1D QCP and underscores the essential role of entanglement in dissipative quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [273] [Flatness-Preserving Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30697)
*Otto Veltheim,Esko Keski-Vakkuri*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子态的资源理论中平坦态（flat states）保持操作的刻画问题。作者首先证明所有正交保持操作（OPOs）都是由幺正/等距嵌入加上附加固定态组成，进而证明所有平坦保持操作（FPOs）要么是映射到固定平坦态的常数映射，要么是附加固定态为平坦态的特殊OPO。此外还刻画了平坦态的平坦凸组合结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦态（与投影子成正比的量子态）在量子信息中具有重要地位。近期学界对反平坦性（antiflatness）及其资源理论产生了兴趣，但该资源理论中的自由操作——平坦保持操作（FPOs）的刻画一直是一个未解决问题。明确FPOs的结构是建立完整资源理论的基础。

Method: 作者首先研究更一般的正交保持操作（OPOs），给出了一个简洁的证明，表明所有OPOs都是等距嵌入，由幺正操作/等距映射和附加固定态的组合构成。基于此结果，进一步刻画了FPOs：证明FPOs要么是常量映射到某个固定平坦态，要么是附加固定态为平坦态的一类特殊OPO。最后，作者还分析了平坦态的平坦凸组合的充要条件，即仅当各平坦态相互正交且权重由各自纯度（purity）决定时成立。

Result: 得到了两个核心结果：（1）所有正交保持操作（OPOs）均可被完整刻画为幺正/等距操作与附加固定态的组合；（2）所有平坦保持操作（FPOs）的完整分类：常数映射或附加平坦固定态的特殊OPO。此外，证明了平坦态的平坦凸组合存在的充要条件（正交性加纯度权重条件）。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了反平坦性资源理论中自由操作刻画这一空白，通过OPO作为中间工具给出了FPOs的完整结构定理，为后续反平坦性资源理论的研究提供了坚实的数学基础。

Abstract: A quantum state is called flat if it is proportional to a projector. There has been recent interest in studying antiflatness, the property of diverging from flat states, and establishing a resource theory for it. Identifying the free operations (the Flatness-Preserving Operations (FPOs)) remained an open problem. For this purpose, we first discuss Orthogonality-Preserving Operations (OPOs), of which trivial examples are unitary operations in an isolated system. More generally, we give a simple proof that all OPOs are isometric embeddings consisting of combinations of unitaries/isometries and appending a fixed state. We then show that all FPOs are either constant maps to some fixed flat state or a special case of an OPO, where the appended fixed state must be a flat state. We also show that the only possible flat convex combinations of flat states are those of orthogonal flat states with weights given by their purities.

</details>


### [274] [PauLie: Fast Classification of Pauli Dynamical Lie Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30771)
*Oxana Shaya,Konstantin Golovkin,Mainak Roy,Vincent Russo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍PauLie，一个开源框架，用于快速判定由任意Pauli字符串生成的动力学李代数（DLA）的同构类型，时间复杂度为O(n|G|·max(n,|G|))，将DLA分类从指数级计算瓶颈降为常规预处理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动力学李代数（DLA）决定量子系统的可控性、可表达性和模拟复杂度，但暴力计算李闭包随量子比特数n指数增长。许多应用只需要知道DLA的同构类型，而非显式结构，因此需要一个高效的分类工具。

Method: 基于Aguilar等人提出的反对易图（anticommutation-graph）约简方法，构建PauLie框架。该框架将DLA的同构类型判定转化为图上的问题，从而将计算复杂度从指数级降低到O(n|G|·max(n,|G|))，其中|G|是生成元的数量。

Result: PauLie实现了多项式时间内的DLA同构类型判定，并展示了其作为结构预言机的多种用途：路由李代数模拟和Cartan分解、诊断变分量子算法中的贫瘠高原（barren plateaus）、以及设计具有最优生成速率的通用Pauli字符串生成元集合。

Conclusion: PauLie将DLA分类从指数级瓶颈转变为一个例行的预处理步骤，为量子系统分析提供了高效的结构性工具，在量子模拟、变分量子算法设计和通用生成元工程等方面具有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: The dynamical Lie algebra (DLA) governs the controllability, expressibility, and simulation complexity of a quantum system. Explicitly computing it has been a major computational bottleneck: brute-force Lie closure scales exponentially in the number of qubits $n$. Many applications, however, consult only the isomorphism type of the DLA. We introduce PauLie, an open-source framework that decides this isomorphism type for DLAs generated by arbitrary Pauli strings, building on the anticommutation-graph reduction of Aguilar et al. PauLie runs in $O(n|\mathcal{G}|\max(n,|\mathcal{G}|))$ time, where $|\mathcal{G}|$ is the number of generators, turning DLA classification into a routine preprocessing step. We demonstrate its use as a structural oracle for routing Lie-algebraic simulation and Cartan decomposition, diagnosing barren plateaus in variational quantum algorithms, and engineering universal Pauli string generator sets with optimal generation rate.

</details>


### [275] [Quantum Block Turbo Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30775)
*Khaled Jebari,Luiz Anet Neto,Ramesh Pyndiah,Jean-Louis de Bougrenet de la Tocnaye*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了量子分组Turbo码（quantum block turbo codes）理论，这是经典Turbo乘积码的量子对应物。作者描述了其迭代译码算法，并在去极化信道上针对不同分量码模拟了性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Turbo码在20世纪90年代初革新了误差校正领域，广泛应用于两类码：卷积Turbo码和Turbo乘积码。前者已被适配到量子领域，发展出量子串行Turbo码理论；但后者（Turbo乘积码）的量子对应物尚未被系统研究，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 本文提出了量子分组Turbo码的理论框架，作为经典Turbo乘积码的量子模拟。作者设计了该码的迭代译码算法，并在去极化信道上对采用不同分量码的量子分组Turbo码进行了性能仿真。

Result: 给出了量子分组Turbo码的完整理论描述及迭代译码流程，并通过在不同分量码配置下的去极化信道仿真，展示了该码类的性能表现。

Conclusion: 量子分组Turbo码是量子纠错领域的一类新码型，与已有的量子串行Turbo码互补，填补了经典两类Turbo码在量子领域对应的缺失部分。

Abstract: In the early nineties, the introduction of turbo codes revolutionized classical error correction. The idea was mainly applied on two types of codes: convolutional turbo codes and turbo product codes. The first type of codes was adapted to quantum error correction which initiated the theory of quantum serial turbo codes. In this paper, we present a theory for quantum block turbo codes, the quantum analog of the second type of turbo codes. We describe their iterative decoding algorithm and simulate their performances on a depolarizing channel for different constituent codes.

</details>


### [276] [Neutral atom quantum computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30783)
*M. Saffman*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了中性原子量子比特在实现大规模量子计算机方面的进展，涵盖其物理原理、当前能力及未来展望。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子量子比特是实现大规模量子计算机的主要途径之一，但该领域发展迅速，需要对物理基础、当前性能水平及未来发展方向进行系统梳理和综述。

Method: 这是一篇综述性论文，回顾了中性原子量子比特的物理原理（约25年前提出），追踪了其发展历程（约10年前首次演示通用门集），并综述了近年来的快速进展，包括量子算法演示和用于容错纠错的逻辑量子比特。

Result: 总结了中性原子量子比特阵列在性能和规模上近年来的快速发展，已成功演示量子算法以及面向容错纠错的逻辑量子比特。

Conclusion: 中性原子量子比特方案已取得显著进展，是未来大规模量子计算的有力候选者，并为后续研究提供了前景展望。

Abstract: Neutral atom qubits are one of the leading approaches for implementation of a large scale quantum computer. The original proposals for neutral atom qubits were formulated more than 25 years ago, with first demonstrations of a universal gate set following 10 years later. In the last few years the performance and scale of neutral atom qubit arrays has developed at a rapid pace leading to demonstrations of quantum algorithms, and logical qubits for fault tolerant error correction. This contribution reviews the physics of the neutral atom approach, surveys current capabilities, and provides an outlook for future progress.

</details>


### [277] [Photon-efficient quantum repeater chains via hyperentanglement-assisted purification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30786)
*Vinay Kumar,Krishan Joshi,Claudio Cicconetti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种名为DAHR（自由度辅助超纠缠中继器）的线性量子中继器链协议，将DAEPP（自由度辅助纠缠纯化协议）与BDCZ纠缠交换方案相结合，在每段链路上进行DAEPP纯化。作者推导了端到端保真度的闭式递归表达式，并证明了在代表性非对称工作点下，要匹配DAHR单光子对的性能，传统BBPSSW纯化需要2-3轮；且在关键操作可靠性阈值以下，无论多少轮BBPSSW都无法匹配DAHR单轮DAEPP的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统线性量子中继器链（基于Werner态BBPSSW纯化与BDCZ纠缠交换）存在严苛的操作可靠性阈值，且递归纯化消耗资源巨大（每轮需消耗两个纠缠对产出一个）。超纠缠利用光子对的多自由度（如极化、空间模式）编码多个独立纠缠态，为提升纯化效率提供了新思路。作者旨在将DAEPP与BDCZ集成，以降低资源开销并提升在低可靠性条件下的性能。

Method: 提出DAHR协议，在端到端路径的每一段集成单次DAEPP纯化步骤与BDCZ纠缠交换。推导了嵌入单段DAEPP的端到端保真度闭式递归公式，并给出严格资源下界，用于衡量传统BBPSSW协议为匹配DAHR单段有效保真度所需的轮数。数值分析选取基于前人实验的代表性非对称工作点进行评估。

Result: 在代表性非对称工作点下，匹配DAHR单光子对的性能需要2至3轮BBPSSW纯化。此外，存在一个关键操作可靠性阈值，低于该阈值时，无论执行多少轮BBPSSW纯化都无法匹配DAHR单轮DAEPP的效果。这表明DAHR在资源效率和低可靠性场景下均具有显著优势。

Conclusion: DAHR将DAEPP与BDCZ集成，显著降低了纯化资源消耗（以单光子对替代多轮双光子对消耗），并在操作可靠性较低时仍能保持优势，优于传统BBPSSW+BDCZ中继链方案。该工作为超纠缠自由度辅助纯化在实际量子网络中的应用提供了理论支撑与资源分析框架。

Abstract: Linear quantum repeater chains based on Werner-state purification (the BBPSSW protocol) and entanglement swapping (the BDCZ scheme) are fundamental to entanglement distribution in quantum networks. However, they operate under a stringent operation reliability threshold and rely on resource-intensive recurrence purification rounds, each consuming two entangled pairs to probabilistically produce one. In the literature, hyperentanglement has been proposed to exploit multiple degrees of freedom (DOF), such as polarisation and spatial modes, to encode independent entangled states within a single photon pair. This has led to the definition of DAEPP (DOF-Assisted Entanglement Purification Protocol), which we propose to integrate with the BDCZ scheme, resulting in a chain protocol that we call DAHR (DOF-Assisted Hyperentanglement Repeater). The DAEPP step distils the fidelity of a DOF by consuming other(s). In this work, we propose and analyse a DAHR variant which integrates DAEPP at every segment of an end-to-end path combined with BDCZ. We derive a closed-form end-to-end fidelity recursion that embeds single-segment DAEPP into the BDCZ scheme and give a strict resource lower bound for any BDCZ baseline utilising BBPSSW purification to match DAHR's per-segment effective fidelity. At a representative asymmetric operating point informed by prior experiment, we show numerically that matching DAHR's single-photon-pair performance requires two to three rounds of BBPSSW purification. Additionally, below a critical operation reliability, no amount of BBPSSW rounds matches DAHR's one DAEPP round performance.

</details>


### [278] [Metrological quantum-to-classical crossover in the volume of a noisy quasiperiodic lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30834)
*Priya Ghosh,Debarupa Saha,Ujjwal Sen,Debraj Rakshit*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于Aubry-André-Harper模型中局域化-退局域化相变的量子多体临界传感器的计量学性能，发现噪声与系统尺寸的相互作用会导致计量学量子-经典交叉现象：量子增强的量子费希尔信息缩放仅在有限的特征系统尺寸内成立，超过该噪声依赖的交叉长度后，量子优势消失并退化为经典极限的广延缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期研究提出利用局域化-退局域化相变构建一类高效的量子多体临界传感器。然而，实际应用中噪声不可避免，亟需审视此类器件在有噪声环境下的计量学性能边界，特别是量子增强优势能否随系统规模扩展而持续。

Method: 以支持有限强度在位势下发生局域化-退局域化相变的Aubry-André-Harper模型为研究对象，分别考虑两类噪声机制：热噪声和晶格跳跃强度缺陷。通过分析量子费希尔信息在不同系统尺寸下的缩放行为，识别量子增强（超广延）缩放向经典广延缩放转变的交叉行为，并确定噪声依赖的特征交叉长度。

Result: 在局域化-退局域化相变点及其附近，小体系下量子费希尔信息呈现量子增强缩放，但超过特征交叉长度后该优势消失。热噪声下交叉长度随温度升高而减小；跳跃强度缺陷下出现类似的交叉，量子增强的超广延缩放退化为经典受限的广延缩放。两种不同噪声机制导致共同的限制：增大探针尺寸超过噪声依赖的极限会破坏计量学量子优势。

Conclusion: 不同噪声机制对量子多体临界传感器造成普遍的可扩展性限制——量子增强传感优势仅在噪声决定的有限尺寸范围内成立。这一结论对量子多体临界传感器的实际设计具有重要指导意义，提示实际应用中需权衡系统规模与噪声水平。

Abstract: Localization-delocalization transitions have recently been proposed for building a class of efficient quantum many-body critical sensors. This work scrutinizes metrological performances of such devices by focusing on the Aubry-André-Harper model that supports a localization-delocalization transition at finite strength of the onsite potential. We identify a metrological quantum-to-classical crossover driven by the interplay between noise and system size, whereby quantum-enhanced scaling of the quantum Fisher information persists only up to a finite, noise-dependent characteristic system-size. We first consider thermal noise and show that, at and near the localization-delocalization transition, the quantum Fisher information exhibits quantum-enhanced scaling for small systems but the system is stripped of this advantage beyond the characteristic crossover length. The crossover length decreases with increasing temperature. We then consider imperfections in the lattice hopping strengths and find a qualitatively similar crossover. There, the quantum-enhanced regime, identified with super-extensive scaling, gives way to an extensive scaling-a classical-limited weaker form-at sufficiently large system sizes. Thus, distinct noise mechanisms lead to a common limitation on the scalability of quantum-enhanced sensing: increasing the probe size beyond a noise-dependent limit can destroy the metrological quantum advantage.

</details>


### [279] [Size-Independent Robustness in Multipartite Bell Self-Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30851)
*Shen Cao,Xingjian Zhang,Fei Shi,Qi Zhao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了与量子网络规模无关的多方纠缠鲁棒自检验框架，推导出n比特GHZ态的完全解析、设备无关的自检验界，该界随观测违反误差线性缩放，且与理论上界仅差常数因子2。通过算子不等式框架将最优界猜想验证简化为高效数值检查（已执行至n=100），使得GHZ纠缠能在固定噪声水平下于任意大规模系统中得到认证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前多方纠缠的实际鲁棒自检验仅适用于小规模系统，因为误差界随系统规模急剧恶化。作者旨在突破这一规模瓶颈，实现与系统大小无关的鲁棒自检验，从而支持可扩展的设备无关验证。

Method: 采用算子不等式框架推导n比特GHZ态的完全解析设备无关自检验界；该界与观测到的违反误差呈线性关系，并通过常数因子2逼近理论最优上界。同时利用算子不等式将最优界的验证转化为高效率的数值检查（计算至n=100）。

Result: 获得了与系统规模无关的鲁棒自检验界，随观测误差线性缩放，与理论上界偏差不超过常数因子2；通过数值验证确认该界的最优性可达n=100，从而在任意大规模系统中实现固定噪声水平下的GHZ纠缠认证。

Conclusion: 该工作将多方纠缠自检验从规模受限推进到可扩展领域，为大规模量子网络中的设备无关纠缠认证提供了实用且理论最优（常数因子内）的工具。

Abstract: Practical robust self-testing of multipartite entanglement has so far been restricted to small-scale systems due to error bounds that degrade severely with system size. In this work, we establish multipartite self-testing with robustness independent of the size of the quantum network. We derive a fully analytic, device-independent self-testing bound for $n$-qubit Greenberger-Horne-Zeilinger (GHZ) states. The bound scales linearly with the observed violation error and lies universally within a constant factor of two from a theoretical upper bound. Furthermore, the operator-inequality framework reduces the verification of the conjectured optimal bound to a highly efficient numerical check, which we perform up to $n=100$. Consequently, GHZ entanglement can be certified under a fixed noise level in arbitrarily large systems, enabling scalable device-independent verification.

</details>


### [280] [Well-conditioned iterative methods for large open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30860)
*Gaspard Beugnot,Paul Gregory,Rémi Robin,Antoine Tilloy*

Main category: quant-ph
