TL;DR: 本文提出利用Lindblad主方程中"无跳跃演化"算符S的可高效求逆特性，构造辅助CPTP映射Φ用于稳态和低激发态谱的迭代求解，实现每步O(n³)复杂度的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限维Markov开放量子系统的Lindblad主方程中，对密度矩阵ρ的演化由线性超算符L控制。显式存储L需O(n⁴)空间，精确对角化或求逆需O(n⁶)时间，对大规模系统代价过高。虽然L的施加只需O(n³)，但迭代方法缺乏良好的预条件子而难以直接应用。

Method: 核心观察是Lindblad方程中对应"无跳跃演化"的部分算符S可以高效求逆。利用该逆映射S⁻¹，构造一个辅助的完全正迹保持（CPTP）映射Φ，其不动点与稳态ρ∞直接相关，且其他特征值幅值更小，非常适合迭代方法。用Arnoldi迭代求解稳态；用S⁻¹作预条件子通过shift-invert Arnoldi计算低能谱；并构造隐式时间积分器。

Result: 稳态和低激发态问题的方法每步迭代复杂度为O(n³)，在CPU和GPU上均达到当前最先进的性能表现。作为概念验证，所构建的隐式时间积分器在低精度范围内的刚性系统上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 通过利用Lindblad方程中S算符的可高效求逆性，实现了大规模开放量子系统稳态、低激发态谱及时间演化的高效数值求解，突破了传统稠密线性代数方法的复杂度瓶颈。

Abstract: Markovian open quantum systems are well modeled by the Lindblad Master Equation (ME) $\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d} t} ρ_t = \mathcal{L} ρ_t$, where $\mathcal{L}$ is a linear (super-)operator and $ρ_t$ is the system state, a positive matrix. When designing or characterizing a quantum system, one is usually interested in the steady state $ρ_\infty$ (such that $\mathcal{L} ρ_\infty = 0$), the first few excited states, and trajectories $t\mapsto ρ_t$. In finite dimension, $ρ_t$ is an $n\times n$ matrix, $\mathcal{L}$ thus typically costs $n^4$ to store explicitly as a dense matrix, and $O(n^6)$ to diagonalize or invert exactly, making standard linear algebraic techniques expensive for large systems. However, $\mathcal{L}$ usually costs only $O(n^3)$ to apply. This makes iterative methods appealing, but they do not work without a good preconditioner. In this article, our main observation is that a part of the Lindblad equation, corresponding to the so-called no-jump evolution $\mathcal{S}$, can be inverted efficiently. Using this inverse map, we introduce an auxiliary completely positive trace-preserving (CPTP) map $Φ$ whose fixed point is directly related to $ρ_\infty$, all the other eigenvalues having smaller magnitude. The map $Φ$ is thus well suited to iterative methods, and $ρ_\infty$ can be found in a few Arnoldi iterations. Using the same inverse map $\mathcal{S}^{-1}$ as preconditioner, we compute the low-lying spectrum efficiently via shift-invert Arnoldi, and, as a proof of concept, build an implicit time integrator that is competitive on stiff systems in the low-precision regime. For the steady-state and low excited states problems, our methods scale like $O(n^3)$ per iteration and offer state-of-the-art performance on CPU and GPU.

</details>


### [281] [Constrained minimax approximation for quantum signal processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30937)
*Yulong Dong,James B. Larsen,Lin Lin,Rahul Sarkar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子信号处理（QSP）中经典设计阶段的约束极小极大逼近问题，提出了Remez交换法和非线性Fourier回缩两种方法来解决半无限约束下的可行性问题，后者通过QSP补全和相位合成将近似可行的多项式转化为可行的QSP多项式，在保持精度的同时更具稳定性，并已集成到qsppack软件包中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QSP的经典设计阶段需要求解一个带半无限约束的极小极大逼近问题（多项式在拟合集上逼近目标函数，且在[0,1]上幅值有界）。将问题离散化为线性规划后，在有限采样点上的可行性不能保证在整个定义域上的可行性，尤其是当最优逼近多项式达到可行集边界时。这构成了一个关键的计算困难。

Method: 提出了两种方法：(1) Remez交换法结合主动集约束执行，该方法在许多测试实例上高效但稳定性依赖于目标函数和问题几何结构；(2) 非线性Fourier回缩法，利用QSP补全和相位合成，将一个近似可行的多项式转化为可行的QSP多项式而不增加次数，并在代表性问题上保持逼近精度。

Result: 在代表性问题上，非线性Fourier回缩方法在很大程度上保持了逼近精度，并且在Remez启发式不稳定的实例上仍然有效。回缩法比Remez方法更具稳健性，两种方法均已实现于qsppack软件包中。

Conclusion: 该工作将经典极小极大逼近与半无限优化和非线性Fourier分析联系起来，为QSP多项式设计提供了实用且稳健的计算框架，拓展了QSP在量子计算中的应用范围。

Abstract: Quantum signal processing (QSP) provides a simple and efficient framework for implementing polynomial transformations using quantum circuits. Its classical design stage leads to a constrained minimax approximation problem: find a polynomial of prescribed parity that approximates a target function uniformly on a fitting set while remaining bounded in magnitude by one on the domain $[0,1]$, which can be viewed as a semi-infinite constraint. Discretization converts the problem into a linear program, but feasibility at a set of finitely many sampled points does not ensure feasibility on the whole domain, especially when an optimal approximant reaches the boundary of the feasible set. We investigate two approaches to address this difficulty. A Remez exchange method combined with active-set constraint enforcement is efficient on many tested instances, but its stability depends on the target and problem geometry. We then introduce nonlinear Fourier retraction, which uses QSP completion and phase synthesis to turn a nearly feasible polynomial into phase factors for a feasible QSP polynomial without increasing the degree. Across representative problems, retraction largely preserves approximation accuracy and remains effective on instances where the Remez heuristic is unstable. The resulting workflow connects classical minimax approximation and semi-infinite optimization with nonlinear Fourier analysis, and is implemented in the qsppack software package.

</details>


### [282] [Entanglement and magic transitions in an all-to-all non-Hermitian spin model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31064)
*Sasanka Dowarah,Michael Kolodrubetz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究非厄米自旋系综在无序作用下的主导本征态转变。构建了具有k-局域全对全相互作用的非厄米自旋系综，证明无序会在谱离群值和多体谱体边缘之间产生竞争。在纯反厄米和混合无序下，当谱体在虚部上超越离群值时，主导本征态从低纠缠、低魔力的离群态转变为高纠缠、高魔力的体态，二者在同一阈值处转变。k>>√L时转变阈值为闭式，k<<√L时转变为交叉过渡。纯厄米无序则无此转变。最后将非厄米演化精确映射到被监测量子系统的后选择轨迹上，将其与测量诱导相变联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米系统的长时间态由虚部最大的本征值决定，但在相互作用多体系统中该本征值通常无法解析追踪，其选择的本征态性质未知。作者希望构建一个可解析追踪主导本征值的非厄米自旋系综，从而理解无序驱动下主导本征态的性质转变。

Method: 构造具有k-局域全对全相互作用的L自旋非厄米系综，从干净极限解析追踪主导本征值进入无序区域。研究三种无序情况：纯反厄米无序、纯厄米无序和等强度混合无序。通过比较谱离群值与谱体边缘的竞争分析主导本征态转变，并将非厄米演化精确映射到被监测量子系统的后选择轨迹上。

Result: 纯反厄米和混合无序下，当谱体在虚部上超越离群值时，主导本征态从低纠缠低魔力的离群态转变为高纠缠高魔力的体态，两种量在同阈值处同时变化。k>>√L时有尖锐谱边和闭式转变阈值；k<<√L时谱尾展宽为交叉过渡。纯厄米无序无离群-体转变。非厄米演化与后选择被监测量子轨迹精确等价。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在可解析处理的多体非厄米模型中发现无序驱动的主导本征态从离群态到体态的转变，该转变同时体现在纠缠和魔力两个量子资源上，并通过与测量诱导相变的映射建立了谱学机制与量子监测动力学之间的深层联系。

Abstract: The long-time state of a non-Hermitian system is determined by the eigenvalue with the largest imaginary part. In interacting many-body systems this eigenvalue usually cannot be tracked analytically, and the character of the state it selects is unknown. We construct a non-Hermitian spin ensemble of $L$ spins with exactly $k$-local all-to-all interactions, in which this dominant eigenvalue can be tracked analytically from the clean limit into the disordered regime. Disorder produces a competition between an isolated spectral outlier and the edge of a many-body spectral bulk. We study three cases: purely anti-Hermitian disorder, purely Hermitian disorder, and mixed disorder of equal strength. For purely anti-Hermitian and mixed disorder, we show that when the bulk overtakes the outlier in imaginary part, the dominant eigenstate switches from an outlier state with low entanglement and magic (nonstabilizerness) to a bulk state with substantially larger entanglement and magic, with both changing at the same threshold. For $k \gg \sqrt{L}$ the bulk has a sharp spectral edge and the transition thresholds follow in closed form, while for $k \ll \sqrt{L}$ spectral tails broaden the transition into a crossover. Purely Hermitian disorder provides a contrasting case with no outlier-to-bulk switching. Finally, we map the non-Hermitian evolution exactly onto postselected trajectories of a monitored quantum system, connecting this spectral mechanism to measurement-induced transitions.

</details>


### [283] [Deterministic Minimum-Output-Entropy Nonadditivity via Haagerup's Inequality and Near-Free Permutation Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31081)
*Guocheng Zhen,Chengkai Zhu,Ranyiliu Chen,Xin Wang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出Collins混合酉证明中有限维二次证书的确定性实现，构造确定性多项式时间算法生成排列并构建信道，证明最小输出熵的次可加性违反及Holevo量的自张量超可加性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最小输出熵的次可加性违反是量子信息论中的核心开放问题之一，先前Collins的证明是非构造性的或依赖概率方法，需要确定性且可验证的实现方案。

Method: 结合Haagerup长度二不等式与O'Donnell-Wu的确定性谱近似方法，构造K个排列对应的实正交Stinespring块，构建信道Φ，利用标准余变扩展将熵间隙转化为Holevo量的超可加性。

Result: 对于任意K≥2和满足log K>2(3+η)²的有理数η>0，算法输出得到信道Φ以及2H_min(Φ)−H_min(Φ^⊗2) ≥ (log K)/K − 2log(1+(3+η)²/K) > 0的正熵差。并证明常数3在相关Hermitian零对角系数类中是渐近最优的。

Conclusion: 该确定性构造实现了最小输出熵非可加性的有限维证明，并指出超越标量半径方法、研究输出体完整几何是下一步自然方向；同时通过余变扩展将熵间隙精确转化为单次Holevo量的自张量超可加性。

Abstract: We give a deterministic realization of the finite-dimensional quadratic certificate underlying Collins's mixed-unitary proof of minimum-output-entropy nonadditivity. For every fixed integer $K\ge 2$ and rational $η>0$ satisfying $\log K>2(3+η)^2$, a deterministic polynomial-time algorithm, for every sufficiently large target size $N$, outputs $K$ permutations on $N'=N+o_{K,η}(N)$ points. Restricting their permutation matrices to the nontrivial standard representation yields real orthogonal Stinespring blocks and a channel $Φ_{N'}:M_{N'-1}(\mathbb{C})\to M_K(\mathbb{C})$ such that \[ 2H_{\min}(Φ_{N'}) -H_{\min}(Φ_{N'}^{\otimes 2}) \ge \frac{\log K}{K} -2\log\left(1+\frac{(3+η)^2}{K}\right) >0. \] The construction combines Haagerup's length-two inequality with the simultaneous deterministic spectral approximation of O'Donnell and Wu. We further show that the constant $3$ is asymptotically sharp on the relevant Hermitian zero-diagonal coefficient class and that the finite spectral transfer is nearly saturated, thereby isolating the finer geometry of the full output body as the natural next level of refinement beyond the scalar-radius method. Finally, a standard covariant extension converts the same deterministic entropy gap exactly into self-tensor superadditivity of the one-shot Holevo quantity.

</details>


### [284] [Phase-noise induced many-body interference suppression in Gaussian Boson Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31089)
*Dario Cilluffo,Matthias Kost,Martin B. Plenio*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了无碰撞高斯玻色采样（GBS）的海森堡绘景张量网络表述，直接在Fock空间中用实验可测量的量表达输出概率，自然恢复Hafnian结构，并将GBS概率分解为相位不敏感贡献和与完美匹配对相关的多级干涉扇区。作为应用，研究了相位扩散如何逐步抑制多体干涉，使输出统计趋于经典二聚体模型区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GBS作为量子计算优势的重要候选方案，其输出概率的相位敏感特性与实验中的相位噪声密切相关，但缺乏一个直接连接实验可测量参数与量子干涉损失的统一理论框架。

Method: 采用海森堡绘景下的张量网络形式，将碰撞无关（collision-free）GBS的输出概率直接在Fock空间中表示为实验可测量的函数。该表示自然产生Hafnian结构，同时将概率分解为相位不敏感部分和对应于成对完美匹配的干涉扇区层级结构。

Result: 揭示了GBS概率的层级分解结构，并通过对相位扩散的分析显示其逐步压制多体干涉，驱动输出统计趋向经典二聚体模型的行为。

Conclusion: 建立了连接实验表征的相位波动与光量子采样中量子干涉损失之间的透明理论框架，为理解和预测实际GBS实验中的退相干效应提供了有力工具。

Abstract: We develop a Heisenberg-picture tensor-network formulation of collision-free Gaussian Boson Sampling, providing a direct Fock-space expression for output probabilities in terms of experimentally accessible quantities. The resulting representation naturally recovers the Hafnian structure while revealing the decomposition of GBS probability into a phase-insensitive contribution and a hierarchy of interference sectors associated with pairs of perfect matchings. As an application, we investigate phase diffusion and show how it progressively suppresses many-body interference, driving the output statistics toward a classical dimer-model regime. Our results establish a transparent framework for connecting experimentally characterized phase fluctuations with the loss of quantum interference in photonic quantum sampling experiments.

</details>


### [285] [Tunable Exceptional Points for Quantum Sensing in a Spin--Orbit-Angular-Momentum Coupled BEC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31095)
*Zicheng Zhang,Xi-Wang Luo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于自旋-轨道角动量耦合玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体中固有非厄米Bogoliubov动力学的可调异常点传感平台，揭示了在PT对称稳定区间接近异常点时量子Fisher信息的显著增强，并证明了自旋密度测量可接近量子Fisher信息极限，为实验上可实现的异常点增强量子传感提供了路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统非厄米系统中异常点虽然能增强对微弱扰动的响应，但伴随的增益和损耗噪声并不一定能转化为量子极限灵敏度的提升。作者希望探索一种内在非厄米系统——无需外部增益/损耗、由约化赫米特微观哈密顿量衍生的Bogoliubov动力学系统——以实现同时具备异常点灵敏增强与量子噪声抑制的传感平台。

Method: 从完全厄米的微观哈密顿量出发，推导出具有PT对称性的Bogoliubov动力学矩阵；通过拉曼耦合和相互作用参数调节异常点；识别多个异常点并映射其轨迹与稳定性景观；计算量子Fisher信息以表征传感性能；分析二阶异常点的能隙开启率作为性能指标；研究两个二阶异常点重叠时的量子Fisher信息线性叠加效应；提出基于自旋密度测量的实验方案。

Result: 从PT对称稳定区间接近异常点时观察到强量子Fisher信息增强；二阶异常点的能隙开启率可作为比较异常点传感性能的相对指标；高阶异常点不一定能从稳定区间可达；两个二阶异常点重叠时可实现量子Fisher信息的线性增强；自旋密度测量可接近量子Fisher信息极限。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个基于玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体内在非厄米动力学的可调异常点增强量子传感平台，克服了传统非厄米系统增益损耗噪声的限制，并给出了实验上可行的测量方案，为无需外部耗散的量子精密测量开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) can induce strongly amplified responses to weak perturbations, but enhanced spectral sensitivity in conventional non-Hermitian systems does not necessarily translate into improved quantum-limited sensing because of the associated gain and loss noise. Here, we investigate a tunable EP sensing platform based on the intrinsic non-Hermitian Bogoliubov dynamics of a spin-orbit-angular-momentum-coupled Bose-Einstein condensate. Starting from a fully Hermitian microscopic Hamiltonian, we derive a Bogoliubov dynamical matrix that exhibits parity-time (PT) symmetry, with EPs tunable through the Raman coupling and interaction parameters. We identify multiple EPs and map their trajectories and associated stability landscapes, revealing strong quantum Fisher information enhancement when the EPs are approached from the PT-symmetric stable regime. We further find that the gap-opening rate around a second-order EP provides a useful relative indicator for comparing the sensing performance of EPs, while higher-order EPs are not necessarily accessible from the stable regime. Moreover, two second-order EPs can be tuned to overlap, allowing their sensing contributions to add and yielding a linear enhancement of the total quantum Fisher information. Finally, we show that a spin-density measurement can approach the quantum Fisher information limit, providing an experimentally accessible route to tunable EP-enhanced quantum sensing in Bose-Einstein condensates.

</details>


### [286] [Unconditional Certified Randomness without Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31112)
*Andrea Coladangelo,Dakshita Khurana,Saachi Mutreja,Bhaskar Roberts,Joseph Slote,Avishay Tal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子随机预言机模型下提出了一种可认证随机性协议，该协议非交互、可公开验证且基于经典验证者，并基于Yamakawa和Zhandry的量子性证明工作，对做出次指数级自适应量子查询的对手实现了无条件安全，突破了先前工作依赖Aaronson-Ambainis猜想或仅针对低查询深度对手的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的工作在随机预言机下实现可认证随机性时，要么需要假设Aaronson-Ambainis猜想这一未经证明的推测，要么只能对低查询深度的对手证明安全性，缺乏广泛且可靠的安全性保障。

Method: 基于Yamakawa和Zhandry的量子性证明协议构建可认证随机性协议，在量子随机预言机模型中分析其安全性，利用量子随机预言机的特性证明协议对次指数级自适应量子查询对手的无条件安全。

Result: 成功构造了非交互、经典验证者公开可验证的可认证随机性协议，并在量子随机预言机模型下证明其对做出次指数多自适应量子查询的对手具有无条件安全性。

Conclusion: 该工作消除了先前可认证随机性协议中对Aaronson-Ambainis猜想的依赖，并将安全性扩展到次指数级查询深度的对手，显著推进了可认证随机性的理论基础。

Abstract: We obtain a certified randomness protocol in the quantum random oracle model. The protocol is non-interactive and publicly verifiable with a classical verifier, and is based on Yamakawa and Zhandry's proof of quantumness [JACM'24]. We prove unconditional security of this protocol against adversaries making subexponentially-many adaptive quantum queries to the random oracle.
  Prior work on certified randomness relative to a random oracle additionally assumed the Aaronson--Ambainis conjecture or proved security only against low query-depth adversaries.

</details>


### [287] ["Train classical, deploy quantum" requires rethinking generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31117)
*Snehal Raj,Natansh Mathur,Alejandro Perdomo-Ortiz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了量子生成模型中使用矩匹配损失（如MMD²）训练时的泛化性能问题，通过在最高30量子比特的基数约束数据集和基因组单核苷酸变异数据集上进行基准测试，发现矩匹配损失训练出的模型在泛化上普遍差于似然训练的模型，表明收敛的矩匹配损失并不能可靠衡量泛化性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的研究主要关注量子生成模型能否被训练以及其采样是否具有经典计算困难性，但对最小化矩匹配目标是否能产生泛化良好的模型（而非仅仅复现训练统计量）这一问题缺乏理解。泛化性是生成模型从训练数据推广到未见数据的关键能力，因此值得深入研究。

Method: 通过对一组广泛的量子与经典生成模型进行直接采样基准测试，对比使用矩匹配损失训练的模型与使用似然训练的模型的泛化性能。实验在两个应用驱动的数据集上进行：一个基数约束数据集（最多30量子比特）和一个基因组单核苷酸变异数据集。

Result: 矩匹配损失训练的模型在泛化性能上普遍差于似然训练的模型，该结论在两个数据集上均得到验证。这表明即使矩匹配损失收敛，也不能保证模型具有良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 矩匹配损失收敛不能作为可靠衡量泛化的指标。"classical训练-quantum部署"的工作流需要直接针对泛化进行优化的方法，无论是改进训练目标函数还是改变模型架构，这个问题仍然开放。

Abstract: Generative models have become central across science and industry, from image and text synthesis to the design of molecules and materials. Quantum generative models are considered one of the most promising applications for quantum computers, since a quantum circuit naturally produces samples from the distribution it encodes, and for suitable circuits that distribution is believed to be hard for any classical computer to reproduce. A leading strategy trains these models on a classical computer and reserves the quantum device for generating samples at deployment. This is possible when the training loss can be evaluated on a classical computer. A prime example is the maximum mean discrepancy (MMD$^2$), a moment-matching loss that compares the model and the data through their Pauli-$Z$ correlations. Research so far has asked whether such models can be trained and whether their sampling is hard; whether minimizing such an objective yields a model that generalizes, rather than one that merely reproduces the training statistics, remains poorly understood. We benchmark a broad set of quantum and classical generative models by direct sampling and show that models trained with a moment-matching loss generally show worse generalization than the likelihood-trained models. We show this on two application-inspired datasets: first a cardinality-constrained dataset at up to $30$ qubits and second a dataset of genomic single-nucleotide variants, whose valid set is the observed data. These results indicate that a converged moment-matching loss is not a reliable measure of generalization, and that train-classical, deploy-quantum workflows will need approaches that target generalization directly, leaving open whether better training objectives suffice or whether the model architectures themselves must change.

</details>

### [288] [The \(V_n\) Invariants as Colored Links--Gould Invariants:A Root--Center Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.28720)
*Jiuhe Liu*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文证明了两类纽结不变量——由秩二Nichols代数与4n维右Yetter–Drinfeld模构造的V_n不变量，与由典型U_q(sl(2|1))模定义的彩色Links–Gould不变量——在镜射与参数反转的意义下完全一致。证明是结构性的，通过构造右-右配对双代数、证明其Hopf配对的完美性、获得完整万有R矩阵，并用显式Abelian Drinfeld扭将双代数分解为全奇U_ħ(sl(2|1))根因子与交换中心因子的张量积，从而建立两个不变量框架间的精确对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两个看似独立的纽结不变量构造——基于Nichols代数的V_n不变量与基于量子超代数典型模的Links–Gould不变量——需要被统一理解。这一对应关系不仅能澄清两者之间的代数结构联系，还能借此证明V_n构造中端值算子的标量恒等性猜想。

Method: 证明方法分为几个结构性步骤：(1) 将辫子算子T_n实现为典范super Yetter–Drinfeld辫子的规范变换；(2) 构造相应的右-右配对双代数，证明其对偶群作用在每个根度上都是完美的；(3) 获得其完备万有R矩阵；(4) 通过显式的Abelian Drinfeld扭将双代数分解为全奇U_ħ(sl(2|1))根因子与交换中心因子；(5) 在此分解下，将Nichols模实现为典型全奇最高权模与一维中心模的张量积；(6) 将对偶性、四种定向交叉、部分转置及扭数归一化全部传输到新框架中。

Result: 对于每个定向纽结K、每个n≥1及每个β≠0,-1，证明了V_{n,K}(Q^{2nβ+n},Q^2)=LG_K^{(n)}(Q^{-nβ},Q^{-1})=LG_{\overline K}^{(n)}(Q^{nβ},Q)。特别地，由典型模的单纯性推出了V_n构造中标量恒等性猜想。

Conclusion: V_n不变量与彩色Links–Gould不变量在结构上是等价的（相差镜射和参数反转）。这一统一揭示了两个独立构造背后的共同代数根源，并附带解决了V_n构造中的标量恒等性猜想。

Abstract: The knot invariants \(V_n\) arise from a rank-two Nichols algebra and a \(4n\)-dimensional right Yetter--Drinfeld module, whereas the colored Links--Gould invariants are defined from typical \(U_q(\mathfrak{sl}(2|1))\)-modules. We prove that these two constructions agree, up to the mirror and parameter inversion forced by the right-module convention. The proof is structural. We first realize the braid operator \(T_n\) as a gauge transform of a canonical super Yetter--Drinfeld braiding. We then construct the relevant right--right paired double, prove that its Hopf pairing is perfect in every root degree, and obtain its completed universal \(R\)-matrix. An explicit Abelian Drinfeld twist separates this double into an all-odd \(U_\hbar(\mathfrak{sl}(2|1))\) root factor and a commutative central factor. Under this factorization, the Nichols module becomes a typical all-odd highest-weight module tensored with a one-dimensional central module. Finally, we transport duality, all four oriented crossings, partial transposes, and writhe normalization. For every oriented knot \(\mathcal K\), every \(n\geq 1\), and every \(β\neq 0,-1\), the result is \[
  V_{n,\mathcal K}\!\left(Q^{2nβ+n},Q^2\right)
  =
  LG_{\mathcal K}^{(n)}\!\left(Q^{-nβ},Q^{-1}\right)
  =
  LG_{\overline{\mathcal K}}^{(n)}\!\left(Q^{nβ},Q\right). \] In particular, the scalar-identity property conjectured for the endomorphism-valued \(V_n\) construction follows from the simplicity of the corresponding typical module.

</details>


### [289] [Bicrossed products of generalized Taft algebras and Radford algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29536)
*Rongchuan Xiong*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文在特征为p>0的代数闭域上，完整分类了广义Taft代数T_{N,n,ξ}与Radford代数R之间的所有matched pairs。当p∤N时所有matched pair平凡，对应的双交叉积为张量积Hopf代数；当p|N时，matched pairs由β∈F_p参数化，双交叉积由显式交叉关系描述，且同构当且仅当参数相等，故恰好有p个同构类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在正特征代数闭域上，广义Taft代数和Radford代数都是重要的有限维Hopf代数（通常非半单、非交换、非余交换）。理解它们之间的matched pairs及双交叉积结构，有助于深入理解正特征下Hopf代数的分解与分类问题。

Method: 论文通过分析广义Taft代数T_{N,n,ξ}与Radford代数R之间的matched pair结构（即作用与余作用满足相容性条件的配对），分p∤N和p|N两种情况分别讨论：前者证明匹配必须平凡；后者通过参数β∈F_p刻画所有非平凡matched pairs，并推导出对应的双交叉积的显式交叉乘法/余乘法关系。

Result: 当p∤N时，所有matched pair平凡，双交叉积即张量积。当p|N时，matched pairs由F_p中的参数β一一参数化；两个双交叉积Hopf代数同构当且仅当对应参数相等，从而恰好存在p个互不同构的Hopf代数。

Conclusion: 本文给出了广义Taft代数与Radford代数之间的完整matched pair分类及双交叉积同构分类，揭示了正特征下p是否整除N对这一结构的本质影响，并确定非平凡情形下恰好有p个Hopf同构类。

Abstract: Over an algebraically closed field of characteristic $p>0$, we classify all matched pairs between a generalized Taft algebra $T_{N,n,ξ}$ and the Radford algebra $R$. If $p\nmid N$, every matched pair is trivial, and the corresponding bicrossed product is the tensor product Hopf algebra. If $p\mid N$, matched pairs are parametrized by $β\in\mathbb F_p$, and the bicrossed products are described by explicit cross relations. Two such bicrossed products are isomorphic as Hopf algebras if and only if their parameters are equal; hence there are exactly $p$ isomorphism classes in this case.

</details>


### [290] [Poisson bialgebras by deformations-to-quasiclassical limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29782)
*Siyuan Chen,Chengming Bai*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文将双代数层面的形变-准经典极限过程从结合代数延伸至泊松代数，建立了反对称无穷小双代数形变与泊松双代数之间的联系，并分别通过结合代数的匹配对和标准Manin三元组给出了等价刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 泊松代数作为交换结合代数形变的准经典极限已被广泛研究，但这一过程尚未被推广到双代数层面。该文旨在将Lie双代数的Hopf代数量子化过程的"无穷小版本"建立起来，填补泊松双代数与反对称无穷小双代数之间形变理论的空白。

Method: 本文从交换余交换的反对称无穷小双代数的形变出发，推导出泊松双代数作为其准经典极限。作者引入了结合代数匹配对的形变和标准Manin三元组的概念，分别与泊松代数匹配对和泊松代数标准Manin三元组相对应，并通过相干导子（coherent derivations）来具体说明这些等价的形变-准经典极限过程。

Result: 成功地建立了泊松双代数作为反对称无穷小双代数形变准经典极限的理论框架，并证明了该过程与结合代数匹配对形变及标准Manin三元组形变之间的等价性，同时通过相干导子给出了具体实例说明。

Conclusion: 该工作为泊松双代数提供了完整的形变-准经典极限理论，可视为Lie双代数Hopf量子化的无穷小版本，为后续研究泊松-Lie群及相关量子化问题奠定了基础。

Abstract: Poisson algebras are the quasiclassical limits of associative algebra deformations of commutative associative algebras.
  This paper extends this process to the level of bialgebras. We derive Poisson bialgebras as the quasiclassical limits of antisymmetric infinitesimal bialgebra deformations of commutative and cocommutative antisymmetric
  infinitesimal bialgebras. It might be regarded as the ``infinitesimal" version of the
  quantization process of Lie bialgebras in terms of Hopf algebras. Such deformations-quasiclassical limits
  process for a Poisson bialgebra is equivalently characterized in
  terms of the introduced notions of
  deformations of a matched pair of associative algebras as well as a standard Manin triple of associative
  algebras, whose corresponding quasiclassical limits
  are a matched pair of Poisson algebras and a standard Manin triple of Poisson algebras,
  respectively. We illustrate these equivalent deformations-quasiclassical
  limits processes via coherent derivations.

</details>


### [291] [A note on the center of the queer super Yangian ${\rm Y}(\mathfrak{q}_1)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30293)
*Hao Chang,Hongmei Hu*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文研究了queer超Yangian Y(q₁)的两族中心元素——分别来自Poletaeva–Serganova构造和Nazarov构造，并在它们的生成级数之间建立了显式关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: queer超Yangian的中心元素是理解其表示理论和结构的重要工具。已有两族中心元素分别由Poletaeva–Serganova和Nazarov的构造方法给出，但两者之间的具体联系尚未明确，本研究旨在澄清这一关系。

Method: 通过对比和分析Poletaeva–Serganova构造与Nazarov构造得到的中心元素生成级数，推导并证明二者之间的显式恒等关系。

Result: 成功地在这两族中心元素的生成级数之间建立了明确的代数关系式。

Conclusion: 该结果统一了两条不同的构造途径，为queer超Yangian的中心结构研究提供了更完整的图景，并可能对后续的表示理论研究有所助益。

Abstract: We investigate two families of central elements of the queer super Yangian ${\rm Y}(\mathfrak{q}_1)$, arising from the constructions of Poletaeva--Serganova and Nazarov, and establish an explicit relation between their generating series.

</details>


### [292] [Exact Factorizations of Rank-One Pointed Hopf Algebras in Positive Characteristic, I](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30947)
*Rongchuan Xiong*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 本文对正特征代数闭域上的第三型秩一尖点Hopf代数的精确分解进行了完整分类。主要步骤是完整分类此类代数与群代数之间的匹配对，结果表明群像部分必须形成有限群的匹配对，而作用于斜本原生成元的唯一可能动作是沿区分群像元素g的平移。双交叉积仍是第三型秩一尖点Hopf代数，并给出了两个此类乘积作为Hopf代数同构的充要条件。作为应用，完整分类了Radford代数与循环群代数之间的所有匹配对，并在循环群为素数阶时显式确定了相应的精确分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在对正特征代数闭域上的第三型秩一尖点Hopf代数的精确分解（exact factorization）进行完整分类，这是Hopf代数理论中的一个基本结构问题。精确分解与匹配对、双交叉积密切相关，分类此类分解有助于深入理解正特征Hopf代数的结构理论。

Method: 1. 首先将问题归结为分类第三型秩一尖点Hopf代数与群代数之间的匹配对。2. 证明群像部分必须形成有限群的匹配对，而斜本原生成元上的唯一可能作用是平移一个标量倍数的$1-g$（g为区分群像元素），由一个群同态编码。3. 证明了双交叉积仍然是第三型秩一尖点Hopf代数，并给出两个双交叉积作为Hopf代数同构的充要条件。4. 由此建立第三型代数精确分解与底层有限群精确分解之间的一一对应。5. 作为应用，分类Radford代数与循环群代数之间的所有匹配对，对素数阶循环群情形显式给出所有精确分解。

Result: 得到了第三型秩一尖点Hopf代数精确分解的完整分类。具体地，除交换两个因子的情况外，固定第三型代数的精确分解与其底层有限群的精确分解之间存在一一对应，且区分群像元素位于秩一因子中。此外，完整分类了Radford代数与循环群代数的匹配对，并对素数阶循环群给出了所有精确分解的显式刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了正特征下第三型秩一尖点Hopf代数精确分解的完整分类，揭示了此类Hopf代数的精确分解结构完全由其底层有限群的精确分解所决定（在置换因子意义下）。这一结果将Hopf代数的分解问题有效地归约为纯群论问题，并为进一步研究正特征Hopf代数的分解理论奠定了基础。

Abstract: We classify exact factorizations of the third-type rank-one pointed Hopf algebras over an algebraically closed field of positive characteristic.
  The main step is a complete classification of matched pairs between such an algebra and a group algebra. It turns out that the group-like part must form a matched pair of finite groups, while the only possible action on the skew-primitive generator is a shift by a scalar multiple of $1-g$, where $g$ is the distinguished group-like element, encoded by a single group homomorphism. The bicrossed product is again a third-type rank-one pointed Hopf algebra, and we give a necessary and sufficient condition for two such products to be isomorphic as Hopf algebras.
  Consequently, up to interchanging the two factors, exact factorizations of a fixed third-type algebra correspond bijectively to exact factorizations of its underlying finite group, with the distinguished group-like element lying in the rank-one factor.
  As an application, all matched pairs between the Radford algebra and cyclic group algebras are classified, and the corresponding exact factorizations are determined explicitly when the cyclic group has prime order.

</details>
